===== FILE: src/views/estimating/admin/Lists.tsx =====
// Picklists maintained by the estimating team: allowed units, scope quantity types, the scope library
// (in-scope, out-of-scope, assumptions) and the maturity / class checklist.
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { AaceClass, AppState, EstimatingConfig } from '@/core/types'
import { CLASS_RANGES } from '@/core/engine/estimates'
import { cx } from '@/core/util'
import { Badge, Button, Callout, ClassBadge, Input, Panel, Select, TextArea } from '@/ui/kit'
import { SaveBar, cellInp, saveSection, useDraft } from './shared'

type QT = EstimatingConfig['quantityTypes'][number]
const FEEDS: { value: string; label: string }[] = [
  { value: '', label: 'Not used by the estimate' }, { value: 'lengthFt', label: 'Length (ft)' }, { value: 'sites', label: 'Valve sites' },
  { value: 'hp', label: 'Station capacity (hp)' }, { value: 'tieIns', label: 'Tie-ins' }, { value: 'services', label: 'Services' },
]

// ───────────────────────────── Units and quantity types
export function UnitsQuantities({ s, canEdit }: { s: AppState; canEdit: boolean }) {
  const cfg = s.settings.estimating
  const U = useDraft(cfg.units)
  const Q = useDraft(cfg.quantityTypes)
  const [nu, setNu] = useState({ id: '', label: '' })
  const unitUse = (id: string) => s.rates.filter((r) => r.unit === id).length + s.estimates.filter((e) => e.status === 'draft').reduce((a, e) => a + e.lines.filter((l) => l.unit === id).length, 0) + Q.draft.filter((q) => q.unit === id).length
  const qtUse = (id: string) => s.projects.filter((p) => p.scope.quantities.some((q) => q.typeId === id)).length
  const saveUnits = () => {
    const added = U.draft.filter((u) => !cfg.units.some((x) => x.id === u.id)).map((u) => u.id), removed = cfg.units.filter((u) => !U.draft.some((x) => x.id === u.id)).map((u) => u.id)
    const renamed = U.draft.filter((u) => cfg.units.some((x) => x.id === u.id && x.label !== u.label)).map((u) => `${u.id} "${u.label}"`)
    saveSection('units', `Allowed units: ${[added.length && `added ${added.join(', ')}`, removed.length && `removed ${removed.join(', ')}`, renamed.length && `renamed ${renamed.join(', ')}`].filter(Boolean).join('; ') || 'no change'}`, (d) => { d.settings.estimating.units = U.draft.map((x) => ({ ...x })) }, 'Allowed units saved')
    U.commit()
  }
  const saveQt = () => {
    const ch: string[] = []
    for (const q of Q.draft) { const o = cfg.quantityTypes.find((x) => x.id === q.id); if (!o) ch.push(`added ${q.label} (${q.unit})`); else if (JSON.stringify(o) !== JSON.stringify(q)) ch.push(`${q.label}: ${o.unit !== q.unit ? `unit ${o.unit} → ${q.unit} ` : ''}${o.feeds !== q.feeds ? `feeds ${o.feeds ?? 'none'} → ${q.feeds ?? 'none'}` : ''}${o.label !== q.label ? `renamed from ${o.label}` : ''}`.trim()) }
    for (const o of cfg.quantityTypes) if (!Q.draft.some((x) => x.id === o.id)) ch.push(`removed ${o.label}`)
    saveSection('quantity-types', `Scope quantity types: ${ch.join('; ') || 'no change'}`, (d) => { d.settings.estimating.quantityTypes = Q.draft.map((x) => ({ ...x })) }, 'Quantity types saved')
    Q.commit()
  }
  const unitOpts = U.draft.map((u) => ({ value: u.id, label: `${u.id} · ${u.label}` }))
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,0.8fr)_minmax(0,1.2fr)]">
      <Panel pad={false} title="Allowed units" subtitle="The only units an estimate line, rate-table row or scope quantity may use (pipe in feet, not inches).">
        <table className="tbl">
          <thead><tr><th style={{ width: 90 }}>Unit</th><th>Label</th><th className="num">In use</th>{canEdit && <th style={{ width: 30 }} />}</tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {U.draft.map((u, i) => (
              <tr key={u.id}>
                <td className="font-mono font-semibold">{u.id}</td>
                <td>{canEdit ? <Input aria-label={`${u.id} label`} className={cellInp} value={u.label} onChange={(e) => U.set((d) => { d[i].label = e.target.value; return d })} /> : u.label}</td>
                <td className="num text-muted">{unitUse(u.id)}</td>
                {canEdit && <td>{!unitUse(u.id) && <button className="text-muted hover:text-bad" aria-label={`Remove ${u.id}`} onClick={() => U.set((d) => d.filter((_, k) => k !== i))}>✕</button>}</td>}
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        {canEdit && <form className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-line px-4 py-2" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); const id = nu.id.trim(); if (!id || U.draft.some((u) => u.id === id)) return; U.set((d) => [...d, { id, label: nu.label.trim() || id }]); setNu({ id: '', label: '' }) }}>
          <Input id="unit-id" className="h-8 w-24 font-mono" placeholder="Code" value={nu.id} onChange={(e) => setNu({ ...nu, id: e.target.value })} />
          <Input id="unit-label" className="h-8 w-40" placeholder="Label" value={nu.label} onChange={(e) => setNu({ ...nu, label: e.target.value })} />
          <Button size="sm" type="submit" disabled={!nu.id.trim() || U.draft.some((u) => u.id === nu.id.trim())}>Add unit</Button>
        </form>}
        <SaveBar dirty={U.dirty} onSave={saveUnits} onDiscard={U.reset} disabled={U.draft.some((u) => !u.label.trim())} />
      </Panel>

      <Panel pad={false} title="Scope quantity types" subtitle="What a scope statement may count, its unit, and which rate-table estimate input it drives."
        actions={canEdit && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => Q.set((d) => [...d, { id: `qt-${Date.now().toString(36)}`, label: 'New quantity', unit: U.draft[0]?.id ?? 'ea' }])}>Add type</Button>}>
        <div className="scroll-x">
          <table className="tbl">
            <thead><tr><th>Quantity</th><th style={{ width: 150 }}>Unit</th><th style={{ width: 190 }}>Feeds the estimate</th><th className="num">Projects</th>{canEdit && <th style={{ width: 30 }} />}</tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {Q.draft.map((q: QT, i) => (
                <tr key={q.id}>
                  <td>{canEdit ? <Input aria-label="Quantity label" className={cellInp} value={q.label} onChange={(e) => Q.set((d) => { d[i].label = e.target.value; return d })} /> : q.label}</td>
                  <td>{canEdit ? <Select aria-label="Unit" className={cellInp} value={q.unit} onChange={(e) => Q.set((d) => { d[i].unit = e.target.value; return d })} options={unitOpts} /> : <span className="font-mono">{q.unit}</span>}</td>
                  <td>{canEdit ? <Select aria-label="Feeds" className={cellInp} value={q.feeds ?? ''} onChange={(e) => Q.set((d) => { d[i].feeds = (e.target.value || undefined) as QT['feeds']; return d })} options={FEEDS} /> : <span className="text-[12px]">{FEEDS.find((f) => f.value === (q.feeds ?? ''))?.label}</span>}</td>
                  <td className="num text-muted">{qtUse(q.id)}</td>
                  {canEdit && <td>{!qtUse(q.id) && <button className="text-muted hover:text-bad" aria-label={`Remove ${q.label}`} onClick={() => Q.set((d) => d.filter((_, k) => k !== i))}>✕</button>}</td>}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
        <SaveBar dirty={Q.dirty} onSave={saveQt} onDiscard={Q.reset} disabled={Q.draft.some((q) => !q.label.trim() || !U.draft.some((u) => u.id === q.unit))} note="The Class 5 rate-table estimate reads length, sites, capacity, tie-ins and services through these." />
      </Panel>
    </div>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Scope library
function Lines({ id, value, onChange, disabled }: { id: string; value: string[]; onChange: (v: string[]) => void; disabled: boolean }) {
  const [txt, setTxt] = useState(value.join('\n'))
  const joined = value.join('\n')
  const last = useRef(joined)
  useEffect(() => { if (joined !== last.current) { setTxt(joined); last.current = joined } }, [joined])
  return <TextArea id={id} disabled={disabled} className="min-h-[260px] text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed" value={txt} onChange={(e) => { setTxt(e.target.value); const v = e.target.value.split('\n').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean); last.current = v.join('\n'); onChange(v) }} />
}

export function ScopeLibrary({ s, canEdit }: { s: AppState; canEdit: boolean }) {
  const L = useDraft(s.settings.estimating.scopeLibrary)
  const cols: { k: keyof EstimatingConfig['scopeLibrary']; title: string; hint: string }[] = [
    { k: 'inScope', title: 'In scope', hint: 'Picklist for scope statements' },
    { k: 'outOfScope', title: 'Out of scope', hint: 'Exclusions offered to PMs' },
    { k: 'assumptions', title: 'Assumptions', hint: 'Standard assumptions' },
  ]
  const save = () => {
    const o = s.settings.estimating.scopeLibrary
    const ch = cols.map((c) => { const a = L.draft[c.k].filter((x) => !o[c.k].includes(x)).length, r = o[c.k].filter((x) => !L.draft[c.k].includes(x)).length; return a || r ? `${c.title.toLowerCase()} +${a}/−${r}` : '' }).filter(Boolean)
    saveSection('scope-library', `Scope library: ${ch.join('; ') || 'reordered'}`, (d) => { d.settings.estimating.scopeLibrary = { inScope: [...L.draft.inScope], outOfScope: [...L.draft.outOfScope], assumptions: [...L.draft.assumptions] } }, 'Scope library saved')
    L.commit()
  }
  return (
    <Panel pad={false} title="Scope library" subtitle="Standard in-scope items, exclusions and assumptions offered when a scope statement is written. One item per line.">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 p-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {cols.map((c) => (
          <div key={c.k} className="min-w-0">
            <div className="mb-1 flex items-baseline justify-between"><span className="font-display text-[14px] font-bold">{c.title}</span><span className="text-xs text-muted">{L.draft[c.k].length} · {c.hint}</span></div>
            <Lines id={`lib-${c.k}`} disabled={!canEdit} value={L.draft[c.k]} onChange={(v) => L.set((d) => ({ ...d, [c.k]: v }))} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <SaveBar dirty={L.dirty} onSave={save} onDiscard={L.reset} />
    </Panel>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Maturity / class checklist
export function MaturityAdmin({ s, canEdit }: { s: AppState; canEdit: boolean }) {
  const M = useDraft(s.settings.estimating.maturityItems)
  const drafts = s.estimates.filter((e) => e.status === 'draft').length
  const keyOk = (k: string, i: number) => /^[a-z0-9-]+$/.test(k) && M.draft.findIndex((x) => x.key === k) === i
  const valid = M.draft.every((m, i) => m.label.trim() && keyOk(m.key, i)) && [5, 4, 3, 2, 1].every((c) => M.draft.some((m) => m.forClass === c) || c === 2 || c === 1)
  const save = () => {
    const o = s.settings.estimating.maturityItems
    const ch: string[] = []
    for (const m of M.draft) { const x = o.find((y) => y.key === m.key); if (!x) ch.push(`added "${m.label}" (Class ${m.forClass})`); else if (x.forClass !== m.forClass) ch.push(`"${m.label}" Class ${x.forClass} → ${m.forClass}`); else if (x.label !== m.label || x.hint !== m.hint) ch.push(`edited "${m.label}"`) }
    for (const x of o) if (!M.draft.some((m) => m.key === x.key)) ch.push(`removed "${x.label}"`)
    if (!ch.length && JSON.stringify(o.map((x) => x.key)) !== JSON.stringify(M.draft.map((x) => x.key))) ch.push('reordered')
    saveSection('maturity', `Maturity checklist: ${ch.join('; ') || 'no change'}`, (d) => { d.settings.estimating.maturityItems = M.draft.map((x) => ({ ...x })) }, 'Maturity checklist saved')
    M.commit()
  }
  const move = (i: number, dir: -1 | 1) => M.set((d) => { const j = i + dir; if (j < 0 || j >= d.length) return d; [d[i], d[j]] = [d[j], d[i]]; return d })
  return (
    <Panel pad={false} title="Maturity and class checklist" subtitle="An estimate's AACE class is computed from these items: it reaches a class when every item for that class and every coarser class is complete."
      actions={canEdit && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => M.set((d) => [...d, { key: `item-${Date.now().toString(36).slice(-5)}`, label: 'New maturity item', forClass: 3, hint: '' }])}>Add item</Button>}>
      <div className="scroll-x">
        <table className="tbl min-w-[760px]">
          <thead><tr><th style={{ width: 150 }}>Key</th><th>Item</th><th>Hint</th><th style={{ width: 120 }}>Needed for</th>{canEdit && <th style={{ width: 90 }} />}</tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {M.draft.map((m, i) => {
              const isNew = !s.settings.estimating.maturityItems.some((x) => x.key === m.key)
              return (
                <tr key={i}>
                  <td>{canEdit && isNew ? <Input aria-label="Key" className={cx(cellInp, 'font-mono', !keyOk(m.key, i) && 'border-bad/60')} value={m.key} onChange={(e) => M.set((d) => { d[i].key = e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-'); return d })} /> : <span className="font-mono text-[12px] text-muted">{m.key}</span>}</td>
                  <td>{canEdit ? <Input aria-label="Item" className={cellInp} value={m.label} onChange={(e) => M.set((d) => { d[i].label = e.target.value; return d })} /> : <b className="font-semibold">{m.label}</b>}</td>
                  <td>{canEdit ? <Input aria-label="Hint" className={cellInp} value={m.hint} onChange={(e) => M.set((d) => { d[i].hint = e.target.value; return d })} /> : <span className="text-[12px] text-muted">{m.hint}</span>}</td>
                  <td>{canEdit ? <Select aria-label="Class" className={cellInp} value={String(m.forClass)} onChange={(e) => M.set((d) => { d[i].forClass = Number(e.target.value) as AaceClass; return d })} options={[5, 4, 3, 2, 1].map((c) => ({ value: String(c), label: `Class ${c}` }))} /> : <ClassBadge cls={m.forClass} />}</td>
                  {canEdit && <td className="whitespace-nowrap text-right">
                    <button className="px-1 text-muted hover:text-ink disabled:opacity-30" disabled={i === 0} aria-label="Move up" onClick={() => move(i, -1)}>↑</button>
                    <button className="px-1 text-muted hover:text-ink disabled:opacity-30" disabled={i === M.draft.length - 1} aria-label="Move down" onClick={() => move(i, 1)}>↓</button>
                    <button className="px-1 text-muted hover:text-bad" aria-label="Remove item" onClick={() => M.set((d) => d.filter((_, k) => k !== i))}>✕</button>
                  </td>}
                </tr>
              )
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 border-t border-line px-4 py-2 text-xs text-muted">
        {([5, 4, 3, 2, 1] as AaceClass[]).map((c) => <span key={c} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1"><ClassBadge cls={c} /> {M.draft.filter((m) => m.forClass === c).length} item{M.draft.filter((m) => m.forClass === c).length === 1 ? '' : 's'} · {CLASS_RANGES[c].definition}</span>)}
      </div>
      {M.dirty && <div className="px-4 pb-2"><Callout tone="info">Saving recomputes the class of the {drafts} open draft{drafts === 1 ? '' : 's'} as they are edited. Approved and in-review estimates keep the class they were submitted at.</Callout></div>}
      {!valid && M.dirty && <p className="px-4 pb-2 text-xs text-bad">Each item needs a label and a unique lowercase key, and Classes 5, 4 and 3 each need at least one item.</p>}
      <SaveBar dirty={M.dirty} onSave={save} onDiscard={M.reset} disabled={!valid} />
    </Panel>
  )
}

/** Change log for the estimating tables: rate table versions plus every audited change. */
export function ChangeLog({ s }: { s: AppState }) {
  const hist = s.settings.estimating.rateTableHistory.slice().reverse()
  const log = s.audit.filter((a) => a.entity === 'estimating-config' || (a.entity === 'rate') || (a.entity === 'estimate' && (a.action === 'reprice' || a.action === 'keep-rates'))).slice().reverse().slice(0, 200)
  const person = (id: string) => s.people.find((p) => p.id === id)?.name ?? id
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-[360px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
      <Panel pad={false} title="Rate table versions">
        <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
          {hist.map((h, i) => (
            <li key={h.version} className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[13px]">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Badge tone={i === 0 ? 'good' : 'neutral'}>v{h.version}</Badge>{i === 0 && <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-good">In force</span>}<span className="ml-auto text-xs text-muted">{h.at.slice(0, 10)}</span></div>
              <div className="mt-1">{h.note}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-faint">{person(h.byId)}</div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Panel>
      <Panel pad={false} title="Change log" subtitle="Every change to the estimating tables and every bulk repricing decision">
        {log.length ? (
          <div className="max-h-[560px] overflow-y-auto">
            <table className="tbl">
              <thead><tr><th style={{ width: 140 }}>When</th><th style={{ width: 150 }}>Who</th><th>Change</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>{log.map((a) => <tr key={a.id}><td className="text-[12px] tabular-nums">{a.at.slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' ')}</td><td className="text-[12px]">{person(a.actorId)}</td><td className="text-[12.5px]">{a.summary}</td></tr>)}</tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        ) : <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-[13px] text-muted">No changes since the demo began. Edits on any tab here are recorded with who, when and what changed.</p>}
      </Panel>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/admin/RateTable.tsx =====
// Rate table by WBS line item and region. Edits are staged in a working copy and published together as a
// new rate table version with a note (audited per change). Estimates stamp the version they were priced on.
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import type { AppState, AssetType, Terrain, UnitCostRate, WorkMethod } from '@/core/types'
import { getState, mutate, personName } from '@/core/store'
import { diffRates, effectiveByRegion, publishRateTable, regionRowsOf, repriceCandidates } from '@/core/engine/estimatingAdmin'
import { cx, fmtDate, money } from '@/core/util'
import { Badge, Button, Callout, Drawer, Field, Input, Modal, Panel, Select, Table, TextArea, type Column } from '@/ui/kit'
import { toast } from '@/ui/toast'
import { NumInput, SpreadBar } from '../parts'
import { catName, directCategories } from '../lib'
import { useDraft } from './shared'

export const ASSET_LABEL: Record<AssetType, string> = { 'transmission-pipeline': 'Transmission pipeline', 'distribution-main': 'Distribution main', 'compressor-station': 'Compressor station', 'valve-site': 'Valve site', 'storage-field': 'Storage field', 'regulator-station': 'Regulator station' }
export const METHOD_LABEL: Record<WorkMethod, string> = { 'replace-open-trench': 'Replace, open trench', 'replace-hdd': 'Replace, HDD', hydrotest: 'Hydrotest', abandon: 'Abandon', 'valve-automation': 'Valve automation', 'station-rebuild': 'Station rebuild', 'compressor-replacement': 'Compressor replacement', 'inline-inspection': 'In-line inspection' }

/** WBS line item (forecast line) a CBS category rolls up to. */
export function wbsLineOf(s: Pick<AppState, 'settings'>, categoryId: string) {
  return s.settings.forecastLines.find((l) => l.categoryIds.includes(categoryId))
}

const m = (v: number) => money(v, v < 1e4 ? 'full' : 'compact')

export function RateTableAdmin({ s, canEdit, onReprice }: { s: AppState; canEdit: boolean; onReprice: () => void }) {
  const { draft, set, dirty, reset, commit } = useDraft(s.rates)
  const cfg = s.settings.estimating
  const [asset, setAsset] = useState('')
  const [method, setMethod] = useState('')
  const [line, setLine] = useState('')
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [onlyRegional, setOnlyRegional] = useState(false)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<UnitCostRate | 'new' | null>(null)
  const [publishing, setPublishing] = useState(false)
  const [published, setPublished] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const changes = useMemo(() => (dirty ? diffRates(s.rates, draft) : []), [dirty, s.rates, draft])
  const changedIds = useMemo(() => new Set(draft.filter((r) => { const o = s.rates.find((x) => x.id === r.id); return !o || JSON.stringify(o) !== JSON.stringify(r) }).map((r) => r.id)), [draft, s.rates])
  const usage = useMemo(() => { const u: Record<string, number> = {}; for (const e of s.estimates) for (const l of e.lines) if (l.rateId) u[l.rateId] = (u[l.rateId] || 0) + 1; return u }, [s.estimates])
  const base = draft.filter((r) => !r.region)
  const assets = Array.from(new Set(base.map((r) => r.assetType)))
  const methods = Array.from(new Set(base.filter((r) => !asset || r.assetType === asset).map((r) => r.workMethod)))
  const lines = s.settings.forecastLines.filter((l) => base.some((r) => l.categoryIds.includes(r.categoryId)))
  const rows = base.filter((r) => (!asset || r.assetType === asset) && (!method || r.workMethod === method) && (!line || wbsLineOf(s, r.categoryId)?.id === line) && (!q || r.description.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())) && (!onlyRegional || regionRowsOf(draft, r).length > 0))
  const sView = { ...s, rates: draft }
  const regional = draft.filter((r) => r.region).length
  const cols: Column<UnitCostRate>[] = [
    { key: 'wbs', header: 'WBS line', sort: (r) => wbsLineOf(s, r.categoryId)?.code ?? '', render: (r) => { const l = wbsLineOf(s, r.categoryId); return <div className="min-w-[120px]"><span className="font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold">{l?.code ?? '—'}</span><div className="text-[11px] text-faint">{l?.name ?? catName(s, r.categoryId)}</div></div> } },
    { key: 'd', header: 'Item', sort: (r) => r.description, render: (r) => <div className="min-w-[220px]"><div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold">{changedIds.has(r.id) || regionRowsOf(draft, r).some((x) => changedIds.has(x.id)) ? <span className="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-marker" title="Changed — not yet published" /> : null}{r.description}</div><div className="text-[11px] text-faint">{ASSET_LABEL[r.assetType]} · {METHOD_LABEL[r.workMethod]}{r.diameterIn ? ` · ${r.diameterIn}"` : ''}{r.terrain ? ` · ${r.terrain}` : ''}</div></div> },
    { key: 'u', header: 'Unit', sort: (r) => r.unit, render: (r) => <span className={cx('font-mono text-[12px]', !cfg.units.some((u) => u.id === r.unit) && 'font-semibold text-bad')} title={cfg.units.find((u) => u.id === r.unit)?.label ?? 'Not on the allowed-units list'}>{r.unit}</span> },
    { key: 'p50', header: 'Base P50', num: true, sort: (r) => r.p50, render: (r) => <div><b>{m(r.p50)}</b><div className="text-[10.5px] text-faint">{m(r.p10)}–{m(r.p90)}</div></div> },
    ...cfg.regions.map((g): Column<UnitCostRate> => ({
      key: `r-${g}`, header: <span title={`${g}: regional row, else base × ${(cfg.regionFactors[g] ?? 1).toFixed(2)}`}>{g}</span>, num: true,
      render: (r) => { const v = effectiveByRegion(sView, r)[g]; return <span className={cx('tabular-nums', v.regional ? 'font-semibold text-ink' : 'text-muted')} title={v.regional ? `${g} regional rate` : `Base × ${(cfg.regionFactors[g] ?? 1).toFixed(2)}`}>{m(v.value)}{v.regional && <sup className="ml-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold text-link">R</sup>}</span> },
    })),
    { key: 'sp', header: 'Spread', render: (r) => <SpreadBar p10={r.p10} p50={r.p50} p90={r.p90} /> },
    { key: 'n', header: 'n', num: true, sort: (r) => r.sampleSize, render: (r) => <span className={cx(r.sampleSize < 5 && 'font-semibold text-warn')}>{r.sampleSize}</span> },
    { key: 'use', header: 'Used', num: true, sort: (r) => usage[r.id] ?? 0, render: (r) => <span className="text-muted">{(usage[r.id] ?? 0) + regionRowsOf(draft, r).reduce((a, x) => a + (usage[x.id] ?? 0), 0)}</span> },
    { key: 'e', header: '', render: (r) => <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={(ev) => { ev.stopPropagation(); setEditing(r) }}>{canEdit ? 'Edit' : 'View'}</Button> },
  ]
  const publish = (note: string) => {
    let v = 0
    const n = changes.length
    mutate((d) => { v = publishRateTable(d, draft, note).version }, { entity: 'estimating-config', entityId: 'rate-table', action: 'publish', summary: `Published rate table v${cfg.rateTableVersion + 1} (${n} change${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}): ${note}` })
    commit(); setPublishing(false); setPublished(v)
    toast(`Rate table v${v} published`)
  }
  const stale = published ? repriceCandidates(getState()).length : 0
  const last = cfg.rateTableHistory[cfg.rateTableHistory.length - 1]
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      {published && <Callout tone="marker" title={`Rate table v${published} is in force`}>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2"><span>{stale} project{stale === 1 ? '' : 's'} have a current estimate priced on an older rate table. Review them and choose which to reprice.</span><Button size="sm" variant="primary" onClick={onReprice}>Review bulk update</Button></div>
      </Callout>}
      <Panel pad={false} title={<span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">Rate table <Badge tone="ink">v{cfg.rateTableVersion}</Badge></span>}
        subtitle={`${base.length} items by WBS line and region · ${regional} regional rows · published ${last ? `${fmtDate(last.at.slice(0, 10))} by ${personName(s, last.byId)}` : '—'}. Regions without their own row price at base × region factor.`}
        actions={canEdit && <Button size="sm" variant="primary" onClick={() => setEditing('new')}>Add rate</Button>}>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-b border-line px-4 py-2.5">
          <Input id="rt-q" className="h-8 w-44" placeholder="Search items" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} />
          <Select id="rt-asset" className="h-8 w-44" value={asset} onChange={(e) => { setAsset(e.target.value); setMethod('') }} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All asset types' }, ...assets.map((a) => ({ value: a, label: ASSET_LABEL[a] }))]} />
          <Select id="rt-method" className="h-8 w-44" value={method} onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All work methods' }, ...methods.map((a) => ({ value: a, label: METHOD_LABEL[a] }))]} />
          <Select id="rt-line" className="h-8 w-52" value={line} onChange={(e) => setLine(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All WBS lines' }, ...lines.map((l) => ({ value: l.id, label: `${l.code} ${l.name}` }))]} />
          <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs"><input type="checkbox" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" checked={onlyRegional} onChange={(e) => setOnlyRegional(e.target.checked)} />With regional rows</label>
          <span className="ml-auto self-center text-[11.5px] text-muted">{rows.length} shown · <sup className="text-[9px] font-bold text-link">R</sup> regional rate</span>
        </div>
        <Table rows={rows} columns={cols} rowKey={(r) => r.id} maxHeight={560} dense onRowClick={(r) => setEditing(r)} empty={<p className="p-6 text-center text-[13px] text-muted">No items match the filters.</p>} />
        {dirty && changes.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-line bg-marker/10 px-4 py-2">
            <Badge tone="marker">{changes.length} unpublished change{changes.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}</Badge>
            <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-xs text-muted">{changes.slice(0, 2).join(' · ')}{changes.length > 2 ? ` · +${changes.length - 2} more` : ''}</span>
            <span className="ml-auto flex gap-2"><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => { reset(); toast('Changes discarded', 'info') }}>Discard</Button><Button size="sm" variant="primary" onClick={() => setPublishing(true)}>Publish rate table v{cfg.rateTableVersion + 1}</Button></span>
          </div>
        )}
      </Panel>
      <Callout tone="info" title="How estimates use the rate table">
        Line-item (Class 3) and rate-table (Class 5) estimates take each unit cost from this table for the project's region and lock it; only an estimator or the estimating lead can override one, with a reason that prints in the basis of estimate. Units are limited to the allowed-units list (pipe in feet, never inches). Publishing a new version leaves existing estimates as priced; use <button className="font-semibold text-link" onClick={onReprice}>Bulk update</button> to reprice the ones you choose.
      </Callout>
      {editing && <RateDrawer s={s} rates={draft} rate={editing === 'new' ? undefined : editing} canEdit={canEdit} usage={usage} onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
        onApply={(rowsIn, removeIds) => { set((d) => [...d.filter((x) => !removeIds.includes(x.id) && !rowsIn.some((y) => y.id === x.id)), ...rowsIn]); setEditing(null); toast('Staged — publish the rate table to put it in force', 'info') }} />}
      {publishing && <PublishModal s={s} changes={changes} onClose={() => setPublishing(false)} onPublish={publish} />}
    </div>
  )
}

function PublishModal({ s, changes, onClose, onPublish }: { s: AppState; changes: string[]; onClose: () => void; onPublish: (note: string) => void }) {
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const v = s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion + 1
  return (
    <Modal open onClose={onClose} wide title={`Publish rate table v${v}`}
      footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" disabled={note.trim().length < 5} onClick={() => onPublish(note.trim())}>Publish v{v}</Button></>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 text-[13px]">
        <p className="text-muted">Every estimate created or repriced after this stamps <b className="text-ink">rate table v{v}</b>. Existing estimates keep the unit costs they were priced with until the estimating team reprices them.</p>
        <div className="max-h-[260px] overflow-y-auto rounded border border-line">
          <ul className="divide-y divide-line text-[12.5px]">{changes.map((c) => <li key={c} className="px-3 py-1.5">{c}</li>)}</ul>
        </div>
        <Field label="Change note (required)" hint="Shown in the rate table history and the audit trail."><TextArea id="rt-note" className="min-h-[64px]" value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Q4 refresh: West contract construction from the 2026 bid tabs; pipe +5% per mill notice" /></Field>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  )
}

/** Edit one rate-table item and its regional rows. */
function RateDrawer({ s, rates, rate, canEdit, usage, onClose, onApply }: { s: AppState; rates: UnitCostRate[]; rate?: UnitCostRate; canEdit: boolean; usage: Record<string, number>; onClose: () => void; onApply: (rows: UnitCostRate[], removeIds: string[]) => void }) {
  const cfg = s.settings.estimating
  const year = Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4))
  const [r, setR] = useState<UnitCostRate>(() => rate ? { ...rate } : { id: `rate-${Date.now().toString(36)}`, assetType: 'transmission-pipeline', workMethod: 'replace-open-trench', categoryId: 'cat-contract', description: '', unit: 'ft', p10: 0, p50: 0, p90: 0, sampleSize: 1, basisYear: year })
  const [regional, setRegional] = useState<UnitCostRate[]>(() => rate ? regionRowsOf(rates, rate).map((x) => ({ ...x })) : [])
  const removed = rate ? regionRowsOf(rates, rate).filter((x) => !regional.some((y) => y.id === x.id)).map((x) => x.id) : []
  const unitOk = cfg.units.some((u) => u.id === r.unit)
  const order = (x: UnitCostRate) => x.p10 <= x.p50 && x.p50 <= x.p90 && x.p50 > 0
  const valid = r.description.trim() && unitOk && order(r) && regional.every(order)
  const ro = !canEdit
  const used = rate ? (usage[rate.id] ?? 0) : 0
  const addRegion = (g: string) => {
    const f = cfg.regionFactors[g] ?? 1
    setRegional([...regional, { ...r, id: `${r.id}-${g.toLowerCase()}-${Date.now().toString(36).slice(-4)}`, region: g, p10: Math.round(r.p10 * f), p50: Math.round(r.p50 * f), p90: Math.round(r.p90 * f) }])
  }
  const setReg = (id: string, k: 'p10' | 'p50' | 'p90' | 'sampleSize', v: number) => setRegional(regional.map((x) => x.id === id ? { ...x, [k]: Math.round(v) } : x))
  // regional rows follow the item's identity and unit
  const sync = (x: UnitCostRate): UnitCostRate => ({ ...x, assetType: r.assetType, workMethod: r.workMethod, categoryId: r.categoryId, description: r.description, unit: r.unit, diameterIn: r.diameterIn, terrain: r.terrain, basisYear: r.basisYear })
  const line = wbsLineOf(s, r.categoryId)
  return (
    <Drawer open onClose={onClose} width={560} title={rate ? (ro ? 'Rate-table item' : 'Edit rate-table item') : 'Add rate-table item'}
      footer={ro ? <Button onClick={onClose}>Close</Button> : <>
        {rate && !used && <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => onApply([], [rate.id, ...regionRowsOf(rates, rate).map((x) => x.id)])}>Remove item</Button>}
        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
        <Button variant="primary" disabled={!valid} onClick={() => onApply([{ ...r, diameterIn: r.diameterIn || undefined, terrain: r.terrain || undefined }, ...regional.map(sync)], removed)}>Stage changes</Button>
      </>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Field label="Description" className="sm:col-span-2"><Input id="rd-desc" disabled={ro} value={r.description} onChange={(e) => setR({ ...r, description: e.target.value })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Asset type"><Select id="rd-asset" disabled={ro || !!rate} value={r.assetType} onChange={(e) => setR({ ...r, assetType: e.target.value as AssetType })} options={Object.entries(ASSET_LABEL).map(([value, label]) => ({ value, label }))} /></Field>
          <Field label="Work method"><Select id="rd-method" disabled={ro || !!rate} value={r.workMethod} onChange={(e) => setR({ ...r, workMethod: e.target.value as WorkMethod })} options={Object.entries(METHOD_LABEL).map(([value, label]) => ({ value, label }))} /></Field>
          <Field label="Category" hint={line ? `WBS line ${line.code} ${line.name}` : undefined}><Select id="rd-cat" disabled={ro || !!rate} value={r.categoryId} onChange={(e) => setR({ ...r, categoryId: e.target.value })} options={directCategories(s).map((c) => ({ value: c.id, label: `${c.code} ${c.name}` }))} /></Field>
          <Field label="Unit" hint={unitOk ? undefined : <span className="text-bad">Not on the allowed-units list</span>}><Select id="rd-unit" disabled={ro} value={r.unit} onChange={(e) => setR({ ...r, unit: e.target.value })} options={[...(unitOk ? [] : [{ value: r.unit, label: `${r.unit} (not allowed)` }]), ...cfg.units.map((u) => ({ value: u.id, label: `${u.id} · ${u.label}` }))]} /></Field>
          <Field label="Diameter (in, optional)"><NumInput id="rd-dia" disabled={ro || !!rate} value={r.diameterIn ?? 0} min={0} onChange={(v) => setR({ ...r, diameterIn: v || undefined })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Terrain (optional)"><Select id="rd-terrain" disabled={ro || !!rate} value={r.terrain ?? ''} onChange={(e) => setR({ ...r, terrain: (e.target.value || undefined) as Terrain | undefined })} options={[{ value: '', label: 'Any' }, 'urban', 'suburban', 'rural']} /></Field>
          <Field label="P10"><NumInput id="rd-p10" disabled={ro} value={r.p10} min={0} onChange={(v) => setR({ ...r, p10: Math.round(v) })} /></Field>
          <Field label="P50 (median) — base"><NumInput id="rd-p50" disabled={ro} value={r.p50} min={0} onChange={(v) => setR({ ...r, p50: Math.round(v) })} /></Field>
          <Field label="P90"><NumInput id="rd-p90" disabled={ro} value={r.p90} min={0} onChange={(v) => setR({ ...r, p90: Math.round(v) })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Sample size"><NumInput id="rd-n" disabled={ro} value={r.sampleSize} min={1} onChange={(v) => setR({ ...r, sampleSize: Math.max(1, Math.round(v)) })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Basis year ($)"><NumInput id="rd-year" disabled={ro} value={r.basisYear} min={2000} onChange={(v) => setR({ ...r, basisYear: Math.round(v) })} /></Field>
        </div>
        {!order(r) && r.p50 > 0 && <p className="text-xs text-bad">P10 must not exceed P50, and P50 must not exceed P90.</p>}

        <div>
          <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-baseline justify-between"><h3 className="font-display text-[14px] font-bold">Rates by region</h3><span className="text-xs text-muted">Without a regional row: base × region factor</span></div>
          <div className="scroll-x"><table className="tbl">
            <thead><tr><th>Region</th><th className="num">Factor</th><th className="num">P10</th><th className="num">P50</th><th className="num">P90</th><th /></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {cfg.regions.map((g) => {
                const x = regional.find((y) => y.region === g)
                const f = cfg.regionFactors[g] ?? 1
                return (
                  <tr key={g}>
                    <td className="font-semibold">{g}{x && <Badge tone="info" className="ml-1.5">Regional</Badge>}</td>
                    <td className="num text-muted">×{f.toFixed(2)}</td>
                    {x ? <>
                      <td className="num"><NumInput ariaLabel={`${g} P10`} className="h-7 w-24 text-[12px]" disabled={ro} value={x.p10} min={0} onChange={(v) => setReg(x.id, 'p10', v)} /></td>
                      <td className="num"><NumInput ariaLabel={`${g} P50`} className="h-7 w-24 text-[12px]" disabled={ro} value={x.p50} min={0} onChange={(v) => setReg(x.id, 'p50', v)} /></td>
                      <td className="num"><NumInput ariaLabel={`${g} P90`} className="h-7 w-24 text-[12px]" disabled={ro} value={x.p90} min={0} onChange={(v) => setReg(x.id, 'p90', v)} /></td>
                      <td>{!ro && !(usage[x.id] ?? 0) && <button className="text-muted hover:text-bad" aria-label={`Remove ${g} rate`} onClick={() => setRegional(regional.filter((y) => y.id !== x.id))}>✕</button>}</td>
                    </> : <>
                      <td className="num text-faint">{m(r.p10 * f)}</td><td className="num text-muted">{m(r.p50 * f)}</td><td className="num text-faint">{m(r.p90 * f)}</td>
                      <td>{!ro && <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => addRegion(g)}>Set {g} rate</Button>}</td>
                    </>}
                  </tr>
                )
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table></div>
        </div>
        {rate && used > 0 && <Callout tone="info">{used} estimate line{used > 1 ? 's' : ''} reference this item. They keep their unit costs until the estimating team reprices them after the next rate table is published.</Callout>}
      </div>
    </Drawer>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/admin/Reprice.tsx =====
// Bulk estimate update: after the rate table changes, list projects whose current estimate was priced on an
// older version, let the estimating team pick which to reprice (or keep on historical rates), and create the
// repriced draft versions. Before → after shown per project, with line-level detail.
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import type { AppState } from '@/core/types'
import { mutate, personName } from '@/core/store'
import { applyReprice, keepHistorical, repriceCandidates, type RepricePlan } from '@/core/engine/estimatingAdmin'
import { stageName } from '@/core/engine/gates'
import { go, projectHref } from '@/core/router'
import { cx, fmtDate, money, moneyDelta, pct, sum } from '@/core/util'
import { Badge, Button, Callout, ClassBadge, Drawer, Empty, Field, Modal, Panel, Stat, StatusBadge, TextArea } from '@/ui/kit'
import { toast } from '@/ui/toast'
import { ProgramTag } from '../../shared/pickers'
import { displayTitle } from '../lib'

export function BulkReprice({ s, canEdit }: { s: AppState; canEdit: boolean }) {
  const plans = useMemo(() => repriceCandidates(s), [s])
  const v = s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({})
  const key = plans.map((p) => p.estimate.id + (p.kept ? 'k' : '')).join()
  useEffect(() => { setSel(Object.fromEntries(plans.map((p) => [p.estimate.id, p.eligible && !p.kept]))) }, [key]) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<RepricePlan | null>(null)
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState<'reprice' | 'keep' | null>(null)
  const chosen = plans.filter((p) => p.eligible && sel[p.estimate.id])
  const eligible = plans.filter((p) => p.eligible)
  const before = sum(chosen.map((p) => p.before.total)), after = sum(chosen.map((p) => p.after.total))
  const allOn = eligible.length > 0 && eligible.every((p) => sel[p.estimate.id])
  const toggleAll = () => setSel(Object.fromEntries(plans.map((p) => [p.estimate.id, p.eligible && !allOn])))

  const doReprice = (note: string) => {
    let r = { created: 0, updated: 0 }
    const ids = chosen.map((p) => p.estimate.id)
    mutate((d) => { const x = applyReprice(d, ids, note); r = { created: x.created.length, updated: x.updated.length } }, { entity: 'estimating-config', entityId: 'bulk-reprice', action: 'reprice', summary: `Bulk update to rate table v${v}: ${ids.length} estimate${ids.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} repriced (${money(before)} → ${money(after)})${note ? ` — ${note}` : ''}` })
    setConfirm(null)
    toast(`${r.created} new draft version${r.created === 1 ? '' : 's'} created${r.updated ? `, ${r.updated} draft${r.updated === 1 ? '' : 's'} updated` : ''} on rate table v${v}`)
  }
  const doKeep = (note: string) => {
    const ids = chosen.map((p) => p.estimate.id)
    let n = 0
    mutate((d) => { n = keepHistorical(d, ids, note) }, { entity: 'estimating-config', entityId: 'bulk-reprice', action: 'keep-rates', summary: `Kept ${ids.length} estimate${ids.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} on historical rates for rate table v${v} — ${note}` })
    setConfirm(null)
    toast(`${n} estimate${n === 1 ? '' : 's'} kept on historical rates`)
  }

  if (!plans.length) return (
    <Panel title="Bulk estimate update"><Empty title={`Every current estimate is on rate table v${v}`}>When the estimating team publishes a new rate table, projects whose current estimate was priced on an older version appear here to reprice or keep on historical rates. Historical quick estimates price from the historical cost table and are not listed.</Empty></Panel>
  )
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-4">
        <Stat label="Rate table in force" value={`v${v}`} sub={s.settings.estimating.rateTableHistory.slice(-1)[0]?.note} />
        <Stat label="On an older version" value={plans.length} sub={`${eligible.length} can be repriced now`} />
        <Stat label="Selected" value={chosen.length} sub={chosen.length ? `${money(before)} → ${money(after)}` : 'Pick projects below'} />
        <Stat label="Net change" value={chosen.length ? moneyDelta(after - before) : '—'} sub={chosen.length && before ? `${after >= before ? '+' : ''}${pct(after / before - 1, 1)} on the selected estimates` : undefined} tone={chosen.length ? (after > before ? 'bad' : 'good') : undefined} />
      </div>
      <Panel pad={false} title="Projects priced on an older rate table" subtitle="Repricing replaces rate-table unit costs with today's table for the project's region and applies the current loaders, escalation, contingency default and county sales tax. Vendor quotes, analogs, estimator judgment and overrides keep their prices. Approved estimates stay as signed: a new draft version is created."
        actions={canEdit && <>
          <Button size="sm" disabled={!chosen.length} onClick={() => setConfirm('keep')}>Keep historical rates ({chosen.length})</Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant="primary" disabled={!chosen.length} onClick={() => setConfirm('reprice')}>Reprice selected ({chosen.length})</Button>
        </>}>
        <div className="scroll-x">
          <table className="tbl min-w-[980px]">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th style={{ width: 34 }}>{canEdit && <input type="checkbox" aria-label="Select all" className="h-4 w-4" checked={allOn} onChange={toggleAll} />}</th>
                <th>Project</th><th>Current estimate</th><th>Priced on</th><th className="num">Before</th><th className="num">After</th><th className="num">Change</th><th>Lines</th><th>Result</th><th />
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {plans.map((p) => {
                const d = p.after.total - p.before.total
                const n = p.lines.filter((x) => x.status === 'repriced').length
                const kept = p.lines.filter((x) => x.status === 'quote' || x.status === 'override' || x.status === 'manual').length
                return (
                  <tr key={p.estimate.id} className={cx(!p.eligible && 'opacity-70')}>
                    <td>{canEdit && <input type="checkbox" aria-label={`Select ${p.project.code}`} className="h-4 w-4" disabled={!p.eligible} checked={!!sel[p.estimate.id] && p.eligible} onChange={(e) => setSel({ ...sel, [p.estimate.id]: e.target.checked })} />}</td>
                    <td><div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><ProgramTag programId={p.project.programId} /><a className="font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold text-link hover:underline" href={projectHref(p.project.id, 'estimates')}>{p.project.code}</a></div><div className="max-w-[240px] truncate text-[12px] text-muted">{p.project.name}</div><div className="text-[11px] text-faint">{stageName(s, p.project)} · {p.project.location.district}</div></td>
                    <td><div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5"><span className="font-mono text-[12px]">v{p.estimate.version}</span><ClassBadge cls={p.estimate.cls} /><StatusBadge status={p.estimate.status} /></div><div className="max-w-[200px] truncate text-[11px] text-faint">{displayTitle(p.estimate)}</div></td>
                    <td><Badge tone="warn">v{p.fromVersion}</Badge></td>
                    <td className="num">{money(p.before.total)}</td>
                    <td className="num font-semibold">{money(p.after.total)}</td>
                    <td className={cx('num font-semibold', d > 0 ? 'text-bad' : d < 0 ? 'text-good' : 'text-faint')}>{d === 0 ? '—' : <>{moneyDelta(d)}<div className="text-[11px] font-normal">{p.before.total ? `${d > 0 ? '+' : ''}${pct(d / p.before.total, 1)}` : ''}</div></>}</td>
                    <td className="text-[12px]">{n} repriced{kept ? <div className="text-faint">{kept} kept as priced</div> : null}</td>
                    <td className="text-[12px]">
                      {!p.eligible ? <span className="text-warn">{p.reason}</span>
                        : p.kept ? <span><Badge tone="neutral">Kept on historical rates</Badge><div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-faint">{personName(s, p.kept.byId)} · {fmtDate(p.kept.at.slice(0, 10))}</div></span>
                          : p.mode === 'update-draft' ? <>Update draft v{p.estimate.version}</> : <>New draft v{p.preview.version}</>}
                    </td>
                    <td><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setDetail(p)}>Details</Button></td>
                  </tr>
                )
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Panel>
      {!canEdit && <Callout tone="info">Read-only: the estimating team (estimators, estimating lead, administrators) reprices estimates.</Callout>}
      {detail && <PlanDrawer s={s} plan={detail} onClose={() => setDetail(null)} />}
      {confirm && <ConfirmModal kind={confirm} plans={chosen} v={v} before={before} after={after} onClose={() => setConfirm(null)} onConfirm={(note) => confirm === 'reprice' ? doReprice(note) : doKeep(note)} />}
    </div>
  )
}

function ConfirmModal({ kind, plans, v, before, after, onClose, onConfirm }: { kind: 'reprice' | 'keep'; plans: RepricePlan[]; v: number; before: number; after: number; onClose: () => void; onConfirm: (note: string) => void }) {
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const needNote = kind === 'keep'
  const newVersions = plans.filter((p) => p.mode === 'new-version').length
  return (
    <Modal open onClose={onClose} title={kind === 'reprice' ? `Reprice ${plans.length} estimate${plans.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} to rate table v${v}` : `Keep ${plans.length} estimate${plans.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} on historical rates`}
      footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" disabled={needNote && note.trim().length < 5} onClick={() => onConfirm(note.trim())}>{kind === 'reprice' ? 'Create repriced drafts' : 'Keep historical rates'}</Button></>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 text-[13px]">
        {kind === 'reprice' ? <>
          <p>Creates <b>{newVersions}</b> new draft version{newVersions === 1 ? '' : 's'}{plans.length - newVersions ? <> and updates <b>{plans.length - newVersions}</b> open draft{plans.length - newVersions === 1 ? '' : 's'}</> : null}. Totals move from <b>{money(before)}</b> to <b>{money(after)}</b> ({moneyDelta(after - before)}). Each draft routes for approval as usual; nothing changes in a budget until it is approved.</p>
          <ul className="max-h-[180px] overflow-y-auto rounded border border-line text-[12.5px]">{plans.map((p) => <li key={p.estimate.id} className="flex justify-between gap-2 border-b border-line px-3 py-1 last:border-0"><span className="font-mono">{p.project.code} v{p.estimate.version}</span><span className="tabular-nums">{money(p.before.total)} → <b>{money(p.after.total)}</b></span></li>)}</ul>
        </> : <p>Records the decision to leave these estimates on the rate table they were priced with. They stay listed with the decision until the next rate table is published.</p>}
        <Field label={needNote ? 'Reason (required)' : 'Note (optional)'} hint="Recorded in each estimate's audit trail."><TextArea id="rp-note" className="min-h-[60px]" value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} placeholder={needNote ? 'e.g. Construction is under way on bid prices; the control budget is set' : 'e.g. Q4 rate refresh'} /></Field>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  )
}

function PlanDrawer({ s, plan, onClose }: { s: AppState; plan: RepricePlan; onClose: () => void }) {
  const p = plan
  const rows = p.lines.filter((x) => x.status !== 'unchanged')
  const label = { repriced: 'Repriced', override: 'Override kept', quote: 'Quote / analog / judgment kept', manual: 'Not from the rate table', missing: 'Rate removed — kept', unchanged: 'Unchanged' } as const
  const mk = (t: RepricePlan['before']) => [
    ['Direct cost', t.lines], ['Sales tax', t.tax], ['Loaders', t.loaders], ['Escalation', t.escalation], ['Contingency', t.contingency], ['Total', t.total],
  ] as const
  const b = mk(p.before), a = mk(p.after)
  return (
    <Drawer open onClose={onClose} width={640} title={`${p.project.code} · v${p.estimate.version} → rate table v${s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion}`}
      footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => { onClose(); go(projectHref(p.project.id, 'estimates')) }}>Open project estimates</Button><Button onClick={onClose}>Close</Button></>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 text-[13px]">
        <div className="text-muted">{p.project.name} · {p.project.location.district} region · priced on rate table v{p.fromVersion}</div>
        <table className="tbl">
          <thead><tr><th /><th className="num">Before (v{p.estimate.version})</th><th className="num">After</th><th className="num">Change</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>{b.map(([k, x], i) => { const y = a[i][1]; return <tr key={k}><td className={cx(k === 'Total' && 'font-bold')}>{k}</td><td className="num">{money(x)}</td><td className={cx('num', k === 'Total' && 'font-bold')}>{money(y)}</td><td className={cx('num', y > x ? 'text-bad' : y < x ? 'text-good' : 'text-faint')}>{y === x ? '—' : moneyDelta(y - x)}</td></tr> })}</tbody>
        </table>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-[12px]">
          <div className="rounded border border-line px-3 py-2"><div className="eyebrow">Markups before</div>Loaders {sum(p.estimate.loaders.map((l) => l.pct)).toFixed(1)}% · escalation {p.estimate.escalationPct}% · contingency {p.estimate.contingency.pct}% · tax {p.estimate.salesTax ? `${p.estimate.salesTax.pct}%` : 'not applied'}</div>
          <div className="rounded border border-line px-3 py-2"><div className="eyebrow">Markups after</div>Loaders {sum(p.preview.loaders.map((l) => l.pct)).toFixed(1)}% · escalation {p.preview.escalationPct}% · contingency {p.preview.contingency.pct}%{p.preview.contingency.method === 'risk-based' ? ' (risk-based, kept)' : ''} · tax {p.preview.salesTax?.pct ?? 0}%</div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="mb-1 font-display text-[14px] font-bold">Lines that change or are kept ({rows.length} of {p.lines.length})</h3>
          {rows.length ? (
            <div className="scroll-x max-h-[380px] overflow-y-auto rounded border border-line">
              <table className="tbl">
                <thead><tr><th>Line</th><th className="num">Unit cost</th><th className="num">New</th><th>Treatment</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>{rows.map((x) => <tr key={x.line.id}><td className="text-[12px]"><span className="text-faint">{x.line.workPackage}</span> · {x.line.description}<div className="text-[11px] text-faint">{x.line.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')} {x.line.unit}</div></td><td className="num">{money(x.from, x.from < 1e4 ? 'full' : 'compact')}</td><td className={cx('num', x.to > x.from ? 'text-bad' : x.to < x.from ? 'text-good' : '')}>{money(x.to, x.to < 1e4 ? 'full' : 'compact')}</td><td className="text-[11.5px]">{label[x.status]}</td></tr>)}</tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          ) : <p className="text-muted">Every line already matches today's table; only markups change.</p>}
        </div>
        {!p.eligible && <Callout tone="warn">{p.reason}</Callout>}
      </div>
    </Drawer>
  )
}

/** Count used for the tab badge. */
export const staleCount = (s: AppState) => repriceCandidates(s).filter((p) => p.eligible && !p.kept).length

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/admin/shared.tsx =====
// Shared pieces for Estimating administration: a working copy with save/discard, the read-only notice,
// and the audited save into settings.estimating.
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { AppState } from '@/core/types'
import { mutate } from '@/core/store'
import { Badge, Button } from '@/ui/kit'
import { toast } from '@/ui/toast'

const clone = <T,>(x: T): T => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x))

/** Working copy of a slice of the store. Follows the store while nothing is pending. */
export function useDraft<T>(source: T): { draft: T; set: (fn: (d: T) => T) => void; dirty: boolean; reset: () => void; commit: () => void } {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<T>(() => clone(source))
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false)
  const key = JSON.stringify(source)
  const last = useRef(key)
  useEffect(() => { if (key !== last.current) { last.current = key; if (!dirty) setDraft(clone(source)) } }, [key]) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  return {
    draft,
    set: (fn) => { setDraft((d) => fn(clone(d))); setDirty(true) },
    dirty,
    reset: () => { setDraft(clone(source)); setDirty(false) },
    commit: () => setDirty(false),
  }
}

/** Save a section of the estimating tables with one audit entry. */
export function saveSection(section: string, summary: string, fn: (d: AppState) => void, msg = 'Saved') {
  mutate(fn, { entity: 'estimating-config', entityId: section, action: 'update', summary })
  toast(msg)
}

/** Footer bar for a section with pending changes. */
export function SaveBar({ dirty, onSave, onDiscard, disabled, note }: { dirty: boolean; onSave: () => void; onDiscard: () => void; disabled?: boolean; note?: React.ReactNode }) {
  if (!dirty) return null
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-line bg-marker/10 px-4 py-2">
      <Badge tone="marker">Unsaved changes</Badge>
      {note && <span className="text-xs text-muted">{note}</span>}
      <span className="ml-auto flex gap-2"><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={onDiscard}>Discard</Button><Button size="sm" variant="primary" disabled={disabled} onClick={onSave}>Save changes</Button></span>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Compact list of what changed between two flat records, for audit summaries. */
export function diffRecord(a: Record<string, unknown>, b: Record<string, unknown>, fmt: (v: unknown) => string = String): string[] {
  const keys = Array.from(new Set([...Object.keys(a), ...Object.keys(b)]))
  return keys.filter((k) => a[k] !== b[k]).map((k) => a[k] == null ? `added ${k} ${fmt(b[k])}` : b[k] == null ? `removed ${k}` : `${k} ${fmt(a[k])} → ${fmt(b[k])}`)
}

export const cellInp = 'h-7 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px]'

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/admin/Tables.tsx =====
// Portfolio tables maintained by the estimating team: regions and factors, county sales tax and taxable
// categories, loaders (locked on projects), escalation by year and contingency by AACE class.
import { useState } from 'react'
import type { AaceClass, AppState } from '@/core/types'
import { CLASS_RANGES } from '@/core/engine/estimates'
import { cx, pct, sum } from '@/core/util'
import { Badge, Button, Callout, Checkbox, ClassBadge, Input, Panel } from '@/ui/kit'
import { NumInput } from '../parts'
import { directCategories } from '../lib'
import { SaveBar, cellInp, diffRecord, saveSection, useDraft } from './shared'

// ───────────────────────────── Regions, factors and sales tax
export function RegionsTax({ s, canEdit }: { s: AppState; canEdit: boolean }) {
  const cfg = s.settings.estimating
  const f = useDraft(cfg.regionFactors)
  const t = useDraft({ taxRates: cfg.taxRates, taxable: cfg.taxableCategoryIds })
  const [county, setCounty] = useState('')
  const projectsIn = (g: string) => s.projects.filter((p) => p.location.district === g && p.status !== 'closed').length
  const countyUse = (c: string) => s.projects.filter((p) => (p.counties ?? [{ county: p.location.county, pct: 100 }]).some((x) => x.county === c)).length
  const split = s.projects.filter((p) => (p.counties?.length ?? 0) > 1)
  const unknown = Array.from(new Set(s.projects.flatMap((p) => (p.counties ?? []).map((c) => c.county)))).filter((c) => !t.draft.taxRates.some((x) => x.county === c))
  const saveFactors = () => {
    const ch = diffRecord(cfg.regionFactors, f.draft, (v) => `×${Number(v).toFixed(2)}`)
    saveSection('region-factors', `Region factors: ${ch.join('; ') || 'no change'}`, (d) => { d.settings.estimating.regionFactors = { ...f.draft }; d.settings.quickEstimate.regionFactors = { ...f.draft } }, 'Region factors saved')
    f.commit()
  }
  const saveTax = () => {
    const ch = diffRecord(Object.fromEntries(cfg.taxRates.map((x) => [x.county, x.rate])), Object.fromEntries(t.draft.taxRates.map((x) => [x.county, x.rate])), (v) => `${v}%`)
    const added = t.draft.taxable.filter((c) => !cfg.taxableCategoryIds.includes(c)), removed = cfg.taxableCategoryIds.filter((c) => !t.draft.taxable.includes(c))
    if (added.length) ch.push(`taxable + ${added.join(', ')}`)
    if (removed.length) ch.push(`taxable − ${removed.join(', ')}`)
    saveSection('sales-tax', `Sales tax table: ${ch.join('; ') || 'no change'}`, (d) => { d.settings.estimating.taxRates = t.draft.taxRates.map((x) => ({ ...x })); d.settings.estimating.taxableCategoryIds = [...t.draft.taxable] }, 'Sales tax table saved')
    t.commit()
  }
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-2">
      <Panel pad={false} title="Regions and region factors" subtitle="Region (not terrain) selects regional rates. Without a regional row, the base rate × the factor applies.">
        <table className="tbl">
          <thead><tr><th>Region</th><th className="num" style={{ width: 120 }}>Factor</th><th className="num">vs base</th><th className="num">Active projects</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {cfg.regions.map((g) => (
              <tr key={g}>
                <td className="font-semibold">{g}</td>
                <td className="num">{canEdit ? <NumInput ariaLabel={`${g} factor`} className={cellInp} value={f.draft[g] ?? 1} min={0.5} step={0.01} onChange={(v) => f.set((d) => ({ ...d, [g]: Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000 }))} /> : `×${(cfg.regionFactors[g] ?? 1).toFixed(2)}`}</td>
                <td className={cx('num', (f.draft[g] ?? 1) > 1 ? 'text-bad' : (f.draft[g] ?? 1) < 1 ? 'text-good' : 'text-faint')}>{(f.draft[g] ?? 1) === 1 ? '—' : `${(f.draft[g] ?? 1) > 1 ? '+' : ''}${pct((f.draft[g] ?? 1) - 1)}`}</td>
                <td className="num">{projectsIn(g)}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        <SaveBar dirty={f.dirty} onSave={saveFactors} onDiscard={f.reset} note="Also used by historical quick estimates." />
      </Panel>

      <Panel pad={false} title="County sales tax" subtitle={`Applied to taxable categories. Projects that cross a county line split the tax by their county shares (${split.length} project${split.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} do today).`}>
        <table className="tbl">
          <thead><tr><th>County</th><th className="num" style={{ width: 120 }}>Rate %</th><th className="num">Projects</th>{canEdit && <th style={{ width: 30 }} />}</tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {t.draft.taxRates.map((x, i) => (
              <tr key={x.county}>
                <td>{x.county}</td>
                <td className="num">{canEdit ? <NumInput ariaLabel={`${x.county} rate`} className={cellInp} value={x.rate} min={0} step={0.05} onChange={(v) => t.set((d) => { d.taxRates[i].rate = Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000; return d })} /> : `${x.rate}%`}</td>
                <td className="num">{countyUse(x.county)}</td>
                {canEdit && <td>{!countyUse(x.county) && <button className="text-muted hover:text-bad" aria-label={`Remove ${x.county}`} onClick={() => t.set((d) => { d.taxRates.splice(i, 1); return d })}>✕</button>}</td>}
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        {canEdit && <form className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-line px-4 py-2" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); const c = county.trim(); if (!c || t.draft.taxRates.some((x) => x.county === c)) return; t.set((d) => { d.taxRates.push({ county: c, rate: 0 }); return d }); setCounty('') }}>
          <Input id="tax-county" className="h-8 w-56" placeholder="Add county" value={county} onChange={(e) => setCounty(e.target.value)} />
          <Button size="sm" type="submit" disabled={!county.trim()}>Add</Button>
        </form>}
        {unknown.length > 0 && <div className="border-t border-line px-4 py-2 text-xs text-warn">No rate for {unknown.join(', ')} — projects there price with 0% tax.</div>}
        <div className="border-t border-line px-4 py-3">
          <div className="eyebrow mb-1.5">Taxable categories</div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {directCategories(s).map((c) => <Checkbox key={c.id} id={`tax-${c.id}`} disabled={!canEdit} checked={t.draft.taxable.includes(c.id)} onChange={(v) => t.set((d) => ({ ...d, taxable: v ? [...d.taxable, c.id] : d.taxable.filter((x) => x !== c.id) }))} label={<span className="text-[12.5px]"><span className="font-mono text-faint">{c.code}</span> {c.name}</span>} />)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <SaveBar dirty={t.dirty} onSave={saveTax} onDiscard={t.reset} />
      </Panel>
    </div>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Loaders, escalation, contingency
export function Markups({ s, canEdit }: { s: AppState; canEdit: boolean }) {
  const cfg = s.settings.estimating
  const L = useDraft(s.settings.loaderRates)
  const E = useDraft(s.settings.escalationByYear)
  const C = useDraft(cfg.contingencyByClass)
  const years = Array.from(new Set(L.draft.map((r) => r.year))).sort((a, b) => b - a)
  const latest = years[0] ?? Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4))
  const escYears = Object.keys(E.draft).sort()
  const saveLoaders = () => {
    const a = Object.fromEntries(s.settings.loaderRates.map((r) => [`${r.year} ${r.name}`, r.pct])), b = Object.fromEntries(L.draft.map((r) => [`${r.year} ${r.name}`, r.pct]))
    saveSection('loaders', `Portfolio loaders: ${diffRecord(a, b, (v) => `${v}%`).join('; ') || 'no change'}`, (d) => { d.settings.loaderRates = L.draft.map((x) => ({ ...x })) }, 'Loaders saved')
    L.commit()
  }
  const saveEsc = () => {
    saveSection('escalation', `Escalation by year: ${diffRecord(s.settings.escalationByYear, E.draft, (v) => `${v}%`).join('; ') || 'no change'}`, (d) => { d.settings.escalationByYear = { ...E.draft } }, 'Escalation table saved')
    E.commit()
  }
  const saveCont = () => {
    saveSection('contingency', `Contingency by class: ${diffRecord(cfg.contingencyByClass, C.draft, (v) => `${v}%`).map((x) => `Class ${x}`).join('; ') || 'no change'}`, (d) => { d.settings.estimating.contingencyByClass = { ...C.draft }; if (C.draft['5'] != null) d.settings.quickEstimate.contingencyPct = C.draft['5'] }, 'Contingency table saved')
    C.commit()
  }
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 xl:grid-cols-2">
      <Panel pad={false} title={<span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">Loaders <Badge tone="ink">Portfolio-wide · locked on projects</Badge></span>} subtitle="Payroll taxes, AFUDC and overheads applied to direct cost. Project estimates read the latest year and cannot change it."
        actions={canEdit && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => L.set((d) => [...d, ...d.filter((r) => r.year === latest).map((r) => ({ ...r, year: latest + 1 }))])} disabled={years.includes(latest + 1)}>Start {latest + 1} table</Button>}>
        <table className="tbl">
          <thead><tr><th className="num" style={{ width: 70 }}>Year</th><th>Loader</th><th className="num" style={{ width: 110 }}>Rate %</th>{canEdit && <th style={{ width: 30 }} />}</tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {years.map((y) => {
              const rows = L.draft.map((r, i) => ({ r, i })).filter((x) => x.r.year === y)
              return [
                ...rows.map(({ r, i }) => (
                  <tr key={`${y}-${i}`} className={cx(y !== latest && 'text-muted')}>
                    <td className="num">{y}{y === latest && <div><Badge tone="good">In force</Badge></div>}</td>
                    <td>{canEdit ? <Input aria-label="Loader name" className={cellInp} value={r.name} onChange={(e) => L.set((d) => { d[i].name = e.target.value; return d })} /> : r.name}</td>
                    <td className="num">{canEdit ? <NumInput ariaLabel="Loader rate" className={cellInp} value={r.pct} min={0} step={0.1} onChange={(v) => L.set((d) => { d[i].pct = Math.round(v * 100) / 100; return d })} /> : `${r.pct}%`}</td>
                    {canEdit && <td><button className="text-muted hover:text-bad" aria-label="Remove loader" onClick={() => L.set((d) => d.filter((_, k) => k !== i))}>✕</button></td>}
                  </tr>
                )),
                <tr key={`${y}-tot`} className="bg-sunken/40"><td /><td className="text-[12px] font-semibold">Total {y}{canEdit && <button className="ml-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-link" onClick={() => L.set((d) => [...d, { year: y, name: 'New loader', pct: 0 }])}>+ Add loader</button>}</td><td className="num font-semibold">{sum(rows.map((x) => x.r.pct)).toFixed(1)}%</td>{canEdit && <td />}</tr>,
              ]
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        <SaveBar dirty={L.dirty} onSave={saveLoaders} onDiscard={L.reset} disabled={L.draft.some((r) => !r.name.trim())} note="Open drafts are asked to apply the new table; approved estimates keep theirs." />
      </Panel>

      <Panel pad={false} title={<span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">Escalation by year <Badge tone="ink">Portfolio-wide · locked on projects</Badge></span>} subtitle="Compounded from the rate basis year to each project's spend midpoint."
        actions={canEdit && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => { const y = Math.max(...escYears.map(Number)) + 1; E.set((d) => ({ ...d, [y]: d[String(y - 1)] ?? 3 })) }}>Add year</Button>}>
        <table className="tbl">
          <thead><tr><th>Year</th><th className="num" style={{ width: 120 }}>Escalation %</th><th className="num">Cumulative from {escYears[0] ?? '—'}</th>{canEdit && <th style={{ width: 30 }} />}</tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {escYears.map((y, i) => {
              const cum = escYears.slice(1, i + 1).reduce((f, k) => f * (1 + (E.draft[k] ?? 0) / 100), 1) - 1
              return (
                <tr key={y}>
                  <td className="font-mono">{y}</td>
                  <td className="num">{canEdit ? <NumInput ariaLabel={`${y} escalation`} className={cellInp} value={E.draft[y]} min={-10} step={0.1} onChange={(v) => E.set((d) => ({ ...d, [y]: Math.round(v * 100) / 100 }))} /> : `${E.draft[y]}%`}</td>
                  <td className="num text-muted">{i === 0 ? 'base' : `+${pct(cum, 1)}`}</td>
                  {canEdit && <td>{i === escYears.length - 1 && escYears.length > 1 && <button className="text-muted hover:text-bad" aria-label={`Remove ${y}`} onClick={() => E.set((d) => { const x = { ...d }; delete x[y]; return x })}>✕</button>}</td>}
                </tr>
              )
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        <SaveBar dirty={E.dirty} onSave={saveEsc} onDiscard={E.reset} />
      </Panel>

      <Panel pad={false} className="xl:col-span-2" title="Contingency by AACE class" subtitle="Default deterministic contingency for new and repriced estimates. Risk-based contingency (Monte Carlo on the risk register) can replace it on an estimate.">
        <div className="px-4 pt-3">
          <Callout tone="marker" title="Contingency policy — pending">
            Contingency percentages and their use will follow the forthcoming <b>contingency drawdown policy</b> (how contingency is held, who may release it and how drawdowns are evidenced). Until it is issued these defaults apply; this note will be replaced with the policy reference and its effective date.
          </Callout>
        </div>
        <div className="scroll-x">
          <table className="tbl">
            <thead><tr><th>Class</th><th>Use</th><th className="num">AACE range</th><th className="num" style={{ width: 130 }}>Contingency %</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {([5, 4, 3, 2, 1] as AaceClass[]).map((c) => (
                <tr key={c}>
                  <td><ClassBadge cls={c} /></td>
                  <td className="text-[12px]">{CLASS_RANGES[c].use}</td>
                  <td className="num text-[12px]">{pct(CLASS_RANGES[c].low)} / +{pct(CLASS_RANGES[c].high)}</td>
                  <td className="num">{canEdit ? <NumInput ariaLabel={`Class ${c} contingency`} className={cellInp} value={C.draft[String(c)] ?? 0} min={0} step={0.5} onChange={(v) => C.set((d) => ({ ...d, [String(c)]: Math.round(v * 10) / 10 }))} /> : `${cfg.contingencyByClass[String(c)] ?? '—'}%`}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
        <SaveBar dirty={C.dirty} onSave={saveCont} onDiscard={C.reset} note="Class 5 also sets the historical quick estimate contingency." />
      </Panel>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/boe.ts =====
// Basis of estimate downloads: PDF (jsPDF, bundled) and CSV (opens in Excel). Both read the same facts
// as the on-screen BOE, so the three formats agree line for line.
import { jsPDF } from 'jspdf'
import type { AppState, Estimate, MonteCarloResult } from '@/core/types'
import { CLASS_RANGES, CONT_CAT, estimateTotals, lineTotal, maturityItems } from '@/core/engine/estimates'
import { pricedOnVersion } from '@/core/engine/estimatingAdmin'
import { fmtDate, pct, sum } from '@/core/util'
import { SOURCE_LABEL, benchmarkExceptions, catCode, catName, displayTitle, methodLabel, sourceSummary, workPackages } from './lib'

const usd = (n: number | undefined) => n == null ? '' : '$' + Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-US')
const usd2 = (n: number) => '$' + n.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: n < 1000 && n % 1 ? 2 : 0, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })

// VERIFY: BOE section wording, class range statements and contingency rationale text are AI-drafted; the estimating lead signs off.
/** Everything a BOE shows, as plain values. */
export function boeFacts(s: AppState, e: Estimate, opts: { mc?: MonteCarloResult } = {}) {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === e.projectId)!
  const t = estimateTotals(e)
  const person = (id?: string) => s.people.find((x) => x.id === id)?.name ?? '-'
  const prog = s.programs.find((x) => x.id === p.programId)
  const r = CLASS_RANGES[e.cls]
  const wf = s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === e.workflowInstanceId)
  const def = wf ? s.workflowDefs.find((d) => d.id === wf.defId) : undefined
  const cats = Object.entries(t.direct).sort((a, b) => catCode(s, a[0]).localeCompare(catCode(s, b[0])))
  return {
    p, t, r, prog, wf, def, cats, person,
    title: `${p.code} Basis of Estimate - v${e.version} Class ${e.cls}`,
    rateVersion: e.method === 'quick' ? undefined : pricedOnVersion(s, e),
    srcs: sourceSummary(e),
    ex: benchmarkExceptions(e, s.rates, s.settings.estimating.regionFactors),
    overrides: e.lines.filter((l) => l.override),
    wps: workPackages(e),
    maturity: maturityItems(s),
    mc: opts.mc && e.contingency.method === 'risk-based' ? opts.mc : undefined,
    history: wf ? wf.history.filter((h) => h.action !== 'system').map((h) => ({ at: h.at.slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' '), who: h.actorId === 'system' ? 'System' : person(h.actorId), action: h.action + (h.signature ? ' (e-signed)' : ''), step: def?.nodes.find((n) => n.id === h.nodeId)?.label ?? '', comment: h.comment ?? '' })) : [],
  }
}

// ───────────────────────────── CSV
const cell = (v: unknown) => { const x = v == null ? '' : String(v); return /[",\n]/.test(x) ? '"' + x.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : x }

/** BOE as one CSV sheet: header facts, summary, categories, markups and every line item. UTF-8 with BOM for Excel. */
export function boeCsv(s: AppState, e: Estimate, opts: { mc?: MonteCarloResult } = {}): string {
  const f = boeFacts(s, e, opts)
  const { p, t } = f
  const rows: unknown[][] = []
  const add = (...r: unknown[]) => rows.push(r)
  add('Basis of Estimate'); add('Organization', s.settings.orgName); add('Project', p.code, p.name); add('Program', f.prog?.code, f.prog?.name)
  add('Estimate', `v${e.version}`, displayTitle(e)); add('AACE class', e.cls); add('Status', e.status); add('Method', methodLabel(e.method))
  if (f.rateVersion) add('Rate table version', f.rateVersion)
  add('Region', e.region ?? p.location.district)
  add('Prepared by', f.person(e.createdById), e.createdAt.slice(0, 10))
  if (e.approvedAt) add('Approved by', f.person(e.approvedById), e.approvedAt.slice(0, 10))
  add('Generated', s.settings.asOf)
  add()
  add('Summary', 'Amount', '% / note')
  add('Direct cost (before tax)', t.lines)
  if (t.tax) add('Sales tax on material', t.tax, `${e.salesTax?.pct}% (${e.salesTax?.counties.map((c) => `${c.county} ${Math.round(c.share * 100)}% at ${c.rate}%`).join('; ')})`)
  add('Loaders', t.loaders, `${sum(e.loaders.map((l) => l.pct)).toFixed(1)}%`)
  add('Escalation', t.escalation, `${e.escalationPct}%`)
  add('Base estimate', t.base)
  add('Contingency', t.contingency, `${e.contingency.pct}% ${e.contingency.method}${e.contingency.method === 'risk-based' ? ' ' + (e.contingency.confidence ?? 'P80') : ''}`)
  add('Total estimate', t.total)
  add('Expected range low', t.low, pct(f.r.low)); add('Expected range high', t.high, '+' + pct(f.r.high))
  add()
  add('Code', 'Category', 'ERP cost element', 'Direct', 'Incl. sales tax', '% of base')
  for (const [c, v] of f.cats) add(catCode(s, c), catName(s, c), s.categories.find((x) => x.id === c)?.erpCostElement, v, t.byCategory[c], t.base ? (t.byCategory[c] / t.base * 100).toFixed(1) + '%' : '')
  add('900', 'Loaders', s.categories.find((x) => x.id === 'cat-loaders')?.erpCostElement, t.loaders, t.loaders)
  add('950', 'Escalation', s.categories.find((x) => x.id === 'cat-escalation')?.erpCostElement, t.escalation, t.escalation)
  add('990', 'Contingency', s.categories.find((x) => x.id === CONT_CAT)?.erpCostElement, t.contingency, t.contingency)
  add()
  add('Loader', 'Rate %', 'Amount')
  for (const l of e.loaders) add(l.name, l.pct, Math.round(t.lines * l.pct / 100))
  add()
  add('Work package', 'Code', 'Category', 'Description', 'Qty', 'Unit', 'Unit cost', 'Line total', 'Source', 'Confidence', 'Rate id', 'Override reason', 'Overridden from')
  for (const l of e.lines) add(l.workPackage, catCode(s, l.categoryId), catName(s, l.categoryId), l.description, l.qty, l.unit, l.unitCost, lineTotal(l), SOURCE_LABEL[l.source], l.confidence, l.rateId ?? '', l.override?.reason ?? '', l.override?.fromUnitCost ?? '')
  add()
  add('Scope basis', e.basis.scopeBasis)
  for (const q of p.scope.quantities) add('Scope quantity', q.label, q.qty, q.unit)
  for (const a of e.basis.assumptions) add('Assumption', a)
  for (const a of e.basis.exclusions) add('Exclusion', a)
  add('Contingency rationale', e.basis.contingencyRationale)
  add('Class justification', e.basis.classJustification)
  for (const m of f.maturity) add('Maturity item', m.label, `Class ${m.forClass}`, e.maturity[m.key] ? 'Complete' : 'Not complete')
  for (const h of f.history) add('Approval history', h.at, h.who, h.action, h.step, h.comment)
  return '﻿' + rows.map((r) => r.map(cell).join(',')).join('\r\n')
}

// ───────────────────────────── PDF
/** Characters the standard PDF fonts cannot draw are replaced with plain equivalents. */
const clean = (x: unknown) => String(x ?? '')
  .replace(/[→⇒]/g, '->').replace(/[←]/g, '<-').replace(/≥/g, '>=').replace(/≤/g, '<=').replace(/×/g, 'x')
  .replace(/[–—]/g, '-').replace(/[‘’]/g, "'").replace(/[“”]/g, '"').replace(/…/g, '...').replace(/[•·]/g, '-')
  .replace(/[^\x09\x0A\x0D\x20-\x7E\xA0-\xFF]/g, '')

type Align = 'left' | 'right'
interface Col { label: string; w: number; align?: Align }

/** A small page-flow writer over jsPDF: headings, wrapped paragraphs and tables with repeating headers. */
class Writer {
  doc = new jsPDF({ unit: 'pt', format: 'letter' })
  x = 48
  y = 56
  W = 612 - 96
  bottom = 792 - 54
  constructor(private footer: string) {}
  ensure(h: number) { if (this.y + h > this.bottom) { this.doc.addPage(); this.y = 56 } }
  h1(t: string) { this.doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold'); this.doc.setFontSize(20); this.doc.setTextColor(20, 27, 36); this.doc.text(clean(t), this.x, this.y); this.y += 18 }
  h2(t: string) {
    this.ensure(40); this.y += 10
    this.doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold'); this.doc.setFontSize(12.5); this.doc.setTextColor(20, 27, 36); this.doc.text(clean(t), this.x, this.y)
    this.y += 5; this.doc.setDrawColor(20, 27, 36); this.doc.setLineWidth(1.2); this.doc.line(this.x, this.y, this.x + this.W, this.y); this.y += 13
  }
  h3(t: string) { this.ensure(26); this.doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold'); this.doc.setFontSize(10.5); this.doc.setTextColor(20, 27, 36); this.doc.text(clean(t), this.x, this.y); this.y += 13 }
  p(t: string, o: { size?: number; muted?: boolean; bold?: boolean } = {}) {
    const size = o.size ?? 9.5
    this.doc.setFont('helvetica', o.bold ? 'bold' : 'normal'); this.doc.setFontSize(size)
    if (o.muted) this.doc.setTextColor(79, 90, 105); else this.doc.setTextColor(20, 27, 36)
    const lines: string[] = this.doc.splitTextToSize(clean(t), this.W)
    for (const l of lines) { this.ensure(size * 1.35); this.doc.text(l, this.x, this.y); this.y += size * 1.35 }
    this.y += 3
  }
  bullets(items: string[]) {
    if (!items.length) { this.p('None recorded', { muted: true }); return }
    this.doc.setFont('helvetica', 'normal'); this.doc.setFontSize(9.5); this.doc.setTextColor(20, 27, 36)
    for (const it of items) {
      const lines: string[] = this.doc.splitTextToSize(clean(it), this.W - 14)
