  const [view, setView] = useState<View>(() => { try { return (prefs.getRaw(TAB_KEY) as View) || 'register' } catch { return 'register' } })
  const pick = (v: View) => { setView(v); try { prefs.setRaw(TAB_KEY, v) } catch { /* storage blocked */ } }
  const latest = s.projects.map((p) => latestEstimate(s, p.id)).filter(Boolean)
  const inReview = s.estimates.filter((e) => e.status === 'in-review')
  const drafts = s.estimates.filter((e) => e.status === 'draft')
  const pts = useMemo(() => accuracyPoints(s), [s])
  const c3 = summarize(pts.filter((x) => x.cls === 3))
  const c5 = summarize(pts.filter((x) => x.cls === 5))
  const reviewValue = sum(inReview.map((e) => estimateTotals(e).total))
  const quickReview = inReview.filter((e) => e.method === 'quick').length
  return (
    <div>
      <PageHeader eyebrow="Controls" title="Estimating" subtitle="Historical quick estimates, Class 5 rate-table and Class 3 line-item estimates, the tables they draw from, and how accurate they turned out to be.">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-4">
          <Stat label="Estimates in review" value={inReview.length} sub={`${money(reviewValue)} awaiting approval`} onClick={() => pick('register')} />
          <Stat label="Drafts in progress" value={drafts.length} sub={`${latest.length} projects estimated`} />
          <Stat label="Class 3 mean accuracy" value={c3.n ? `${c3.mean > 0 ? '+' : ''}${pct(c3.mean, 1)}` : '—'} sub={c3.n ? `${pct(c3.within)} within AACE −20%/+30% · n=${c3.n}` : 'No outcomes yet'} tone={c3.n ? (c3.within >= 0.8 ? 'good' : 'warn') : undefined} onClick={() => pick('accuracy')} />
          <Stat label="Class 5 mean accuracy" value={c5.n ? `${c5.mean > 0 ? '+' : ''}${pct(c5.mean, 1)}` : '—'} sub={c5.n ? `${pct(c5.within)} within AACE −50%/+100% · n=${c5.n}` : 'No outcomes yet'} onClick={() => pick('accuracy')} />
        </div>
      </PageHeader>
      <Tabs<View> className="mb-4" value={view} onChange={pick} tabs={[
        { id: 'register', label: 'Estimate register', count: inReview.length || undefined },
        { id: 'quick', label: 'Quick estimate', count: quickReview || undefined },
        { id: 'historical', label: 'Historical costs', count: s.historicalCosts.filter((h) => h.active).length },
        { id: 'library', label: 'Rate table', count: s.rates.filter((r) => !r.region).length },
        { id: 'accuracy', label: 'Accuracy' },
        { id: 'analogs', label: 'Analog finder' },
        { id: 'admin', label: 'Administration' },
      ]} />
      {view === 'register' && <Register s={s} />}
      {view === 'quick' && <QuickTab />}
      {view === 'historical' && <HistoricalCosts />}
      {view === 'library' && <Library s={s} onAdmin={() => pick('admin')} />}
      {view === 'accuracy' && <Accuracy s={s} pts={pts} />}
      {view === 'analogs' && <Analogs s={s} />}
      {view === 'admin' && <EstimatingAdmin s={s} />}
    </div>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Register
function Register({ s }: { s: AppState }) {
  const [prog, setProg] = useState('')
  const [cls, setCls] = useState('')
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('')
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const rows = s.projects.map((p) => {
    const e = latestEstimate(s, p.id)
    const rec = latestApproved(s, p.id)
    const wf = e?.workflowInstanceId ? s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === e.workflowInstanceId && w.status === 'running') : undefined
    const def = wf ? s.workflowDefs.find((d) => d.id === wf.defId) : undefined
    const step = wf?.active[0]
    return { p, e, t: e ? estimateTotals(e) : undefined, rec, recT: rec ? estimateTotals(rec) : undefined, versions: projectEstimates(s, p.id).length, waiting: step ? { label: def?.nodes.find((n) => n.id === step.nodeId)?.label ?? 'Step', who: step.assigneeIds.map((id) => personName(s, id)).join(', '), due: step.due } : undefined }
  }).filter((r) => (!prog || r.p.programId === prog) && (!cls || String(r.e?.cls) === cls) && (!status || r.e?.status === status) && (!q || (r.p.code + ' ' + r.p.name).toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())))
  const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a, b) => (a.e?.status === 'in-review' ? 0 : 1) - (b.e?.status === 'in-review' ? 0 : 1) || a.p.code.localeCompare(b.p.code))
  const cols: Column<(typeof rows)[number]>[] = [
    { key: 'p', header: 'Project', sort: (r) => r.p.code, render: (r) => <div className="flex min-w-[220px] items-start gap-2"><span className={cx('mt-0.5 h-8 w-1 shrink-0 rounded-full', r.e?.status === 'in-review' ? 'bg-marker' : 'bg-transparent')} /><div className="min-w-0"><div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><ProgramTag programId={r.p.programId} /><a href={projectHref(r.p.id, 'estimates')} onClick={(ev) => ev.stopPropagation()} className="font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold text-link hover:underline">{r.p.code}</a></div><div className="truncate text-[12.5px]">{r.p.name}</div></div></div> },
    { key: 'stage', header: 'Stage', render: (r) => <span className="text-[12px]">{stageName(s, r.p)}{r.p.status !== 'active' && <div><StatusBadge status={r.p.status} /></div>}</span> },
    { key: 'v', header: 'Latest', sort: (r) => r.e?.cls ?? 9, render: (r) => r.e ? <span className="inline-flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5"><span className="font-mono text-[12px]">v{r.e.version}</span><ClassBadge cls={r.e.cls} />{r.e.method === 'quick' ? <QuickBadge /> : <span className={cx('text-[11px]', pricedOnVersion(s, r.e) < s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion ? 'text-warn' : 'text-faint')} title="Rate table version the estimate was priced on">RT v{pricedOnVersion(s, r.e)}</span>}</span> : '—' },
    { key: 'st', header: 'Status', render: (r) => r.e ? <div><StatusBadge status={r.e.status} />{r.waiting && <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-muted">{r.waiting.label} · {r.waiting.who}{r.waiting.due && <span className={cx(r.waiting.due < s.settings.asOf && 'font-semibold text-bad')}> · due {fmtDate(r.waiting.due)}</span>}</div>}</div> : <Badge>None</Badge> },
    { key: 'tot', header: 'Total', num: true, sort: (r) => r.t?.total ?? 0, render: (r) => r.t ? <b>{money(r.t.total)}</b> : '—' },
    { key: 'rng', header: 'Expected range', width: 200, render: (r) => r.t ? <div className="min-w-[160px]"><RangeBar low={r.t.low} point={r.t.total} high={r.t.high} /><div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] tabular-nums text-faint"><span>{money(r.t.low)}</span><span>{money(r.t.high)}</span></div></div> : null },
    { key: 'rec', header: 'Estimate of record', num: true, sort: (r) => r.recT?.total ?? 0, render: (r) => r.rec && r.rec.id !== r.e?.id ? <span className="text-[12px]">{money(r.recT!.total)} <ClassBadge cls={r.rec.cls} /><div className={cx('text-[11px]', r.t!.total > r.recT!.total ? 'text-bad' : 'text-good')}>{r.t!.total >= r.recT!.total ? '+' : ''}{pct(r.t!.total / r.recT!.total - 1, 1)} in latest</div></span> : r.rec ? <span className="text-[11px] text-faint">Latest</span> : <span className="text-faint">—</span> },
    { key: 'est', header: 'Estimator', render: (r) => <span className="text-[12px]">{personName(s, r.e?.createdById ?? r.p.team.estimator)}</span> },
  ]
  const exportCsv = async () => {
    const data = sorted.map((r) => ({ project: r.p.code, name: r.p.name, program: s.programs.find((x) => x.id === r.p.programId)?.code, stage: stageName(s, r.p), version: r.e?.version ?? '', class: r.e?.cls ?? '', status: r.e?.status ?? '', method: r.e?.method ?? '', total: r.t?.total ?? '', low: r.t?.low ?? '', high: r.t?.high ?? '', estimateOfRecord: r.recT?.total ?? '', estimator: personName(s, r.e?.createdById) }))
    const res = await saveFile(`estimate-register-${s.settings.asOf}.csv`, toCSV(data), 'text/csv')
    if (res === 'saved') { mutate(() => {}, { entity: 'estimate', entityId: 'register', action: 'export', summary: `Exported estimate register (${data.length} projects)` }); toast('Register downloaded') } else toast('Download not available', 'info')
  }
  const byClass = groupBy(rows.filter((r) => r.e), (r) => String(r.e!.cls))
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 2xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_340px]">
      <Panel pad={false} title="Portfolio estimate register" subtitle={`Latest estimate for each project · ${rows.length} shown · ${Object.entries(byClass).sort().map(([c, v]) => `${v.length} Class ${c}`).join(', ')}`}
        actions={<Button size="sm" onClick={exportCsv}>Export CSV</Button>}>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-b border-line px-4 py-2.5">
          <Input id="reg-q" className="h-8 w-48" placeholder="Search projects" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} />
          <div className="w-52"><ProgramSelect id="reg-prog" value={prog} onChange={setProg} /></div>
          <Select id="reg-cls" className="h-8 w-36" value={cls} onChange={(e) => setCls(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All classes' }, ...[5, 4, 3, 2, 1].map((c) => ({ value: String(c), label: `Class ${c}` }))]} />
          <Select id="reg-status" className="h-8 w-40" value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All statuses' }, ...['draft', 'in-review', 'approved', 'superseded', 'rejected'].map((x) => ({ value: x, label: x === 'in-review' ? 'In review' : x[0].toUpperCase() + x.slice(1) }))]} />
          {(prog || cls || status || q) && <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => { setProg(''); setCls(''); setStatus(''); setQ('') }}>Clear filters</Button>}
        </div>
        <Table rows={sorted} columns={cols} rowKey={(r) => r.p.id} onRowClick={(r) => { go(projectHref(r.p.id, 'estimates')) }} empty={<div className="p-4"><Empty title="No estimates match">Change or clear the filters.</Empty></div>} />
      </Panel>
      <ClassReference />
    </div>
  )
}

function ClassReference() {
  return (
    <Panel pad={false} title="AACE estimate classes" subtitle="18R-97 indicative ranges used across Crosswalk">
      <table className="tbl">
        <thead><tr><th>Class</th><th>Definition</th><th>Use</th><th className="num">Range</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          {([5, 4, 3, 2, 1] as AaceClass[]).map((c) => (
            <tr key={c}>
              <td><ClassBadge cls={c} /></td>
              <td className="tabular-nums">{CLASS_RANGES[c].definition}</td>
              <td className="text-[12px]">{CLASS_RANGES[c].use}</td>
              <td className="num text-[12px]">{pct(CLASS_RANGES[c].low)}<br />+{pct(CLASS_RANGES[c].high)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <div className="border-t border-line px-4 py-2.5 text-[11.5px] text-muted">The class is computed from the estimate's maturity checklist. Gate 1 needs an approved Class 5 (planning budget); the authorization gate needs Class 3 (control budget).</div>
    </Panel>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Rate table, read view (ES-03). Maintained in Administration.
function Library({ s, onAdmin }: { s: AppState; onAdmin: () => void }) {
  const [asset, setAsset] = useState('')
  const [method, setMethod] = useState('')
  const [cat, setCat] = useState('')
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [normalize, setNormalize] = useState(false)
  const me = useMe()
  const canEdit = me.roles.some((r) => ['estimating-lead', 'estimator', 'admin'].includes(r))
  const year = Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4))
  const base = s.rates.filter((r) => !r.region)
  const assets = Array.from(new Set(base.map((r) => r.assetType)))
  const methods = Array.from(new Set(base.filter((r) => !asset || r.assetType === asset).map((r) => r.workMethod)))
  const rows = base.filter((r) => (!asset || r.assetType === asset) && (!method || r.workMethod === method) && (!cat || r.categoryId === cat) && (!q || r.description.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())))
  const f = (r: UnitCostRate) => (normalize ? 1 + escalationTo(s, r.basisYear, year) / 100 : 1)
  const m = (v: number) => money(v, v < 1e4 ? 'full' : 'compact')
  const usage = useMemo(() => { const u: Record<string, number> = {}; for (const e of s.estimates) for (const l of e.lines) if (l.rateId) u[l.rateId] = (u[l.rateId] || 0) + 1; return u }, [s.estimates])
  const regions = s.settings.estimating.regions
  const cols: Column<UnitCostRate>[] = [
    { key: 'd', header: 'Rate', sort: (r) => r.description, render: (r) => <div className="min-w-[220px]"><div className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold">{r.description}</div><div className="text-[11px] text-faint">{ASSET_LABEL[r.assetType]} · {METHOD_LABEL[r.workMethod]}</div></div> },
    { key: 'c', header: 'Category', sort: (r) => catName(s, r.categoryId), render: (r) => <span className="text-[12px]">{catName(s, r.categoryId)}</span> },
    { key: 'dia', header: 'Dia.', num: true, sort: (r) => r.diameterIn ?? 0, render: (r) => (r.diameterIn ? `${r.diameterIn}"` : '—') },
    { key: 't', header: 'Terrain', render: (r) => <span className="text-[12px] capitalize">{r.terrain ?? 'Any'}</span> },
    { key: 'u', header: 'Unit', render: (r) => r.unit },
    { key: 'p10', header: 'P10', num: true, sort: (r) => r.p10, render: (r) => <span className="text-muted">{m(r.p10 * f(r))}</span> },
    { key: 'p50', header: 'P50 base', num: true, sort: (r) => r.p50, render: (r) => <b>{m(r.p50 * f(r))}</b> },
    { key: 'p90', header: 'P90', num: true, sort: (r) => r.p90, render: (r) => <span className="text-muted">{m(r.p90 * f(r))}</span> },
    { key: 'reg', header: <span title="Effective P50 by region: regional row, else base × factor">By region ({regions.join(' / ')})</span>, render: (r) => { const e = effectiveByRegion(s, r); return <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-[11.5px] tabular-nums text-muted">{regions.map((g, i) => <span key={g} className={cx(e[g].regional && 'font-semibold text-ink')}>{i ? ' / ' : ''}{m(e[g].value * f(r))}</span>)}</span> } },
    { key: 'sp', header: 'Spread', render: (r) => <SpreadBar p10={r.p10} p50={r.p50} p90={r.p90} /> },
    { key: 'n', header: 'n', num: true, sort: (r) => r.sampleSize, render: (r) => <span className={cx(r.sampleSize < 5 && 'font-semibold text-warn')} title={r.sampleSize < 5 ? 'Thin sample: fewer than 5 closed projects' : undefined}>{r.sampleSize}</span> },
    { key: 'y', header: 'Basis', num: true, render: (r) => `${r.basisYear}$` },
    { key: 'use', header: 'Used', num: true, sort: (r) => usage[r.id] ?? 0, render: (r) => <span className="text-muted">{usage[r.id] ?? 0}</span> },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <Panel pad={false} title={<span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">Rate table <Badge tone="ink">v{s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion}</Badge></span>} subtitle={`${rows.length} of ${base.length} items · P10/P50/P90 from closed projects · estimates price at P50 for the project's region`}
        actions={<>
          <Checkbox checked={normalize} onChange={setNormalize} label={`Show in ${year}$`} />
          <Button size="sm" variant={canEdit ? 'primary' : 'secondary'} onClick={onAdmin}>{canEdit ? 'Edit in Administration' : 'Open Administration'}</Button>
        </>}>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-b border-line px-4 py-2.5">
          <Input id="lib-q" className="h-8 w-48" placeholder="Search rates" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} />
          <Select id="lib-asset" className="h-8 w-48" value={asset} onChange={(e) => { setAsset(e.target.value); setMethod('') }} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All asset types' }, ...assets.map((a) => ({ value: a, label: ASSET_LABEL[a] }))]} />
          <Select id="lib-method" className="h-8 w-48" value={method} onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All work methods' }, ...methods.map((a) => ({ value: a, label: METHOD_LABEL[a] }))]} />
          <Select id="lib-cat" className="h-8 w-52" value={cat} onChange={(e) => setCat(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All categories' }, ...directCategories(s).map((c) => ({ value: c.id, label: `${c.code} ${c.name}` }))]} />
        </div>
        <Table rows={rows} columns={cols} rowKey={(r) => r.id} maxHeight={620} dense />
      </Panel>
      <Callout tone="info" title="Normalizing for escalation">
        Rates are stored in their basis-year dollars ({Array.from(new Set(s.rates.map((r) => r.basisYear))).join(', ')}$) and carry no escalation. Estimates escalate the direct-plus-tax-plus-loaders base once, from the basis year to the project's spend midpoint, using the portfolio escalation table ({Object.entries(s.settings.escalationByYear).map(([y, v]) => `${y} ${v}%`).join(' · ')}). Toggle "Show in {year}$" to view rates escalated to today ({escalationTo(s, 2025, year)}% from 2025$). The estimating team maintains the table, its regional rows and every other estimating table under Administration; each publish creates a new rate table version.
      </Callout>
    </div>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Accuracy (ES-13)
function Accuracy({ s, pts: all }: { s: AppState; pts: AccuracyPoint[] }) {
  const [basis, setBasis] = useState<'all' | 'final'>('all')
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState<'data' | 'aace'>('data')
  const [prog, setProg] = useState('')
  const pts = all.filter((x) => (basis === 'all' || x.basis === 'final') && (!prog || x.programId === prog))
  const classes = ([5, 4, 3, 2, 1] as AaceClass[]).filter((c) => pts.some((x) => x.cls === c))
  const accs = pts.map((x) => x.acc)
  const domain: [number, number] = zoom === 'aace' ? [Math.min(-0.55, ...accs), Math.max(1.05, ...accs)] : [Math.min(-0.25, ...accs.map((a) => a - 0.04)), Math.max(0.35, ...accs.map((a) => a + 0.04))]
  const person = (id: string) => personName(s, id)
  const projects = Array.from(new Set(pts.map((x) => x.projectId))).map((id) => s.projects.find((p) => p.id === id)!).sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code))
  const groupTable = (key: (x: AccuracyPoint) => string, label: (k: string) => React.ReactNode, title: string) => {
    const g = groupBy(pts, key)
    return (
      <Panel pad={false} title={title}>
        <table className="tbl">
          <thead><tr><th>{title.replace('By ', '')}</th><th className="num">C5 mean</th><th className="num">C3 mean</th><th className="num">n</th><th className="num">Within range</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {Object.keys(g).sort().map((k) => {
              const a = summarize(g[k].filter((x) => x.cls === 5)), b = summarize(g[k].filter((x) => x.cls === 3)), t = summarize(g[k])
              return <tr key={k}><td>{label(k)}</td><td className="num">{a.n ? <AccPct v={a.mean} cls={5} /> : '—'}</td><td className="num">{b.n ? <AccPct v={b.mean} cls={3} /> : '—'}</td><td className="num">{t.n}</td><td className="num">{pct(t.within)}</td></tr>
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </Panel>
    )
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <SegButtons value={basis} onChange={setBasis} options={[{ id: 'all', label: 'Final cost + current EAC' }, { id: 'final', label: 'Closed projects only' }]} />
        <div className="w-56"><ProgramSelect id="acc-prog" value={prog} onChange={setProg} /></div>
        <span className="text-xs text-muted">Accuracy = outcome ÷ approved estimate − 1. In-flight projects use the current EAC; their accuracy is final at closeout.</span>
      </div>
      {!pts.length ? <Empty title="No outcomes to compare">{basis === 'final' ? 'No closed projects match. Include in-flight projects to compare against current forecasts.' : 'No approved estimates with actual cost yet.'}</Empty> : <>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 md:grid-cols-3">
          {([5, 3] as AaceClass[]).map((c) => {
            const x = summarize(pts.filter((p) => p.cls === c))
            const r = CLASS_RANGES[c]
            return (
              <Panel key={c} title={<span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"><ClassBadge cls={c} /> Class {c} estimates</span>}>
                {x.n ? <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-[13px]">
                  <div><div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">Mean</div><div className="font-display text-[20px] font-bold"><AccPct v={x.mean} cls={c} /></div></div>
                  <div><div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">Mean |error|</div><div className="font-display text-[20px] font-bold tabular-nums">{pct(x.meanAbs, 1)}</div></div>
                  <div><div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">In range</div><div className={cx('font-display text-[20px] font-bold tabular-nums', x.within >= 0.8 ? 'text-good' : 'text-warn')}>{pct(x.within)}</div></div>
                  <div className="col-span-3 text-xs text-muted">n={x.n} · spread {pct(x.min, 1)} to {x.max > 0 ? '+' : ''}{pct(x.max, 1)} · AACE expects {pct(r.low)} to +{pct(r.high)}</div>
                </div> : <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">No Class {c} outcomes yet.</p>}
              </Panel>
            )
          })}
          <Panel title="Reading this">
            <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">A negative mean says estimates ran high (projects came in under). If Class 3 estimates cluster tighter than Class 5 around zero, maturing the estimate is paying for itself. Points outside the shaded AACE band need a lessons-learned entry.</p>
          </Panel>
        </div>
        <Panel title="Accuracy by class against the AACE expected range" subtitle={`${pts.length} approved estimates on ${projects.length} projects`} actions={<SegButtons value={zoom} onChange={setZoom} options={[{ id: 'data', label: 'Zoom to data' }, { id: 'aace', label: 'Full AACE range' }]} />}>
          <AccuracyStrip domain={domain} rows={classes.map((c) => {
            const x = summarize(pts.filter((p) => p.cls === c))
            return { label: `Class ${c}`, sub: `n=${x.n}`, low: CLASS_RANGES[c].low, high: CLASS_RANGES[c].high, mean: x.mean, points: pts.filter((p) => p.cls === c).map((p) => ({ v: p.acc, title: `${p.code} v${p.version} Class ${p.cls}: estimate ${money(p.estimate)} → ${p.basis === 'final' ? 'final' : 'EAC'} ${money(p.outcome)} (${p.acc > 0 ? '+' : ''}${(p.acc * 100).toFixed(1)}%)` })) }
          })} />
          <Legend items={[{ swatch: <Swatch className="border border-info/50 bg-info/20" />, label: 'AACE expected range' }, { swatch: <span className="inline-block h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-link" />, label: 'Within range' }, { swatch: <span className="inline-block h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-bad" />, label: 'Outside range' }, { swatch: <span className="inline-block h-3 w-[3px] bg-ink" />, label: 'Mean' }]} />
        </Panel>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
          {groupTable((x) => x.programId, (k) => <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"><ProgramTag programId={k} /><span className="text-[12px]">{s.programs.find((p) => p.id === k)?.name}</span></span>, 'By program')}
          {groupTable((x) => x.estimatorId, (k) => person(k), 'By estimator')}
        </div>
        <Panel pad={false} title="Estimate vs outcome by project" subtitle="Each bar is an approved estimate with its AACE band; the diamond is the outcome">
          <div className="scroll-x">
            <table className="tbl">
              <thead><tr><th>Project</th><th>Outcome</th><th style={{ minWidth: 260 }}>Class 5 · <span className="normal-case">◆ outcome</span></th><th className="num">C5</th><th style={{ minWidth: 260 }}>Class 3 · <span className="normal-case">◆ outcome</span></th><th className="num">C3</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {projects.map((p) => {
                  const ps = pts.filter((x) => x.projectId === p.id)
                  const a = ps.filter((x) => x.cls === 5).pop(), b = ps.filter((x) => x.cls === 3).pop()
                  const max = Math.max(...ps.map((x) => Math.max(x.high, x.outcome))) * 1.04
                  return (
                    <tr key={p.id} className="clickable" onClick={() => { go(projectHref(p.id, 'estimates')) }}>
                      <td><div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><ProgramTag programId={p.programId} /><span className="font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold">{p.code}</span></div><div className="max-w-[220px] truncate text-[12px] text-muted">{p.name}</div></td>
                      <td className="text-[12px]"><b className="tabular-nums">{money(ps[0].outcome)}</b><div className="text-faint">{ps[0].basis === 'final' ? 'Final cost' : 'Current EAC'}</div></td>
                      <td>{a ? <OutcomeBar low={a.low} point={a.estimate} high={a.high} outcome={a.outcome} scaleMax={max} /> : <span className="text-faint">—</span>}</td>
                      <td className="num">{a ? <AccPct v={a.acc} cls={5} /> : '—'}</td>
                      <td>{b ? <OutcomeBar low={b.low} point={b.estimate} high={b.high} outcome={b.outcome} scaleMax={max} /> : <span className="text-[12px] text-faint">Not yet approved</span>}</td>
                      <td className="num">{b ? <AccPct v={b.acc} cls={3} /> : '—'}</td>
                    </tr>
                  )
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          <div className="border-t border-line px-4 py-2"><Legend items={[{ swatch: <Swatch className="border border-info/50 bg-info/20" />, label: 'AACE range' }, { swatch: <span className="inline-block h-3 w-[3px] bg-ink" />, label: 'Approved estimate' }, { swatch: <span className="inline-block h-2.5 w-2.5 rotate-45 bg-marker" />, label: 'Outcome (final or EAC)' }]} /></div>
        </Panel>
      </>}
    </div>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Analog finder (ES-14)
interface AiRank { ranked: { code: string; why: string }[]; summary: string; caution?: string }

function Analogs({ s }: { s: AppState }) {
  const ai = useAiAvailable()
  const me = useMe()
  const [pid, setPid] = useState(() => s.projects.find((p) => p.code === 'PRJ-1003')?.id ?? s.projects[0].id)
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [res, setRes] = useState<{ pid: string; r: AiRank } | null>(null)
  const [err, setErr] = useState('')
  const target = s.projects.find((p) => p.id === pid)!
  const list = useMemo(() => analogs(s, target), [s, target])
  const top = list.slice(0, 8)
  const tu = unitBasis(target)
  const tc = bestCost(s, target)
  const tPer = tc ? tc.value / tu.qty : null
  const comparable = list.filter((a) => a.perUnit != null && a.score >= 60).slice(0, 5)
  const per = comparable.map((a) => a.perUnit!).sort((a, b) => a - b)
  const median = per.length ? per[Math.floor(per.length / 2)] : null
  const draft = s.estimates.find((e) => e.projectId === pid && e.status === 'draft')
  const facts = (p: Project) => { const c = bestCost(s, p); const u = unitBasis(p); return { code: p.code, name: p.name, program: s.programs.find((x) => x.id === p.programId)?.code, assetType: p.asset.type, workMethod: p.workMethod, diameterIn: p.asset.diameterIn || null, lengthFt: p.asset.lengthFt || null, units: `${u.qty} ${u.unit}`, terrain: p.location.terrain, district: p.location.district, classLocation: p.location.classLocation, hca: p.location.hca, status: p.status, stage: stageName(s, p), cost: c ? Math.round(c.value) : null, costBasis: c?.label ?? null, costPerUnit: c ? Math.round(c.value / u.qty) : null, scope: p.scope.summary } }
  const run = async () => {
    setBusy(true); setErr('')
    const cands = list.slice(0, 14).map((a) => ({ ...facts(a.project), deterministicScore: a.score }))
    const prompt = `You are a utility capital cost estimator choosing analog projects to sanity-check an estimate. Data is a fictional demo for "Arroyo Valley Gas".
TARGET PROJECT:
${JSON.stringify(facts(target))}
CANDIDATE PROJECTS (cost = final cost if closed, current forecast EAC if under way, else the latest estimate; costPerUnit is cost divided by units):
${JSON.stringify(cands)}
Rank the 5 candidates most useful as cost analogs for the target. Prefer the same asset type and work method, then similar diameter, terrain, length/scale and class location, and prefer outcomes (final or EAC) over estimates. Explain each pick in one short sentence citing the specific similarity or difference and what it means for $/unit.
Return ONLY JSON: {"ranked":[{"code":"PRJ-0000","why":"..."}] (exactly 5, best first, codes from the candidates), "summary":"2-3 sentences on what the analogs say about the target's cost per unit", "caution":"one sentence on the biggest limitation of these analogs"}`
    try {
      const r = await askJSON<AiRank>(prompt)
      if (!r || !Array.isArray(r.ranked)) throw new Error('format')
      setRes({ pid, r: { ranked: r.ranked.filter((x) => s.projects.some((p) => p.code === x.code)).slice(0, 5), summary: String(r.summary ?? ''), caution: r.caution ? String(r.caution) : undefined } })
    } catch (e) { setErr(e instanceof Error && e.message === 'format' ? 'The AI reply had an unexpected format. Try again.' : aiMessage(e)) } finally { setBusy(false) }
  }
  const addToBoe = () => {
    if (!draft) return
    const text = `Analog check (${fmtDate(s.settings.asOf)}): ${comparable.map((a) => `${a.project.code} ${fmtPerUnit(a.perUnit, a.unit.unit)} (${a.cost?.label.toLowerCase()})`).join('; ')}${median != null ? `; median ${fmtPerUnit(median, tu.unit)}` : ''}${tPer != null ? ` vs this project ${fmtPerUnit(tPer, tu.unit)}` : ''}.`
    mutate((d) => { const e = d.estimates.find((x) => x.id === draft.id); if (e) e.basis.assumptions.push(text) }, { entity: 'estimate', entityId: draft.id, projectId: pid, action: 'update', summary: `Added analog cost check to draft v${draft.version} BOE assumptions (${comparable.length} analogs)` })
    toast(`Added to draft v${draft.version} basis of estimate`)
  }
  const aiRes = res?.pid === pid ? res.r : null
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <Panel>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
          <Field label="Project to find analogs for" className="w-full max-w-md"><ProjectSelect id="an-proj" value={pid} onChange={(v) => { setPid(v); setErr('') }} /></Field>
          {ai !== false ? <Button variant="marker" onClick={run} disabled={busy} icon={<span aria-hidden>✦</span>}>{busy ? 'Thinking…' : 'AI rank analogs'}</Button> : null}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-4">
          <Fact label="Asset · method" value={`${ASSET_LABEL[target.asset.type]} · ${METHOD_LABEL[target.workMethod]}`} />
          <Fact label="Size" value={target.asset.type === 'valve-site' ? `${tu.qty} site${tu.qty > 1 ? 's' : ''}, ${target.asset.diameterIn}"` : target.asset.lengthFt ? `${target.asset.lengthFt.toLocaleString('en-US')} ft, ${target.asset.diameterIn}"` : target.scope.quantities.map((q) => `${q.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${q.unit}`).join(', ')} />
          <Fact label="Terrain · district" value={<span className="capitalize">{target.location.terrain} · {target.location.district}</span>} />
          <Fact label={tc?.label ?? 'Cost'} value={tc ? <span>{money(tc.value)} <span className="text-muted">({fmtPerUnit(tPer, tu.unit)})</span></span> : '—'} />
        </div>
      </Panel>
      {err && <Callout tone="bad">{err}</Callout>}
      {aiRes && (
        <Panel title={<span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">AI ranking <AiTag /></span>} subtitle="Suggested analogs. Check the cost basis before relying on them." actions={<Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setRes(null)}>Dismiss</Button>}>
          <ol className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {aiRes.ranked.map((x, i) => {
              const a = list.find((y) => y.project.code === x.code)
              return (
                <li key={x.code} className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[13px]">
                  <span className="w-5 font-display font-bold text-muted">{i + 1}</span>
                  <a className="font-mono font-semibold text-link hover:underline" href={a ? projectHref(a.project.id, 'estimates') : '#estimating'}>{x.code}</a>
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">{x.why}</span>
                  {a && <span className="tabular-nums text-muted">{fmtPerUnit(a.perUnit, a.unit.unit)} · {a.cost?.label}</span>}
                </li>
              )
            })}
          </ol>
          {aiRes.summary && <p className="mt-3 text-[13px]">{aiRes.summary}</p>}
          {aiRes.caution && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-warn">{aiRes.caution}</p>}
        </Panel>
      )}
      <Panel pad={false} title="Most similar projects" subtitle="Deterministic similarity: asset type, work method, diameter, terrain, scale and district"
        actions={draft && comparable.length > 0 && <Button size="sm" onClick={addToBoe}>Add analog check to draft v{draft.version} BOE</Button>}>
        <div className="scroll-x">
          <table className="tbl">
            <thead><tr><th>Project</th><th className="num">Similarity</th><th>Why</th><th>Size</th><th className="num">Cost</th><th>Basis</th><th className="num">Cost {perUnitLabel(tu.unit)}</th><th className="num">vs target</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
              {top.map((a) => (
                <tr key={a.project.id} className="clickable" onClick={() => { go(projectHref(a.project.id, 'estimates')) }}>
                  <td><div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><ProgramTag programId={a.project.programId} /><span className="font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold">{a.project.code}</span></div><div className="max-w-[240px] truncate text-[12px] text-muted">{a.project.name}</div></td>
                  <td className="num"><div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2"><span className="h-1.5 w-14 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-sunken"><span className="block h-full bg-link" style={{ width: `${a.score}%` }} /></span><b>{a.score}</b></div></td>
                  <td className="text-[11.5px] text-muted">{a.reasons.slice(0, 4).join(' · ') || 'Different asset and method'}</td>
                  <td className="text-[12px]">{a.unit.unit === 'ft' ? `${a.unit.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')} ft` : `${a.unit.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${a.unit.unit}`}{a.project.asset.diameterIn ? `, ${a.project.asset.diameterIn}"` : ''}</td>
                  <td className="num">{money(a.cost?.value)}</td>
                  <td>{a.cost ? <Badge tone={a.cost.basis === 'final' ? 'good' : a.cost.basis === 'eac' ? 'info' : 'neutral'}>{a.cost.label}</Badge> : '—'}</td>
                  <td className="num font-semibold">{fmtPerUnit(a.perUnit, a.unit.unit)}</td>
                  <td className="num">{a.perUnit != null && tPer ? <span className={cx(a.perUnit > tPer ? 'text-bad' : 'text-good')}>{a.perUnit >= tPer ? '+' : ''}{pct(a.perUnit / tPer - 1)}</span> : '—'}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-1 border-t border-line px-4 py-2.5 text-[12.5px]">
          {median != null ? <>
            <span>Analog median (similarity ≥ 60, n={per.length}): <b className="tabular-nums">{fmtPerUnit(median, tu.unit)}</b></span>
            <span>Range <b className="tabular-nums">{fmtPerUnit(per[0], tu.unit)}</b> – <b className="tabular-nums">{fmtPerUnit(per[per.length - 1], tu.unit)}</b></span>
            {tPer != null && <span>This project: <b className="tabular-nums">{fmtPerUnit(tPer, tu.unit)}</b> <span className={cx(tPer > median * 1.25 ? 'text-bad' : tPer < median * 0.75 ? 'text-warn' : 'text-good')}>({tPer >= median ? '+' : ''}{pct(tPer / median - 1)} vs median{Math.abs(tPer / median - 1) > 0.25 ? ' — explain in the BOE' : ''})</span></span>}
          </> : <span className="text-muted">No close analogs with a comparable unit basis. Rely on the unit-cost library and document the gap.</span>}
          {!draft && comparable.length > 0 && <span className="text-xs text-faint">Open a draft estimate on this project to add the analog check to its BOE.</span>}
        </div>
      </Panel>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted">Signed in as {me.name}. Similarity is computed in the page; AI ranking (when enabled) sees only the project facts listed above.</p>
    </div>
  )
}

function Fact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="min-w-0"><div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">{label}</div><div className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] font-semibold">{value}</div></div>
}

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/lib.ts =====
// Estimating module helpers (pure functions over AppState). UI lives next to this file.
import type { AaceClass, AppState, Estimate, EstimateLine, LineSource, MonteCarloResult, Project, Risk } from '@/core/types'
import { CLASS_RANGES, CONT_CAT, ESC_CAT, LOADER_CAT, MATURITY_ITEMS, benchmark, computeClass, estimateTotals, lineTotal, maturityItems, missingFor, parametricLines, type EstimateTotals, type MaturityItem } from '@/core/engine/estimates'
import { basisYearOf, contingencyForClass, currentLoaders, escalationPctFor, pricedOnVersion, regionOf, salesTaxFor, scopeQuantities, spendMidpoint } from '@/core/engine/estimatingAdmin'
import { position } from '@/core/engine/position'
import { isActive, pert } from '@/core/engine/risk'
import { templateFor } from '@/core/engine/gates'
import { actOnWorkflow, startWorkflow } from '@/core/engine/workflow'
import { fmtDate, groupBy, money, pct, sum, uid, nowISO } from '@/core/util'

// ───────────────────────────── Lookups
export const directCategories = (s: Pick<AppState, 'categories'>) => s.categories.filter((c) => c.group === 'direct')

export const projectEstimates = (s: Pick<AppState, 'estimates'>, pid: string) =>
  s.estimates.filter((e) => e.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.version - b.version)

export const latestEstimate = (s: Pick<AppState, 'estimates'>, pid: string): Estimate | undefined => projectEstimates(s, pid).pop()

export const latestApproved = (s: Pick<AppState, 'estimates'>, pid: string): Estimate | undefined =>
  projectEstimates(s, pid).filter((e) => e.status === 'approved').pop()

/** Estimates that were approved at some point (approved or later superseded). */
export const everApproved = (s: Pick<AppState, 'estimates'>, pid: string) => projectEstimates(s, pid).filter((e) => !!e.approvedAt && (e.status === 'approved' || e.status === 'superseded'))

export const isEditable = (e: Estimate) => e.status === 'draft'

export const nextVersion = (s: Pick<AppState, 'estimates'>, pid: string) => Math.max(0, ...projectEstimates(s, pid).map((e) => e.version)) + 1

/** Short labels for chart axes (Waterfall truncates at 14 chars). */
const SHORT: Record<string, string> = {
  'cat-pmc': 'Project mgmt', 'cat-eng': 'Engineering', 'cat-pipe': 'Pipe/fittings', 'cat-valves': 'Valves', 'cat-equipment': 'Equipment',
  'cat-test': 'Consumables', 'cat-fabrication': 'Fabrication', 'cat-contract': 'Construction', 'cat-traffic': 'Traffic',
  'cat-restore': 'Restoration', 'cat-inspect': 'Inspection', 'cat-commission': 'Commissioning', 'cat-co-labor': 'Operations',
  'cat-support': 'Proj. support', 'cat-env': 'Environmental', 'cat-land': 'Land & ROW', 'cat-land-purchase': 'Land purchase',
  'cat-permit-fees': 'Permit fees', 'cat-undiff': 'Undifferent.', [LOADER_CAT]: 'Overhead', [ESC_CAT]: 'Escalation', [CONT_CAT]: 'Contingency',
}
export const shortCat = (s: Pick<AppState, 'categories'>, id: string) => SHORT[id] ?? s.categories.find((c) => c.id === id)?.name ?? id
export const catName = (s: Pick<AppState, 'categories'>, id: string) => s.categories.find((c) => c.id === id)?.name ?? id
export const catCode = (s: Pick<AppState, 'categories'>, id: string) => s.categories.find((c) => c.id === id)?.code ?? ''

export const SOURCE_LABEL: Record<LineSource, string> = { library: 'Rate table', quote: 'Vendor quote', analog: 'Analog project', judgment: 'Estimator judgment' }

/** Plain-language method names (internal ids stay 'parametric' / 'line-item' / 'quick'). */
export const METHOD_LABEL: Record<Estimate['method'], string> = { parametric: 'Rate-table estimate', 'line-item': 'Line-item estimate', quick: 'Historical quick estimate' }
export const methodLabel = (m: Estimate['method']) => METHOD_LABEL[m] ?? m
/** Estimate title in plain language (older records say "parametric"). */
export const displayTitle = (e: Pick<Estimate, 'title'>) => e.title.replace(/\bparametric\b/i, 'rate-table')

// ───────────────────────────── Escalation & contingency guidance
/** Compound escalation from the rate basis year to a target year using the settings table. */
export function escalationTo(s: Pick<AppState, 'settings'>, fromYear: number, toYear: number): number {
  return escalationPctFor(s, fromYear, toYear)
}

/** Year of the spend midpoint: halfway between today and the in-service forecast. */
export const midpointYear = spendMidpoint

/** Product default deterministic contingency by class (the live table is settings.estimating.contingencyByClass). */
export const CONTINGENCY_GUIDE: Record<AaceClass, number> = { 5: 30, 4: 20, 3: 15, 2: 10, 1: 5 }

/** Risk-based contingency: percent of base needed to reach the chosen confidence. */
export function riskBasedPct(mc: Pick<MonteCarloResult, 'costP' | 'baseCost'>, conf: 'P50' | 'P80'): number {
  if (!mc.baseCost) return 0
  return Math.max(0, Math.round(((mc.costP[conf] - mc.baseCost) / mc.baseCost) * 1000) / 10)
}

// ───────────────────────────── Building estimates
/**
 * Inputs to the Class 5 rate-table estimate. They are read from the project's scope statement
 * (quantity types say which input each quantity feeds), the asset record (diameter) and its region.
 */
export interface ParametricInputs { lengthFt: number; diameterIn: number; region: string; sites: number; hp?: number; tieIns?: number; services?: number; terrain: Project['location']['terrain'] }

type CfgState = Pick<AppState, 'settings'>
export function defaultInputs(p: Project, s?: CfgState): ParametricInputs {
  if (s?.settings?.estimating) {
    const q = scopeQuantities(s, p)
    return { lengthFt: q.lengthFt, diameterIn: p.asset.diameterIn, region: regionOf(p), sites: q.sites, hp: q.hp, tieIns: q.tieIns, services: q.services, terrain: p.location.terrain }
  }
  return { lengthFt: p.asset.lengthFt, diameterIn: p.asset.diameterIn, region: regionOf(p), terrain: p.location.terrain, sites: p.scope.quantities.find((q) => q.unit === 'ea' && /site/i.test(q.label))?.qty ?? p.scope.quantities.find((q) => q.unit === 'ea')?.qty ?? 1 }
}

/** Where each input came from (scope statement quantity label, asset record or project location). */
export function inputSources(s: CfgState, p: Project): Record<string, string> {
  const q = scopeQuantities(s, p)
  return { lengthFt: q.sources.lengthFt ?? 'Asset record', sites: q.sources.sites ?? 'Asset record', hp: q.sources.hp ?? '', tieIns: q.sources.tieIns ?? '', services: q.sources.services ?? '', diameterIn: 'Asset record', region: 'Project location' }
}

/** A copy of the project with the estimate's inputs applied (the project record is not changed). */
export function projectForInputs(p: Project, i: ParametricInputs): Project {
  const q = p.scope.quantities.map((x) => ({ ...x }))
  if (p.asset.type === 'valve-site') {
    const k = q.findIndex((x) => x.unit === 'ea' || x.unit === 'site')
    if (k >= 0) q[k].qty = i.sites
    else q.push({ label: 'Valve sites automated', qty: i.sites, unit: 'ea' })
  }
  return { ...p, asset: { ...p.asset, lengthFt: i.lengthFt, diameterIn: i.diameterIn }, location: { ...p.location, terrain: i.terrain ?? p.location.terrain, district: i.region ?? p.location.district }, scope: { ...p.scope, quantities: q } }
}

export function describeInputs(p: Project, i: ParametricInputs): string {
  const reg = i.region ? `${i.region} region` : `${i.terrain} terrain`
  if (p.asset.type === 'valve-site') return `${i.sites} valve site${i.sites === 1 ? '' : 's'}, ${i.diameterIn}" line, ${reg}`
  if (p.asset.type === 'compressor-station' || p.asset.type === 'storage-field') return `One station (lump-sum rate-table rows)${i.hp ? `, ${i.hp.toLocaleString('en-US')} hp` : ''}, ${reg}`
  return `${i.lengthFt.toLocaleString('en-US')} ft of ${i.diameterIn}" pipe${i.tieIns ? `, ${i.tieIns} tie-ins` : ''}, ${reg}`
}

export function blankMaturity(items: MaturityItem[] = MATURITY_ITEMS): Record<string, boolean> {
  return Object.fromEntries(items.map((m) => [m.key, m.forClass === 5]))
}

/** Build a new Class 5 rate-table draft (ES-01) from the scope statement. Not yet pushed into state. */
export function buildParametric(s: AppState, p: Project, inputs: ParametricInputs, byId: string): Estimate {
  const proj = projectForInputs(p, inputs)
  const region = inputs.region ?? regionOf(p)
  const lines = parametricLines(proj, s.rates, { region, regionFactors: s.settings.estimating.regionFactors }, { lengthFt: inputs.lengthFt, sites: inputs.sites, hp: inputs.hp, tieIns: inputs.tieIns, services: inputs.services })
  const items = maturityItems(s)
  const maturity = blankMaturity(items)
  const cls = computeClass(maturity, items)
  const mid = midpointYear(s, p)
  const by = basisYearOf(s, lines)
  const esc = escalationPctFor(s, by, mid)
  const cont = contingencyForClass(s, cls)
  const tax = salesTaxFor(s, p)
  const rf = s.settings.estimating.regionFactors[region] ?? 1
  return {
    id: uid('est'), projectId: p.id, version: nextVersion(s, p.id), title: 'Class 5 rate-table estimate', cls, status: 'draft', method: 'parametric',
    createdAt: nowISO(), createdById: byId, lines, maturity,
    loaders: currentLoaders(s),
    escalationPct: esc,
    contingency: { method: 'deterministic', pct: cont },
    rateTableVersion: s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion,
    region,
    salesTax: tax,
    basis: {
      scopeBasis: `${p.scope.summary} Rate-table basis: ${describeInputs(p, inputs)}.`,
      assumptions: [
        'Quantities read from the project scope statement',
        `Unit costs at the rate-table median (P50), rate table v${s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion}, for the ${region} region (region factor ×${rf.toFixed(2)} where no regional rate is kept)`,
        `Escalated from ${by}$ to the ${mid} spend midpoint using the portfolio escalation table`,
        `Sales tax ${tax.pct}% on material (${tax.counties.map((c) => `${c.county} ${Math.round(c.share * 100)}%`).join(', ')})`,
      ],
      exclusions: ['Customer-side work', 'Unknown contamination beyond Phase I findings', 'Escalation beyond the planned in-service year'],
      contingencyRationale: `Deterministic ${cont}% for Class ${cls} per the estimating standard.`,
      classJustification: classJustification(maturity, items),
    },
  }
}

/** Default work packages for a Class 3 split, by asset type. */
export function defaultPackages(p: Project): string[] {
  if (p.asset.type === 'compressor-station') return ['WP1 Equipment', 'WP2 EPC construction', 'WP3 Electrical']
  if (p.asset.type === 'valve-site') return ['WP1 Sites']
  return ['WP1 Segment A', 'WP2 Segment B']
}

/** Split parametric lines into work packages: linear quantities spread evenly, lump sums and counts go to the first package. */
export function splitIntoPackages(lines: EstimateLine[], wps: string[]): EstimateLine[] {
  const pk = wps.length ? wps : ['WP1']
  const out: EstimateLine[] = []
  for (const l of lines) {
    if ((l.unit === 'ft' || l.unit === 'mi') && pk.length > 1) {
      let left = l.qty
      pk.forEach((wp, i) => {
        const q = i === pk.length - 1 ? Math.round(left * 100) / 100 : Math.round((l.qty / pk.length) * 100) / 100
        left -= q
        out.push({ ...l, id: uid('ln'), workPackage: wp, qty: q })
      })
    } else out.push({ ...l, id: uid('ln'), workPackage: pk[0] })
  }
  return out
}

/** New draft version copied from an existing estimate (ES-02). */
export function buildFromExisting(s: AppState, src: Estimate, byId: string, opts: { split?: string[]; toClass3?: boolean } = {}): Estimate {
  const lines = opts.split ? splitIntoPackages(src.lines, opts.split) : src.lines.map((l) => ({ ...l, id: uid('ln') }))
  const maturity = { ...src.maturity }
  const cls = computeClass(maturity, maturityItems(s))
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === src.projectId)
  // Loaders and escalation are portfolio tables, locked per project: a new version carries the ones in force.
  // Unit costs are copied as they were, so the version keeps the rate table they were priced on.
  const fromQuick = src.method === 'quick'
  const tables = p && !fromQuick ? { escalationPct: escalationPctFor(s, basisYearOf(s, lines), spendMidpoint(s, p)), salesTax: salesTaxFor(s, p), region: regionOf(p) } : {}
  return {
    ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(src)) as Estimate,
    id: uid('est'), version: nextVersion(s, src.projectId), status: 'draft', createdAt: nowISO(), createdById: byId,
    approvedAt: undefined, approvedById: undefined, workflowInstanceId: undefined, repricedFrom: undefined,
    title: opts.toClass3 ? 'Class 3 line-item estimate' : fromQuick ? 'Class 5 estimate (from quick estimate)' : src.title,
    method: opts.toClass3 || fromQuick ? 'line-item' : src.method,
    quick: undefined, // a new version is worked as line items; the quick estimate record stays on its own version
    lines, maturity, cls,
    loaders: currentLoaders(s).length ? currentLoaders(s) : src.loaders,
    rateTableVersion: fromQuick ? undefined : pricedOnVersion(s, src),
    ...tables,
  }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Basis of estimate
export function classJustification(m: Record<string, boolean>, items: MaturityItem[] = MATURITY_ITEMS): string {
  const cls = computeClass(m, items)
  const done = items.filter((i) => m[i.key]).map((i) => i.label.toLowerCase())
  const nextMissing = cls > 1 ? missingFor(m, (cls - 1) as AaceClass, items) : []
  const r = CLASS_RANGES[cls]
  return `Class ${cls} (AACE 18R-97; project definition ${r.definition}). Complete: ${done.length ? done.join('; ') : 'none'}.` +
    (nextMissing.length ? ` Not yet complete for Class ${cls - 1}: ${nextMissing.map((i) => i.label.toLowerCase()).join('; ')}.` : '') +
    ` Expected accuracy ${pct(r.low)} to +${pct(r.high)}.`
}

export function contingencyRationale(e: Pick<Estimate, 'contingency' | 'cls'>, mc?: Pick<MonteCarloResult, 'costP' | 'iterations'>, riskCount?: number, guide?: number): string {
  if (e.contingency.method === 'deterministic') {
    const g = guide ?? CONTINGENCY_GUIDE[e.cls]
    return `Deterministic ${e.contingency.pct}% of base` + (e.contingency.pct === g ? ` per the estimating standard for Class ${e.cls}.` : ` (standard for Class ${e.cls} is ${g}%; estimator adjustment documented in assumptions).`)
  }
  return `Risk-based: ${e.contingency.pct}% of base sets the budget at the ${e.contingency.confidence ?? 'P80'} of a Monte Carlo simulation` +
    (mc ? ` (${mc.iterations.toLocaleString('en-US')} iterations; P50 ${money(mc.costP.P50)}, P80 ${money(mc.costP.P80)})` : '') +
    (riskCount != null ? ` over ${riskCount} active quantified risks on the project register.` : ' over the active quantified risk register.')
}

export function sourceSummary(e: Pick<Estimate, 'lines'>): { source: LineSource; n: number; amount: number }[] {
  const g = groupBy(e.lines, (l) => l.source)
  return (['library', 'quote', 'analog', 'judgment'] as LineSource[]).map((src) => ({ source: src, n: g[src]?.length ?? 0, amount: sum((g[src] ?? []).map(lineTotal)) }))
}

export const workPackages = (e: Pick<Estimate, 'lines'>) => Array.from(new Set(e.lines.map((l) => l.workPackage)))

/** Lines more than 25% from the rate-table median for the estimate's region. */
export function benchmarkExceptions(e: Pick<Estimate, 'lines'> & { region?: string }, rates: AppState['rates'], regionFactors?: Record<string, number>) {
  const pr = e.region && regionFactors ? { region: e.region, regionFactors } : undefined
  return e.lines.map((l) => ({ line: l, b: benchmark(l, rates, pr) })).filter((x) => x.b?.flag) as { line: EstimateLine; b: { pct: number; flag: boolean; ref: number } }[]
}

export function activeRisks(s: Pick<AppState, 'risks'>, pid: string): Risk[] {
  return s.risks.filter((r) => r.projectId === pid && isActive(r))
}

// ───────────────────────────── Outcomes & accuracy (ES-13)
export interface Outcome { value: number; basis: 'final' | 'eac' }

/** What a project actually costs: final actuals once closed, else the current EAC once spending has started. */
export function outcomeFor(s: AppState, p: Project): Outcome | null {
  const pos = position(s, p.id)
  if (p.status === 'closed' && pos.actualToDate > 0) return { value: pos.actualToDate, basis: 'final' }
  if (pos.actualToDate > 0 && pos.eac > 0) return { value: pos.eac, basis: 'eac' }
  return null
}

export interface AccuracyPoint {
  projectId: string; code: string; programId: string; estimatorId: string; estimateId: string; version: number
  cls: AaceClass; estimate: number; low: number; high: number; outcome: number; basis: Outcome['basis']; acc: number; within: boolean; approvedAt?: string
}

export function accuracyForProject(s: AppState, p: Project): AccuracyPoint[] {
  const o = outcomeFor(s, p)
  if (!o) return []
  return everApproved(s, p.id).map((e) => {
    const t = estimateTotals(e)
    const acc = o.value / t.total - 1
    const r = CLASS_RANGES[e.cls]
    return { projectId: p.id, code: p.code, programId: p.programId, estimatorId: e.createdById, estimateId: e.id, version: e.version, cls: e.cls, estimate: t.total, low: t.low, high: t.high, outcome: o.value, basis: o.basis, acc, within: acc >= r.low && acc <= r.high, approvedAt: e.approvedAt }
  })
}

export function accuracyPoints(s: AppState, opts: { includeInFlight: boolean } = { includeInFlight: true }): AccuracyPoint[] {
  return s.projects.flatMap((p) => accuracyForProject(s, p)).filter((x) => opts.includeInFlight || x.basis === 'final')
}

export function summarize(points: AccuracyPoint[]) {
  if (!points.length) return { n: 0, mean: 0, meanAbs: 0, min: 0, max: 0, within: 0 }
  const accs = points.map((p) => p.acc)
  return { n: points.length, mean: sum(accs) / accs.length, meanAbs: sum(accs.map(Math.abs)) / accs.length, min: Math.min(...accs), max: Math.max(...accs), within: points.filter((p) => p.within).length / points.length }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Analogs (ES-14)
export interface UnitBasis { unit: 'ft' | 'site' | 'hp' | 'station'; qty: number }
export function unitBasis(p: Project): UnitBasis {
  if (p.asset.type === 'valve-site') return { unit: 'site', qty: p.scope.quantities.find((q) => q.unit === 'ea')?.qty ?? 1 }
  if (p.asset.type === 'compressor-station' || p.asset.type === 'storage-field') {
    const hp = p.scope.quantities.find((q) => q.unit === 'hp')?.qty
    return hp ? { unit: 'hp', qty: hp } : { unit: 'station', qty: 1 }
  }
  return { unit: 'ft', qty: Math.max(1, p.asset.lengthFt) }
}

export interface CostBasis { value: number; basis: 'final' | 'eac' | 'estimate'; label: string; cls?: AaceClass }
export function bestCost(s: AppState, p: Project): CostBasis | null {
  const o = outcomeFor(s, p)
  if (o) return { value: o.value, basis: o.basis, label: o.basis === 'final' ? 'Final cost' : 'Current EAC' }
  const e = latestApproved(s, p.id) ?? latestEstimate(s, p.id)
  if (!e) return null
  return { value: estimateTotals(e).total, basis: 'estimate', label: `Class ${e.cls} estimate`, cls: e.cls }
}

export interface Analog { project: Project; score: number; reasons: string[]; cost: CostBasis | null; perUnit: number | null; unit: UnitBasis }

export function analogs(s: AppState, target: Project): Analog[] {
  const tu = unitBasis(target)
  return s.projects.filter((p) => p.id !== target.id).map((p) => {
    const reasons: string[] = []
    let score = 0
    if (p.asset.type === target.asset.type) { score += 35; reasons.push('Same asset type') }
    if (p.workMethod === target.workMethod) { score += 30; reasons.push('Same work method') }
    const d1 = target.asset.diameterIn, d2 = p.asset.diameterIn
    if (d1 && d2) { const f = 1 - Math.abs(d1 - d2) / Math.max(d1, d2); score += 15 * f; if (d1 === d2) reasons.push(`Same ${d1}" diameter`); else reasons.push(`${d2}" vs ${d1}"`) }
    else if (!d1 && !d2) score += 15
    if (p.location.terrain === target.location.terrain) { score += 10; reasons.push(`${p.location.terrain[0].toUpperCase() + p.location.terrain.slice(1)} terrain`) }
    const u = unitBasis(p)
    if (u.unit === tu.unit) { const f = Math.min(u.qty, tu.qty) / Math.max(u.qty, tu.qty); score += 10 * f }
    if (p.location.district === target.location.district) { score += 3; reasons.push(`${p.location.district} district`) }
    const cost = bestCost(s, p)
    const perUnit = cost && u.unit === tu.unit ? cost.value / u.qty : null
    return { project: p, score: Math.round(Math.min(100, score)), reasons, cost, perUnit, unit: u }
  }).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || (b.cost?.basis === 'final' ? 1 : 0) - (a.cost?.basis === 'final' ? 1 : 0))
}

export function perUnitLabel(u: UnitBasis['unit']) {
  return u === 'ft' ? '/ft' : u === 'site' ? '/site' : u === 'hp' ? '/hp' : '/station'
}
export function fmtPerUnit(v: number | null, u: UnitBasis['unit']) {
  if (v == null) return '—'
  return (v >= 1e4 ? money(v) : '$' + Math.round(v).toLocaleString('en-US')) + perUnitLabel(u)
}

// ───────────────────────────── BOE document (ES-08)
const esc = (t: unknown) => String(t ?? '').replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' })[c]!)

export function boeHtml(s: AppState, e: Estimate, opts: { mc?: MonteCarloResult } = {}): { title: string; body: string } {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === e.projectId)!
  const t = estimateTotals(e)
  const person = (id?: string) => s.people.find((x) => x.id === id)?.name ?? '—'
  const prog = s.programs.find((x) => x.id === p.programId)
  const r = CLASS_RANGES[e.cls]
  const wf = s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === e.workflowInstanceId)
  const def = wf ? s.workflowDefs.find((d) => d.id === wf.defId) : undefined
  const cats = Object.entries(t.byCategory).sort((a, b) => catCode(s, a[0]).localeCompare(catCode(s, b[0])))
  const srcs = sourceSummary(e)
  const ex = benchmarkExceptions(e, s.rates, s.settings.estimating.regionFactors)
  const title = `${p.code} Basis of Estimate — v${e.version} Class ${e.cls}`
  const wps = workPackages(e)
  const body = `
<h1>Basis of Estimate</h1>
<div class="meta">${esc(s.settings.orgName)} · ${esc(p.code)} ${esc(p.name)} · ${esc(prog?.code)} ${esc(prog?.name)}</div>
<div class="meta">Estimate v${e.version} · ${esc(displayTitle(e))} · <span class="tag">AACE Class ${e.cls}</span> · Status: ${esc(e.status)} · Method: ${esc(methodLabel(e.method))}${e.method !== 'quick' ? ` · Rate table v${pricedOnVersion(s, e)}${e.region ? ` · ${esc(e.region)} region` : ''}` : ''}</div>
<div class="meta">Prepared by ${esc(person(e.createdById))} on ${fmtDate(e.createdAt.slice(0, 10))}${e.approvedAt ? ` · Approved by ${esc(person(e.approvedById))} on ${fmtDate(e.approvedAt.slice(0, 10))}` : ''} · Generated ${fmtDate(s.settings.asOf)}</div>

<h2>1. Estimate summary</h2>
<table><tr><th>Point estimate</th><th>Expected range (AACE Class ${e.cls})</th><th>Base (excl. contingency)</th><th>Contingency</th></tr>
<tr><td class="num"><b>${money(t.total, 'full')}</b></td><td class="num">${money(t.low, 'full')} to ${money(t.high, 'full')} (${pct(r.low)} / +${pct(r.high)})</td><td class="num">${money(t.base, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${money(t.contingency, 'full')} (${e.contingency.pct}%)</td></tr></table>

<h2>2. Scope basis</h2>
<p>${esc(e.basis.scopeBasis)}</p>
${p.scope.quantities.length ? `<table><tr><th>Quantity</th><th class="num">Amount</th><th>Unit</th></tr>${p.scope.quantities.map((q) => `<tr><td>${esc(q.label)}</td><td class="num">${q.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')}</td><td>${esc(q.unit)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>` : ''}
<p class="meta">Asset: ${esc(p.asset.lineId)} · ${p.asset.diameterIn ? p.asset.diameterIn + '" ' : ''}${esc(p.asset.material)} · ${p.asset.lengthFt ? p.asset.lengthFt.toLocaleString('en-US') + ' ft · ' : ''}MAOP ${p.asset.maopPsig} psig · ${esc(p.location.terrain)} · Class location ${p.location.classLocation}${p.location.hca ? ' · HCA' : ''}</p>

<h2>3. Cost by category</h2>
<table><tr><th>Code</th><th>Category</th><th>ERP cost element</th><th class="num">Amount</th><th class="num">% of base</th></tr>
${cats.map(([c, v]) => `<tr><td>${esc(catCode(s, c))}</td><td>${esc(catName(s, c))}</td><td>${esc(s.categories.find((x) => x.id === c)?.erpCostElement)}</td><td class="num">${money(v, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${t.base ? pct(v / t.base, 1) : '—'}</td></tr>`).join('')}
<tr><td></td><td><b>Base estimate</b></td><td></td><td class="num"><b>${money(t.base, 'full')}</b></td><td class="num">100%</td></tr>
<tr><td>990</td><td>Contingency (${e.contingency.method}${e.contingency.confidence && e.contingency.method === 'risk-based' ? ', ' + e.contingency.confidence : ''})</td><td>${esc(s.categories.find((x) => x.id === CONT_CAT)?.erpCostElement)}</td><td class="num">${money(t.contingency, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${e.contingency.pct}%</td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td><b>Total estimate</b></td><td></td><td class="num"><b>${money(t.total, 'full')}</b></td><td></td></tr></table>

<h2>4. Markups</h2>
<table><tr><th>Markup</th><th class="num">Rate</th><th class="num">Amount</th></tr>
${e.loaders.map((l) => `<tr><td>${esc(l.name)} (on direct cost)</td><td class="num">${l.pct}%</td><td class="num">${money(Math.round(t.lines * l.pct / 100), 'full')}</td></tr>`).join('')}
${e.salesTax && t.tax ? `<tr><td>Sales tax on material (${e.salesTax.counties.map((c) => `${esc(c.county)} ${Math.round(c.share * 100)}% at ${c.rate}%`).join('; ')})</td><td class="num">${e.salesTax.pct}%</td><td class="num">${money(t.tax, 'full')}</td></tr>` : ''}
<tr><td>Escalation (on direct + tax + loaders)</td><td class="num">${e.escalationPct}%</td><td class="num">${money(t.escalation, 'full')}</td></tr></table>

<h2>5. Line items by work package</h2>
${wps.map((wp) => {
    const ls = e.lines.filter((l) => l.workPackage === wp)
    return `<h3>${esc(wp)} — ${money(sum(ls.map(lineTotal)), 'full')}</h3><table><tr><th>Category</th><th>Description</th><th class="num">Qty</th><th>Unit</th><th class="num">Unit cost</th><th class="num">Total</th><th>Source</th><th>Confidence</th></tr>${ls.map((l) => `<tr><td>${esc(catCode(s, l.categoryId))} ${esc(shortCat(s, l.categoryId))}</td><td>${esc(l.description)}</td><td class="num">${l.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')}</td><td>${esc(l.unit)}</td><td class="num">${money(l.unitCost, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${money(lineTotal(l), 'full')}</td><td>${esc(l.source)}</td><td>${esc(l.confidence)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>`
  }).join('')}

<h2>6. Sources of pricing</h2>
<table><tr><th>Source</th><th class="num">Lines</th><th class="num">Amount</th><th class="num">% of direct</th></tr>
${srcs.map((x) => `<tr><td>${SOURCE_LABEL[x.source]}</td><td class="num">${x.n}</td><td class="num">${money(x.amount, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${t.lines ? pct(x.amount / t.lines, 1) : '—'}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>
${ex.length ? `<p><b>Benchmark exceptions</b> (unit cost more than 25% from the rate-table median for the region):</p><table><tr><th>Line</th><th class="num">Unit cost</th><th class="num">Rate-table P50</th><th class="num">Difference</th></tr>${ex.map((x) => `<tr><td>${esc(x.line.workPackage)} · ${esc(x.line.description)}${x.line.override ? ` (override: ${esc(x.line.override.reason)})` : ''}</td><td class="num">${money(x.line.unitCost, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${money(x.b.ref, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${x.b.pct > 0 ? '+' : ''}${pct(x.b.pct, 0)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>` : '<p class="meta">No line is more than 25% from its rate-table median.</p>'}

<h2>7. Assumptions</h2>
<ul>${e.basis.assumptions.map((a) => `<li>${esc(a)}</li>`).join('') || '<li>None recorded</li>'}</ul>
<h2>8. Exclusions</h2>
<ul>${e.basis.exclusions.map((a) => `<li>${esc(a)}</li>`).join('') || '<li>None recorded</li>'}</ul>

<h2>9. Contingency rationale</h2>
<p>${esc(e.basis.contingencyRationale)}</p>
${opts.mc && e.contingency.method === 'risk-based' ? `<table><tr><th>P10</th><th>P50</th><th>P80</th><th>P90</th><th>Iterations</th></tr><tr><td class="num">${money(opts.mc.costP.P10, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${money(opts.mc.costP.P50, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${money(opts.mc.costP.P80, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${money(opts.mc.costP.P90, 'full')}</td><td class="num">${opts.mc.iterations}</td></tr></table>` : ''}

<h2>10. Class justification</h2>
<p>${esc(e.basis.classJustification)}</p>
<table><tr><th>Maturity item</th><th>Needed for</th><th>Status</th></tr>
${maturityItems(s).map((m) => `<tr><td>${esc(m.label)}</td><td>Class ${m.forClass}</td><td>${e.maturity[m.key] ? 'Complete' : 'Not complete'}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>

<h2>11. Review and approval</h2>
${wf ? `<p class="meta">${esc(def?.name)} v${wf.defVersion} · ${esc(wf.status)}</p><table><tr><th>When</th><th>Who</th><th>Action</th><th>Step</th><th>Comment</th></tr>${wf.history.filter((h) => h.action !== 'system').map((h) => `<tr><td>${esc(h.at.slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' '))}</td><td>${esc(h.actorId === 'system' ? 'System' : person(h.actorId))}</td><td>${esc(h.action)}${h.signature ? ' (e-signed)' : ''}</td><td>${esc(def?.nodes.find((n) => n.id === h.nodeId)?.label ?? '')}</td><td>${esc(h.comment ?? '')}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>` : '<p class="meta">Not yet submitted for approval.</p>'}
`
  return { title, body }
}

/** Compact facts for AI prompts. */
export function estimateFacts(s: AppState, e: Estimate) {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === e.projectId)!
  const t: EstimateTotals = estimateTotals(e)
  return {
    project: { code: p.code, name: p.name, program: s.programs.find((x) => x.id === p.programId)?.code, assetType: p.asset.type, workMethod: p.workMethod, diameterIn: p.asset.diameterIn, lengthFt: p.asset.lengthFt, terrain: p.location.terrain, classLocation: p.location.classLocation, hca: p.location.hca, scope: p.scope.summary, inScope: p.scope.inScope, outOfScope: p.scope.outOfScope, quantities: p.scope.quantities },
    estimate: { version: e.version, class: e.cls, method: e.method, total: t.total, base: t.base, low: t.low, high: t.high, loaders: e.loaders, escalationPct: e.escalationPct, contingency: e.contingency },
    byCategory: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(t.byCategory).map(([k, v]) => [catName(s, k), v])),
    workPackages: workPackages(e).map((wp) => ({ name: wp, total: sum(e.lines.filter((l) => l.workPackage === wp).map(lineTotal)) })),
    lines: e.lines.slice().sort((a, b) => lineTotal(b) - lineTotal(a)).slice(0, 20).map((l) => ({ wp: l.workPackage, desc: l.description, qty: l.qty, unit: l.unit, unitCost: l.unitCost, source: l.source, confidence: l.confidence })),
    sources: sourceSummary(e),
    maturityComplete: maturityItems(s).filter((m) => e.maturity[m.key]).map((m) => m.label),
    maturityMissing: maturityItems(s).filter((m) => !e.maturity[m.key]).map((m) => `${m.label} (Class ${m.forClass})`),
    topRisks: activeRisks(s, p.id).sort((a, b) => b.probability * pert(b.cost) - a.probability * pert(a.cost)).slice(0, 6).map((r) => ({ title: r.title, probability: r.probability, mostLikely: r.cost.ml })),
  }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Actions (mutate drafts)
/**
 * Start the estimate-approval workflow for a saved draft (ES-09). If the current user holds the
 * first "prepare" step (or is an admin) it is submitted on their behalf. Returns a toast message.
 */
export function submitEstimate(d: AppState, estimateId: string, mc?: MonteCarloResult): string {
  const e = d.estimates.find((x) => x.id === estimateId)
  if (!e || e.status !== 'draft') return 'Only drafts can be submitted'
  const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === e.projectId)!
  e.cls = computeClass(e.maturity, maturityItems(d))
  const total = estimateTotals(e).total
  if (e.contingency.method === 'risk-based' && mc) d.monteCarlo.push({ ...mc, id: uid('mc') })
  const inst = startWorkflow(d, 'estimate-approval', { title: `${p.code} Class ${e.cls} estimate v${e.version}`, recordType: 'estimate', recordId: e.id, projectId: p.id, data: { amount: total, cls: String(e.cls) } })
  if (!inst) return 'No published estimate-approval workflow'
  const slot = inst.active[0]
  const actor = d.people.find((x) => x.id === d.currentUserId)
  const name = (id: string) => d.people.find((x) => x.id === id)?.name ?? id
  if (slot && (slot.assigneeIds.includes(d.currentUserId) || actor?.roles.includes('admin'))) {
    actOnWorkflow(d, inst.id, slot.nodeId, d.currentUserId, 'submit', 'Estimate and basis of estimate prepared')
    const next = inst.active[0]
    return next ? `Submitted for approval — now with ${next.assigneeIds.map(name).join(', ')}` : 'Submitted and approved'
  }
  return slot ? `Sent to ${slot.assigneeIds.map(name).join(', ')} to complete and submit` : 'Submitted for approval'
}

/** Which budget kind an approved estimate can become now: the latest passed gate with a budget event. */
export function budgetKindAvailable(s: AppState, p: Project): { kind: 'planning' | 'control'; gate: string } | null {
  const tpl = templateFor(s, p)
  const passed = tpl.stages.slice(0, p.stageIndex).filter((st) => st.gate.budgetEvent)
  const last = passed[passed.length - 1]
  return last ? { kind: last.gate.budgetEvent!, gate: last.gate.name } : null
}

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/parts.tsx =====
// Small presentational pieces for the estimating module. Token colors only.
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { AaceClass } from '@/core/types'
import { CLASS_RANGES } from '@/core/engine/estimates'
import { cx, money, pct } from '@/core/util'

/** Numeric input that lets people type freely and commits a number on every valid change. */
export function NumInput({ value, onChange, className, step, min, id, ariaLabel, disabled }: { value: number; onChange: (n: number) => void; className?: string; step?: number; min?: number; id?: string; ariaLabel?: string; disabled?: boolean }) {
  const [txt, setTxt] = useState(fmt(value))
  const focused = useRef(false)
  useEffect(() => { if (!focused.current) setTxt(fmt(value)) }, [value])
  return (
    <input id={id} aria-label={ariaLabel} disabled={disabled} inputMode="decimal" className={cx('inp text-right tabular-nums', className)} value={txt} step={step} min={min}
      onFocus={() => { focused.current = true; setTxt(String(value)) }}
      onBlur={() => { focused.current = false; setTxt(fmt(value)) }}
      onChange={(e) => {
        setTxt(e.target.value)
        const n = Number(e.target.value.replace(/[$,\s%]/g, ''))
        if (e.target.value.trim() !== '' && Number.isFinite(n)) onChange(min != null ? Math.max(min, n) : n)
      }} />
  )
}
const fmt = (n: number) => (Number.isFinite(n) ? n.toLocaleString('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }) : '')

export const cellInp = 'h-7 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px]'

/** P10–P90 spread around the median on a common relative axis (0.5×–1.6× of P50). */
export function SpreadBar({ p10, p50, p90 }: { p10: number; p50: number; p90: number }) {
  const lo = 0.5, hi = 1.6
  const x = (v: number) => `${((Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v / (p50 || 1))) - lo) / (hi - lo)) * 100}%`
  return (
    <div className="relative h-4 w-28" title={`P10 ${money(p10, 'full')} · P50 ${money(p50, 'full')} · P90 ${money(p90, 'full')}`}>
      <div className="absolute top-[7px] h-0.5 w-full bg-line" />
      <div className="absolute top-[5px] h-1.5 rounded-sm bg-info/45" style={{ left: x(p10), width: `calc(${x(p90)} - ${x(p10)})` }} />
      <div className="absolute top-0.5 h-3 w-0.5 bg-ink" style={{ left: x(p50) }} />
    </div>
  )
}

/** One horizontal accuracy bar: AACE band around the estimate, with the outcome marker. */
export function OutcomeBar({ low, point, high, outcome, scaleMax }: { low: number; point: number; high: number; outcome?: number; scaleMax: number }) {
  const p = (v: number) => `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (v / scaleMax) * 100))}%`
  return (
    <div className="relative h-6 w-full min-w-[140px]">
      <div className="absolute top-[11px] h-0.5 w-full bg-line" />
      <div className="absolute top-[7px] h-2.5 rounded-sm border border-info/50 bg-info/20" style={{ left: p(low), width: `calc(${p(high)} - ${p(low)})` }} />
      <div className="absolute top-1 h-4 w-[3px] rounded-sm bg-ink" style={{ left: p(point) }} title={`Estimate ${money(point)}`} />
      {outcome != null && <div className="absolute top-[5px] h-3 w-3 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-45 border-2 border-surface bg-marker" style={{ left: p(outcome) }} title={`Outcome ${money(outcome)}`} />}
    </div>
  )
}

export function AccPct({ v, cls }: { v: number; cls: AaceClass }) {
  const r = CLASS_RANGES[cls]
  const ok = v >= r.low && v <= r.high
  return <span className={cx('tabular-nums font-semibold', ok ? 'text-good' : 'text-bad')}>{v > 0 ? '+' : ''}{pct(v, 1)}</span>
}

export interface StripRow { label: string; sub?: string; low: number; high: number; points: { v: number; title: string }[]; mean?: number }

/** Strip plot of accuracy (outcome ÷ estimate − 1) by class against the AACE expected band. */
export function AccuracyStrip({ rows, domain }: { rows: StripRow[]; domain: [number, number] }) {
  const W = 720, L = 110, R = 18, RH = 58, T = 8, B = 26
  const H = T + rows.length * RH + B
  const [d0, d1] = domain
  const X = (v: number) => L + ((Math.max(d0, Math.min(d1, v)) - d0) / (d1 - d0)) * (W - L - R)
  const span = d1 - d0
  const step = span > 1.2 ? 0.25 : span > 0.6 ? 0.1 : 0.05
  const ticks: number[] = []
  for (let v = Math.ceil(d0 / step) * step; v <= d1 + 1e-9; v += step) ticks.push(Math.round(v * 100) / 100)
  return (
    <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H}`} className="w-full" role="img" aria-label="Estimate accuracy by class">
      {ticks.map((t) => (
        <g key={t}>
          <line x1={X(t)} x2={X(t)} y1={T} y2={H - B} stroke="rgb(var(--line))" strokeWidth={t === 0 ? 0 : 1} />
          <text x={X(t)} y={H - 8} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={11} fill="rgb(var(--muted))">{t > 0 ? '+' : ''}{Math.round(t * 100)}%</text>
        </g>
      ))}
      <line x1={X(0)} x2={X(0)} y1={T} y2={H - B} stroke="rgb(var(--ink))" strokeWidth={1.2} strokeDasharray="3 3" />
      {rows.map((r, i) => {
        const y0 = T + i * RH, cy = y0 + RH / 2
        return (
          <g key={r.label}>
            <text x={8} y={cy - 2} fontSize={12.5} fontWeight={700} fill="rgb(var(--ink))">{r.label}</text>
            {r.sub && <text x={8} y={cy + 13} fontSize={11} fill="rgb(var(--muted))">{r.sub}</text>}
            <rect x={X(r.low)} y={cy - 14} width={Math.max(1, X(r.high) - X(r.low))} height={28} rx={3} fill="rgb(var(--info))" fillOpacity={0.13} stroke="rgb(var(--info))" strokeOpacity={0.5} />
            <text x={X(r.low) + 4} y={cy - 17} fontSize={10} fill="rgb(var(--info))">AACE {r.low > 0 ? '+' : ''}{Math.round(r.low * 100)}% to +{Math.round(r.high * 100)}%</text>
            {r.points.map((p, k) => (
              <circle key={k} cx={X(p.v)} cy={cy + ((k * 7) % 21) - 10} r={4.5} fill={p.v >= r.low && p.v <= r.high ? 'rgb(var(--link))' : 'rgb(var(--bad))'} fillOpacity={0.8} stroke="rgb(var(--surface))" strokeWidth={1}>
                <title>{p.title}</title>
              </circle>
            ))}
            {r.mean != null && (
              <g>
                <line x1={X(r.mean)} x2={X(r.mean)} y1={cy - 16} y2={cy + 16} stroke="rgb(var(--ink))" strokeWidth={3} />
                <text x={X(r.mean)} y={cy + 27} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={10.5} fontWeight={700} fill="rgb(var(--ink))">mean {r.mean > 0 ? '+' : ''}{(r.mean * 100).toFixed(1)}%</text>
              </g>
            )}
          </g>
        )
      })}
    </svg>
  )
}

export function Legend({ items }: { items: { swatch: ReactNode; label: string }[] }) {
  return <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs text-muted">{items.map((i) => <span key={i.label} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">{i.swatch}{i.label}</span>)}</div>
}
export const Swatch = ({ className }: { className: string }) => <span className={cx('inline-block h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-sm', className)} />

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/quick/factorParts.tsx =====
// Class 5 factor model — presentational pieces shared by the quick estimate screens and the model admin:
// driver inputs, the itemized driver table, the build-up, the rate-table comparison, the BOE text and Monte Carlo.
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import type { BasisOfEstimate, DriverKey, FactorInputs, FactorItem, PermitComplexity, Remoteness, SiteDrivers, SoilClass, TrafficLevel, WorkHours } from '@/core/types'
import { DRIVER_INFO, monteCarloFactor, type FactorResult } from '@/core/engine/factorModel'
import { cx, money, moneyDelta, pct } from '@/core/util'
import { Badge, Button, Checkbox, Field, Select } from '@/ui/kit'
import { Histogram, LineChart } from '@/ui/charts'
import { NumInput } from '../parts'

export const KIND_TONE: Record<string, 'info' | 'warn' | 'neutral' | 'ink' | 'marker'> = { physical: 'info', legal: 'warn', logistical: 'neutral', size: 'ink', region: 'ink', escalation: 'ink', adjustment: 'marker' }
export const KIND_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { physical: 'Physical', legal: 'Legal', logistical: 'Logistical', size: 'Size', region: 'Region', escalation: 'Escalation', adjustment: 'Estimator' }

/** Small "verify" tag for generated wording a human must check (AGENT_RULES 28). */
export function VerifyTag({ children = 'Draft wording — verify' }: { children?: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <Badge tone="marker" title="Generated from the pricing. An estimator must check this text before it is relied on.">{children}</Badge>
}

const opts = (k: DriverKey) => DRIVER_INFO[k].levels.map((l) => ({ value: l.value, label: l.label }))

/** The site drivers the factor model reads. Highlights values that differ from the project record. */
export function DriverInputs({ value, onChange, fromProject, idPrefix = 'drv', disabled }: { value: FactorInputs; onChange: (v: FactorInputs) => void; fromProject?: FactorInputs; idPrefix?: string; disabled?: boolean }) {
  const site = value.site
  const set = (patch: Partial<SiteDrivers>) => onChange({ ...value, site: { ...site, ...patch } })
  const diff = (k: keyof SiteDrivers) => !!fromProject && fromProject.site[k] !== site[k]
  const lab = (k: keyof SiteDrivers, text: string) => <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">{text}{diff(k) && <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-marker" title="Differs from the project record" />}</span>
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
      <Field label={lab('soil', 'Soil / rock')}><Select disabled={disabled} id={`${idPrefix}-soil`} value={site.soil} onChange={(e) => set({ soil: e.target.value as SoilClass })} options={opts('soil')} /></Field>
      <Field label={lab('crossings', 'Road, rail & water crossings')}><NumInput disabled={disabled} id={`${idPrefix}-cross`} value={site.crossings} min={0} onChange={(v) => set({ crossings: Math.max(0, Math.round(v)) })} /></Field>
      <Field label={lab('permitComplexity', 'Permit complexity')}><Select disabled={disabled} id={`${idPrefix}-permit`} value={site.permitComplexity} onChange={(e) => set({ permitComplexity: e.target.value as PermitComplexity })} options={opts('permitComplexity')} /></Field>
      <Field label={lab('classLocation', 'Class location')}><Select disabled={disabled} id={`${idPrefix}-class`} value={String(site.classLocation)} onChange={(e) => set({ classLocation: Number(e.target.value) as SiteDrivers['classLocation'] })} options={opts('classLocation')} /></Field>
      <Field label={lab('traffic', 'Traffic')}><Select disabled={disabled} id={`${idPrefix}-traffic`} value={site.traffic} onChange={(e) => set({ traffic: e.target.value as TrafficLevel })} options={opts('traffic')} /></Field>
