      <Field label={lab('workHours', 'Work-hour restrictions')}><Select disabled={disabled} id={`${idPrefix}-hours`} value={site.workHours} onChange={(e) => set({ workHours: e.target.value as WorkHours })} options={opts('workHours')} /></Field>
      <Field label={lab('remoteness', 'Remoteness / access')}><Select disabled={disabled} id={`${idPrefix}-remote`} value={site.remoteness} onChange={(e) => set({ remoteness: e.target.value as Remoteness })} options={opts('remoteness')} /></Field>
      <Field label={lab('hca', 'High consequence area')}><div className="flex h-[34px] items-center"><Checkbox disabled={disabled} id={`${idPrefix}-hca`} checked={site.hca} onChange={(v) => set({ hca: v })} label="Pipe is in an HCA" /></div></Field>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Itemized drivers: level, effective multiplier, weight and the $ each one adds to direct cost and to the total. */
export function DriverTable({ items, baseline, direct, total }: { items: FactorItem[]; baseline: number; direct: number; total: number }) {
  const k = direct ? total / direct : 1 // markups (tax, loaders, contingency) are proportional to direct cost
  const [all, setAll] = useState(false)
  const shown = all ? items : items.filter((x) => Math.abs(x.multiplier - 1) > 1e-9 || x.kind === 'region' || x.kind === 'escalation')
  const hidden = items.length - shown.length
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0">
      <div className="scroll-x">
        <table className="tbl [&_td]:py-1.5">
          <thead><tr><th>Driver</th><th>Level</th><th className="num">×</th><th className="num">Direct $</th><th className="num">All-in $</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr><td className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold">Base rate × quantity</td><td className="text-[12px] text-muted">Before drivers</td><td className="num text-[12px]">—</td><td className="num text-[12.5px] font-semibold">{money(baseline)}</td><td className="num text-[12.5px] text-muted">{money(baseline * k)}</td></tr>
            {shown.map((x) => (
              <tr key={x.id}>
                <td className="min-w-[150px] text-[12.5px]"><div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">{x.label}<Badge tone={KIND_TONE[x.kind]}>{KIND_LABEL[x.kind]}</Badge></div>{x.weight != null && x.weight < 1 && <div className="text-[11px] text-faint">weight {x.weight.toFixed(2)}</div>}</td>
                <td className="min-w-[110px] text-[12px] text-muted">{x.level}</td>
                <td className="num text-[12px]">{x.multiplier.toFixed(3)}</td>
                <td className={cx('num text-[12.5px]', x.effect > 0 ? 'text-bad' : x.effect < 0 ? 'text-good' : 'text-faint')}>{x.effect ? moneyDelta(x.effect) : '—'}</td>
                <td className={cx('num text-[12.5px]', x.effect > 0 ? 'text-bad' : x.effect < 0 ? 'text-good' : 'text-faint')}>{x.effect ? moneyDelta(x.effect * k) : '—'}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
            <tr className="border-t border-line"><td className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold" colSpan={3}>= Direct cost</td><td className="num text-[12.5px] font-semibold">{money(direct)}</td><td className="num text-[12.5px] font-semibold">{money(total)}</td></tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      {hidden > 0 && <button className="mt-1 text-[12px] font-semibold text-link" onClick={() => setAll(true)}>Show {hidden} driver{hidden > 1 ? 's' : ''} at baseline (no effect)</button>}
      {all && <button className="mt-1 text-[12px] font-semibold text-link" onClick={() => setAll(false)}>Hide drivers at baseline</button>}
      <p className="mt-1 text-[11.5px] text-muted">Each driver's share of the change from the base is attributed by the log of its multiplier, so the shares add up exactly and do not depend on the order the factors are applied in. All-in includes sales tax, loaders and contingency.</p>
    </div>
  )
}

export interface FactorBuild { direct: number; tax: number; taxPct: number; loaders: number; loaderPct: number; contingency: number; contingencyPct: number; total: number }

/** direct → + sales tax → + loaders → + contingency = total. */
export function FactorBuildUp({ b }: { b: FactorBuild }) {
  const row = (label: React.ReactNode, value: React.ReactNode, strong?: boolean) => <tr className={cx(strong && 'border-t border-line')}><td className={cx('text-[12.5px]', strong && 'font-semibold')}>{label}</td><td className={cx('num text-[12.5px]', strong && 'font-semibold')}>{value}</td></tr>
  return (
    <table className="tbl [&_td]:py-1">
      <tbody>
        {row('Direct cost (after drivers)', money(b.direct))}
        {row(<>+ Sales tax <span className="text-faint">({b.taxPct}% on taxable material)</span></>, money(b.tax))}
        {row(<>+ Loaders <span className="text-faint">({b.loaderPct.toFixed(1)}%)</span></>, money(b.loaders))}
        {row(<>+ Contingency <span className="text-faint">({b.contingencyPct}% Class 5)</span></>, money(b.contingency))}
        <tr className="border-t-2 border-ink/60"><td className="font-display text-[13.5px] font-bold">= Total</td><td className="num font-display text-[13.5px] font-bold">{money(b.total)}</td></tr>
      </tbody>
    </table>
  )
}

export const buildOf = (r: FactorResult): FactorBuild => ({ direct: r.direct, tax: r.tax, taxPct: r.salesTax.pct, loaders: r.loaders, loaderPct: r.loaderPct, contingency: r.contingency, contingencyPct: r.contingencyPct, total: r.total })

/** Factor model vs the Class 5 rate-table (parametric) estimate for the same project. */
export function RateTableCompare({ factor, rateTable, low, high }: { factor: number; rateTable?: number; low: number; high: number }) {
  if (!rateTable) return <div className="rounded-md border border-line px-3 py-2 text-[12.5px] text-muted"><span className="eyebrow mr-2">Rate-table check</span>No rate-table rows for this asset and method, or no project yet.</div>
  const d = factor / rateTable - 1
  const inside = rateTable >= low && rateTable <= high
  return (
    <div className={cx('rounded-md border px-3 py-2.5', inside ? 'border-line' : 'border-warn/50 bg-warn/5')}>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><span className="eyebrow">Rate-table check</span><Badge tone={inside ? 'good' : 'warn'}>{inside ? 'Inside the model range' : 'Outside the model range'}</Badge></div>
      <div className="mt-1.5 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-[12px]">
        <div><div className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">Factor model</div><div className="font-semibold tabular-nums">{money(factor)}</div></div>
        <div><div className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">Class 5 rate table</div><div className="font-semibold tabular-nums">{money(rateTable)}</div></div>
        <div><div className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">Difference</div><div className={cx('font-semibold tabular-nums', Math.abs(d) > 0.25 ? 'text-warn' : 'text-ink')}>{d >= 0 ? '+' : ''}{pct(d)}</div></div>
      </div>
      <p className="mt-1 text-[11.5px] text-muted">{inside ? 'The parametric estimate priced from the rate table agrees within the model\'s P10–P90.' : 'The two methods disagree by more than the model range: check the drivers, the scope quantities, or whether the rate table reflects the site conditions.'}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Generated basis-of-estimate text, flagged for human verification. */
export function BoeText({ basis, title = 'Basis of estimate' }: { basis: BasisOfEstimate; title?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-md border border-marker/60 bg-marker/5 px-3 py-2.5 text-[12.5px]">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><span className="eyebrow">{title}</span><VerifyTag /></div>
      <p className="mt-1.5"><b>Scope basis.</b> {basis.scopeBasis}</p>
      <div className="mt-1"><b>Assumptions.</b><ul className="ml-4 list-disc">{basis.assumptions.map((a, k) => <li key={k}>{a}</li>)}</ul></div>
      <p className="mt-1"><b>Exclusions.</b> {basis.exclusions.join('; ')}.</p>
      <p className="mt-1"><b>Contingency.</b> {basis.contingencyRationale}</p>
      <p className="mt-1"><b>Class.</b> {basis.classJustification}</p>
      <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11.5px] text-muted">Generated from the pricing above. An estimator checks and edits this wording before the estimate is used.</p>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Monte Carlo of a priced estimate: histogram with percentiles, S-curve, tornado of each uncertainty's contribution. */
export function MonteCarloPanel({ r, iterations = 5000, seed = 11 }: { r: Pick<FactorResult, 'total' | 'parts'>; iterations?: number; seed?: number }) {
  const mc = useMemo(() => monteCarloFactor(r, iterations, seed), [r, iterations, seed])
  const lo = Math.min(...mc.tornado.map((t) => t.low)), hi = Math.max(...mc.tornado.map((t) => t.high))
  const x = (v: number) => `${((v - lo) / (hi - lo || 1)) * 100}%`
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-5">
        {(['P10', 'P50', 'P80', 'P90'] as const).map((k) => <div key={k} className="rounded border border-line px-2.5 py-1.5"><div className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">{k}</div><div className="font-display text-[16px] font-bold tabular-nums">{money(mc.p[k])}</div></div>)}
        <div className="rounded border border-line px-2.5 py-1.5"><div className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">Mean</div><div className="font-display text-[16px] font-bold tabular-nums">{money(mc.mean)}</div></div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="eyebrow mb-1">Distribution of total cost · {mc.iterations.toLocaleString('en-US')} draws</div>
        <Histogram bins={mc.histogram} markers={[{ x: mc.p.P10, label: 'P10' }, { x: mc.p.P50, label: 'P50' }, { x: mc.p.P80, label: 'P80', color: 'rgb(var(--warn))' }, { x: mc.p.P90, label: 'P90' }]} />
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="eyebrow mb-1">S-curve · cost at each confidence level</div>
        <LineChart labels={mc.scurve.map((p) => `${p.pct}%`)} series={[{ name: 'Total cost', values: mc.scurve.map((p) => p.value), area: true }]} height={200} yMin={mc.scurve[0].value} />
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="eyebrow mb-1">Tornado · P10–P90 swing from each source of uncertainty alone</div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          {mc.tornado.map((t) => (
            <div key={t.id} className="grid grid-cols-1 items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-0.5 text-[12px] sm:grid-cols-[minmax(0,200px)_1fr_auto]">
              <span className="truncate" title={t.label}>{t.label}</span>
              <span className="relative h-3.5 rounded-sm bg-sunken">
                <span className="absolute top-0 h-3.5 rounded-l-sm bg-good/60" style={{ left: x(t.low), width: `calc(${x(r.total)} - ${x(t.low)})` }} />
                <span className="absolute top-0 h-3.5 rounded-r-sm bg-bad/60" style={{ left: x(r.total), width: `calc(${x(t.high)} - ${x(r.total)})` }} />
                <span className="absolute -top-0.5 h-[18px] w-0.5 bg-ink" style={{ left: x(r.total) }} />
              </span>
              <span className="tabular-nums text-muted">{money(t.low)}–{money(t.high)} · <b className="text-ink">{pct(t.share)}</b> of variance</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <p className="text-[11.5px] text-muted">Seeded draws (seed {mc.seed}) of the model's base-rate uncertainty and the uncertainty of each driver off its baseline, lognormal and independent, centred on the estimate. Same inputs, same result.</p>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Button that opens the Monte Carlo panel inline. */
export function MonteCarloToggle({ r, iterations }: { r: Pick<FactorResult, 'total' | 'parts'>; iterations?: number }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <div><Button size="sm" onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}>{open ? 'Hide Monte Carlo' : 'Run Monte Carlo'}</Button></div>
      {open && <MonteCarloPanel r={r} iterations={iterations} />}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/quick/Historical.tsx =====
// Estimating → Historical costs: the table Class 5 quick estimates price from, maintained by the estimating team.
// Filter, edit (including the category mix), add / deactivate, CSV export & import, and derive from closed projects.
import { prefs } from '@/core/data'
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import type { AppState, AssetType, DiameterBand, HistoricalCost, Terrain, WorkMethod } from '@/core/types'
import { mutate, personName, useMe, useStore } from '@/core/store'
import { DIAMETER_BANDS, QUICK_CATALOG } from '@/core/engine/quick'
import { saveFile, toCSV } from '@/core/download'
import { go } from '@/core/router'
import { cx, fmtDate } from '@/core/util'
import { Badge, Button, Callout, Checkbox, ConfirmButton, Drawer, Empty, Field, Input, Modal, Panel, SegButtons, Select, TextArea, Table, type Column } from '@/ui/kit'
import { toast } from '@/ui/toast'
import { NumInput, SpreadBar } from '../parts'
import { catName, directCategories } from '../lib'
import {
  ASSET_LABEL, METHOD_LABEL, TERRAIN_LABEL, applyImport, bandLabel, catalogOf, deriveSuggestions, fmtRate, histKey, historicalCsvRows, mixTotal,
  parseHistoricalCsv, planImport, rowUsage, validateHistorical, type ImportPlan,
} from './lib'
import { rowTitle } from './parts'

const EDIT_ROLES = ['estimator', 'estimating-lead', 'admin']
export const useCanEditHistory = () => { const me = useMe(); return me.roles.some((r) => EDIT_ROLES.includes(r)) }

export function HistoricalCosts() {
  const s = useStore()
  const canEdit = useCanEditHistory()
  const [asset, setAsset] = useState('')
  const [method, setMethod] = useState('')
  const [band, setBand] = useState('')
  const [terrain, setTerrain] = useState('')
  const [active, setActive] = useState<'active' | 'inactive' | 'all'>('active')
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<HistoricalCost | 'new' | null>(null)
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false)
  const [deriving, setDeriving] = useState(false)
  const methods = asset ? catalogOf(asset as AssetType)?.methods ?? [] : Array.from(new Set(QUICK_CATALOG.flatMap((c) => c.methods)))
  const rows = s.historicalCosts.filter((h) => (!asset || h.assetType === asset) && (!method || h.workMethod === method) && (!band || h.diameterBand === band) && (!terrain || h.terrain === terrain) && (active === 'all' || (active === 'active' ? h.active : !h.active)) && (!q || `${h.sources.join(' ')} ${h.note ?? ''} ${h.id}`.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())))
  const usage = useMemo(() => Object.fromEntries(s.historicalCosts.map((h) => [h.id, rowUsage(s, h.id)])), [s])
  const suggestions = useMemo(() => deriveSuggestions(s), [s])
  const thin = s.historicalCosts.filter((h) => h.active && h.n < 5).length
  const cols: Column<HistoricalCost>[] = [
    { key: 'a', header: 'Asset · method', sort: (h) => `${h.assetType}${h.workMethod}`, render: (h) => <div className="min-w-[180px]"><div className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold">{ASSET_LABEL[h.assetType]}</div><div className="text-[11px] text-muted">{METHOD_LABEL[h.workMethod]} · <span className="font-mono text-faint">{h.id}</span></div></div> },
    { key: 'b', header: 'Diameter band', sort: (h) => DIAMETER_BANDS.findIndex((b) => b.id === h.diameterBand), render: (h) => <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-[12px]">{catalogOf(h.assetType)?.banded ? bandLabel(h.diameterBand) : <span className="text-faint">Not banded</span>}</span> },
    { key: 't', header: 'Setting', sort: (h) => h.terrain, render: (h) => <span className="text-[12px]">{TERRAIN_LABEL[h.terrain]}</span> },
    { key: 'u', header: 'Unit', render: (h) => <span className="text-[12px]">{h.unit}</span> },
    { key: 'p10', header: 'P10', num: true, sort: (h) => h.p10, render: (h) => <span className="text-muted">{fmtRate(h.p10, h.unit)}</span> },
    { key: 'p50', header: 'P50', num: true, sort: (h) => h.p50, render: (h) => <b>{fmtRate(h.p50, h.unit)}</b> },
    { key: 'p90', header: 'P90', num: true, sort: (h) => h.p90, render: (h) => <span className="text-muted">{fmtRate(h.p90, h.unit)}</span> },
    { key: 'sp', header: 'Spread', render: (h) => <SpreadBar p10={h.p10} p50={h.p50} p90={h.p90} /> },
    { key: 'n', header: 'n', num: true, sort: (h) => h.n, render: (h) => <span className={cx(h.n < 5 && 'font-semibold text-warn')} title={h.n < 5 ? 'Small sample: fewer than 5 closed projects' : undefined}>{h.n}</span> },
    { key: 'y', header: 'Base yr', num: true, sort: (h) => h.baseYear, render: (h) => `${h.baseYear}$` },
    { key: 'src', header: 'Sources', render: (h) => <span className="block max-w-[180px] truncate text-[11.5px] text-muted" title={h.sources.join(', ')}>{h.sources.join(', ') || '—'}</span> },
    { key: 'upd', header: 'Updated', sort: (h) => h.updatedAt, render: (h) => <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-[11.5px]">{fmtDate(h.updatedAt)}<div className="text-faint">{personName(s, h.updatedById)}</div></span> },
    { key: 'use', header: 'Used', num: true, sort: (h) => usage[h.id]?.total ?? 0, render: (h) => <span className="text-muted" title={`${usage[h.id]?.inReview ?? 0} in review`}>{usage[h.id]?.total ?? 0}</span> },
    { key: 'act', header: 'Status', render: (h) => h.active ? <Badge tone="good">Active</Badge> : <Badge>Inactive</Badge> },
  ]
  const exportCsv = async () => {
    const r = await saveFile(`historical-costs-${s.settings.asOf}.csv`, toCSV(historicalCsvRows(rows)), 'text/csv')
    if (r === 'saved') { mutate(() => {}, { entity: 'historical-cost', entityId: 'table', action: 'export', summary: `Exported ${rows.length} historical cost rows to CSV` }); toast('Historical costs downloaded') } else toast(r === 'declined' ? 'Download cancelled' : 'Downloads are not available here', 'info')
  }
  const cleared = !asset && !method && !band && !terrain && active === 'active' && !q
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <Panel pad={false} title="Historical cost table" subtitle={`${rows.length} of ${s.historicalCosts.length} rows · direct cost per unit from closed projects, in base-year dollars (no loaders, escalation or contingency) · Class 5 quick estimates price from the active rows`}
        actions={<>
          <Button size="sm" onClick={exportCsv}>Export CSV</Button>
          {canEdit && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setImporting(true)}>Import CSV</Button>}
          {canEdit && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setDeriving(true)}>Derive from closed projects{suggestions.length ? ` (${suggestions.length})` : ''}</Button>}
          {canEdit && <Button size="sm" variant="primary" onClick={() => setEditing('new')}>Add row</Button>}
        </>}>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-b border-line px-4 py-2.5">
          <Input id="hc-q" aria-label="Search sources and notes" className="h-8 w-44" placeholder="Search sources, notes" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} />
          <Select id="hc-asset" aria-label="Asset type" className="h-8 w-44" value={asset} onChange={(e) => { setAsset(e.target.value); setMethod('') }} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All asset types' }, ...QUICK_CATALOG.map((c) => ({ value: c.asset, label: c.label }))]} />
          <Select id="hc-method" aria-label="Work method" className="h-8 w-44" value={method} onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All work methods' }, ...methods.map((m) => ({ value: m, label: METHOD_LABEL[m] }))]} />
          <Select id="hc-band" aria-label="Diameter band" className="h-8 w-36" value={band} onChange={(e) => setBand(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All bands' }, ...DIAMETER_BANDS.map((b) => ({ value: b.id, label: bandLabel(b.id) }))]} />
          <Select id="hc-terrain" aria-label="Setting" className="h-8 w-36" value={terrain} onChange={(e) => setTerrain(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: '', label: 'All settings' }, ...(['urban', 'suburban', 'rural', 'any'] as const).map((t) => ({ value: t, label: TERRAIN_LABEL[t] }))]} />
          <SegButtons value={active} onChange={setActive} options={[{ id: 'active', label: 'Active' }, { id: 'inactive', label: 'Inactive' }, { id: 'all', label: 'All' }]} />
          {!cleared && <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => { setAsset(''); setMethod(''); setBand(''); setTerrain(''); setActive('active'); setQ('') }}>Clear filters</Button>}
        </div>
        <Table rows={rows} columns={cols} rowKey={(h) => h.id} dense maxHeight={620} onRowClick={(h) => setEditing(h)} empty={<div className="p-4"><Empty title="No rows match">Change or clear the filters.</Empty></div>} />
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-1 border-t border-line px-4 py-2 text-[11.5px] text-muted">
          {thin > 0 && <span className="text-warn">{thin} active row{thin > 1 ? 's' : ''} rest on fewer than 5 projects — requests priced from them are flagged to the reviewer.</span>}
          <span>Region factors and the Class 5 contingency are set in <button className="font-semibold text-link" onClick={() => { try { prefs.setRaw('crosswalk:admin-tab', JSON.stringify('quick')) } catch { /* blocked */ } go('#admin') }}>Configuration → Class 5 quick estimate</button>.</span>
          {!canEdit && <span>Only the estimating team can change this table.</span>}
        </div>
      </Panel>
      {editing && <HistDrawer s={s} row={editing === 'new' ? undefined : editing} canEdit={canEdit} onClose={() => setEditing(null)} />}
      {importing && <ImportModal s={s} onClose={() => setImporting(false)} />}
      {deriving && <DeriveModal s={s} onClose={() => setDeriving(false)} />}
    </div>
  )
}

function blankRow(s: AppState): HistoricalCost {
  return { id: '', assetType: 'transmission-pipeline', workMethod: 'replace-open-trench', diameterBand: '20-24', terrain: 'suburban', unit: 'ft', p10: 0, p50: 0, p90: 0, n: 1, baseYear: Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4)), mix: {}, sources: [], updatedAt: s.settings.asOf, updatedById: s.currentUserId, active: true }
}

function changesOf(a: HistoricalCost, b: HistoricalCost): string[] {
  const out: string[] = []
  for (const k of ['assetType', 'workMethod', 'diameterBand', 'terrain', 'unit', 'p10', 'p50', 'p90', 'n', 'baseYear', 'active', 'note'] as const) if (a[k] !== b[k]) out.push(`${k} ${a[k] ?? '—'} → ${b[k] ?? '—'}`)
  if (a.sources.join(',') !== b.sources.join(',')) out.push(`sources ${a.sources.length} → ${b.sources.length}`)
  if (JSON.stringify(a.mix) !== JSON.stringify(b.mix)) out.push('category mix')
  return out
}

export function HistDrawer({ s, row, canEdit, onClose }: { s: AppState; row?: HistoricalCost; canEdit: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [h, setH] = useState<HistoricalCost>(() => (row ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(row)) : blankRow(s)))
  const [src, setSrc] = useState((row?.sources ?? []).join(', '))
  const c = catalogOf(h.assetType)!
  const cats = directCategories(s)
  const full: HistoricalCost = { ...h, sources: src.split(/[,;]/).map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean) }
  const errs = validateHistorical(full, s.historicalCosts)
  const total = mixTotal(h.mix)
  const use = row ? rowUsage(s, row.id) : { total: 0, inReview: 0 }
  const setAsset = (a: AssetType) => { const k = catalogOf(a)!; setH({ ...h, assetType: a, workMethod: k.methods.includes(h.workMethod) ? h.workMethod : k.methods[0], unit: k.unit, diameterBand: k.banded ? (h.diameterBand === 'n/a' ? '20-24' : h.diameterBand) : 'n/a' }) }
  const setMix = (cat: string, v: number) => { const m = { ...h.mix }; if (v > 0) m[cat] = Math.round(v * 100) / 10000; else delete m[cat]; setH({ ...h, mix: m }) }
  const normalize = () => { const t = total / 100; if (!t) return; setH({ ...h, mix: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(h.mix).map(([k, v]) => [k, Math.round((v / t) * 10000) / 10000])) }) }
  const save = () => {
    if (errs.length || !canEdit) return
    if (row) {
      const ch = changesOf(row, full)
      mutate((d) => { const x = d.historicalCosts.find((y) => y.id === row.id); if (x) Object.assign(x, full, { updatedAt: d.settings.asOf, updatedById: d.currentUserId }) }, { entity: 'historical-cost', entityId: row.id, action: 'update', summary: `Edited historical cost ${row.id} (${rowTitle(full)}): ${ch.join('; ') || 'no change'}` })
      toast('Historical row saved')
    } else {
      let id = ''
      mutate((d) => { let k = d.historicalCosts.length; do id = `hc-${++k}`; while (d.historicalCosts.some((x) => x.id === id)); d.historicalCosts.push({ ...full, id, updatedAt: d.settings.asOf, updatedById: d.currentUserId }) }, { entity: 'historical-cost', entityId: 'new', action: 'create', summary: `Added historical cost row ${rowTitle(full)}: P50 ${fmtRate(full.p50, full.unit)} (n=${full.n}, ${full.baseYear}$)` })
      toast('Historical row added')
    }
    onClose()
  }
  const toggleActive = () => {
    if (!row) return
    mutate((d) => { const x = d.historicalCosts.find((y) => y.id === row.id); if (x) { x.active = !x.active; x.updatedAt = d.settings.asOf; x.updatedById = d.currentUserId } }, { entity: 'historical-cost', entityId: row.id, action: row.active ? 'deactivate' : 'reactivate', summary: `${row.active ? 'Deactivated' : 'Reactivated'} historical cost ${row.id} (${rowTitle(row)})` })
    toast(row.active ? 'Row deactivated — new requests will not price from it' : 'Row reactivated')
    onClose()
  }
  const dis = !canEdit
  return (
    <Drawer open onClose={onClose} width={620} title={row ? `Historical cost ${row.id}` : 'Add historical cost row'}
      footer={<>
        {row && canEdit && <ConfirmButton variant="ghost" onConfirm={toggleActive} confirmLabel={row.active ? 'Deactivate' : 'Reactivate'}>{row.active ? 'Deactivate row' : 'Reactivate row'}</ConfirmButton>}
        <span className="flex-1" />
        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>{canEdit ? 'Cancel' : 'Close'}</Button>
        {canEdit && <Button variant="primary" disabled={errs.length > 0} onClick={save}>{row ? 'Save row' : 'Add row'}</Button>}
      </>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {row && use.total > 0 && <Callout tone="info">{use.total} quick estimate{use.total > 1 ? 's' : ''} priced from this row{use.inReview ? `, ${use.inReview} still in review` : ''}. Approved estimates keep the figures they were signed with; an estimator adjusting one in review reprices from the saved row.</Callout>}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Field label="Asset type"><Select id="hc-e-asset" disabled={dis} value={h.assetType} onChange={(e) => setAsset(e.target.value as AssetType)} options={QUICK_CATALOG.map((x) => ({ value: x.asset, label: x.label }))} /></Field>
          <Field label="Work method"><Select id="hc-e-method" disabled={dis} value={h.workMethod} onChange={(e) => setH({ ...h, workMethod: e.target.value as WorkMethod })} options={c.methods.map((m) => ({ value: m, label: METHOD_LABEL[m] }))} /></Field>
          <Field label="Diameter band" hint={c.banded ? '"All diameters" covers every band' : 'Not banded for this asset type'}><Select id="hc-e-band" disabled={dis || !c.banded} value={h.diameterBand} onChange={(e) => setH({ ...h, diameterBand: e.target.value as DiameterBand })} options={DIAMETER_BANDS.map((b) => ({ value: b.id, label: bandLabel(b.id) }))} /></Field>
          <Field label="Setting"><Select id="hc-e-terrain" disabled={dis} value={h.terrain} onChange={(e) => setH({ ...h, terrain: e.target.value as Terrain | 'any' })} options={(['urban', 'suburban', 'rural', 'any'] as const).map((t) => ({ value: t, label: TERRAIN_LABEL[t] }))} /></Field>
          <Field label="Unit"><Input id="hc-e-unit" disabled value={`${h.unit} (from the catalog)`} /></Field>
          <Field label="Base year (dollars)"><NumInput id="hc-e-year" disabled={dis} value={h.baseYear} min={2000} onChange={(v) => setH({ ...h, baseYear: Math.round(v) })} /></Field>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
          <Field label={`P10 $/${h.unit}`}><NumInput id="hc-e-p10" disabled={dis} value={h.p10} min={0} onChange={(v) => setH({ ...h, p10: Math.round(v) })} /></Field>
          <Field label={`P50 $/${h.unit}`}><NumInput id="hc-e-p50" disabled={dis} value={h.p50} min={0} onChange={(v) => setH({ ...h, p50: Math.round(v) })} /></Field>
          <Field label={`P90 $/${h.unit}`}><NumInput id="hc-e-p90" disabled={dis} value={h.p90} min={0} onChange={(v) => setH({ ...h, p90: Math.round(v) })} /></Field>
          <Field label="n (projects)"><NumInput id="hc-e-n" disabled={dis} value={h.n} min={1} onChange={(v) => setH({ ...h, n: Math.max(1, Math.round(v)) })} /></Field>
        </div>
        {h.p50 > 0 && <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[12px] text-muted"><SpreadBar p10={h.p10} p50={h.p50} p90={h.p90} /> P10 {h.p50 ? `${Math.round((h.p10 / h.p50 - 1) * 100)}%` : '—'} · P90 +{h.p50 ? Math.round((h.p90 / h.p50 - 1) * 100) : 0}% around the median</div>}
        <Field label="Sources" hint="Closed project codes or benchmark notes, comma-separated"><Input id="hc-e-src" disabled={dis} value={src} onChange={(e) => setSrc(e.target.value)} /></Field>
        <Field label="Note"><TextArea id="hc-e-note" disabled={dis} className="min-h-[48px]" value={h.note ?? ''} onChange={(e) => setH({ ...h, note: e.target.value || undefined })} /></Field>
        <div className="rounded-md border border-line">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-line px-3 py-2">
            <div><div className="text-[13px] font-semibold">Category mix</div><div className="text-[11.5px] text-muted">Share of direct cost by CBS category. Becomes the estimate's lines.</div></div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Badge tone={Math.abs(total - 100) <= 0.5 ? 'good' : 'bad'}>{total.toFixed(1)}%</Badge>{canEdit && Math.abs(total - 100) > 0.5 && total > 0 && <Button size="sm" onClick={normalize}>Scale to 100%</Button>}</div>
          </div>
          <table className="tbl [&_td]:py-1">
            <tbody>
              {cats.map((ct) => (
                <tr key={ct.id}>
                  <td className="text-[12px]"><span className="font-mono text-faint">{ct.code}</span> {catName(s, ct.id)}</td>
                  <td className="w-28"><NumInput ariaLabel={`${ct.name} share %`} className="h-7 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px]" disabled={dis} value={Math.round((h.mix[ct.id] ?? 0) * 10000) / 100} min={0} onChange={(v) => setMix(ct.id, v)} /></td>
                  <td className="num w-20 text-[11.5px] text-muted">{h.p50 ? fmtRate((h.mix[ct.id] ?? 0) * h.p50, h.unit) : ''}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
        {errs.length > 0 && canEdit && <Callout tone="bad" title="Fix before saving">{errs.map((e) => <div key={e}>{e}</div>)}</Callout>}
        {row && <p className="text-[11.5px] text-muted">Last updated {fmtDate(row.updatedAt)} by {personName(s, row.updatedById)}. Every change is written to the audit log.</p>}
      </div>
    </Drawer>
  )
}

export function ImportModal({ s, onClose }: { s: AppState; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState('')
  const [file, setFile] = useState('')
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'merge' | 'replace'>('merge')
  const parsed = useMemo(() => (text.trim() ? parseHistoricalCsv(text, s) : null), [text, s])
  const plan: ImportPlan | null = parsed && parsed.rows.length ? planImport(s.historicalCosts, parsed.rows, mode) : null
  const loadSample = () => { setText(toCSV(historicalCsvRows(s.historicalCosts.filter((h) => h.active)))); setFile('current table (as exported)') }
  const apply = () => {
    if (!plan || parsed?.errors.length) return
    mutate((d) => applyImport(d, plan), { entity: 'historical-cost', entityId: 'table', action: 'import', summary: `Imported historical costs from ${file || 'pasted CSV'} (${mode}): ${plan.add.length} added, ${plan.update.length} updated, ${plan.deactivate.length} deactivated, ${plan.unchanged} unchanged` })
    toast(`Import applied: ${plan.add.length} added, ${plan.update.length} updated${plan.deactivate.length ? `, ${plan.deactivate.length} deactivated` : ''}`)
    onClose()
  }
  return (
    <Modal open wide onClose={onClose} title="Import historical costs (CSV)"
      footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" disabled={!plan || !!parsed?.errors.length || (!plan.add.length && !plan.update.length && !plan.deactivate.length)} onClick={apply}>Apply import</Button></>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 text-[13px]">
        <p className="text-muted">Use the columns from Export CSV. <span className="font-mono text-[12px]">mix</span> is <span className="font-mono text-[12px]">category:share</span> pairs separated by semicolons (shares as fractions or percents); <span className="font-mono text-[12px]">sources</span> is semicolon-separated. Rows match on id, else on asset · method · band · setting.</p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <label className="inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded border border-line px-3 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold hover:bg-sunken">
            Choose file…
            <input type="file" accept=".csv,text/csv" className="hidden" onChange={async (e) => { const f = e.target.files?.[0]; if (!f) return; setText(await f.text()); setFile(f.name) }} />
          </label>
          <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={loadSample}>Start from the current table</Button>
          {file && <span className="text-[12px] text-muted">{file}</span>}
          <span className="ml-auto"><SegButtons value={mode} onChange={setMode} options={[{ id: 'merge', label: 'Merge' }, { id: 'replace', label: 'Replace' }]} /></span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-[12px] text-muted">{mode === 'merge' ? 'Merge: matching rows are updated, new rows added, rows not in the file are left alone.' : 'Replace: the file becomes the active table. Matching rows are updated, new rows added, and active rows not in the file are deactivated (kept for the record, never deleted).'}</p>
        <TextArea id="hc-import" aria-label="CSV text" className="min-h-[140px] font-mono text-[11.5px]" placeholder="Paste CSV here, or choose a file" value={text} onChange={(e) => { setText(e.target.value); if (!file) setFile('pasted CSV') }} />
        {parsed?.errors.length ? <Callout tone="bad" title={`${parsed.errors.length} problem${parsed.errors.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} — nothing will be imported until fixed`}><ul className="max-h-40 overflow-y-auto">{parsed.errors.slice(0, 30).map((e) => <li key={e}>{e}</li>)}</ul></Callout> : null}
        {plan && !parsed?.errors.length && (
          <div className="rounded-md border border-line">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-1 border-b border-line px-3 py-2 text-[12.5px]"><span><b>{plan.add.length}</b> to add</span><span><b>{plan.update.length}</b> to update</span><span className={cx(plan.deactivate.length > 0 && 'text-warn')}><b>{plan.deactivate.length}</b> to deactivate</span><span className="text-muted">{plan.unchanged} unchanged</span></div>
            <ul className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto text-[12px]">
              {plan.add.map((r) => <li key={'a' + histKey(r)} className="border-b border-line/60 px-3 py-1"><Badge tone="good">Add</Badge> {rowTitle(r)} · P50 {fmtRate(r.p50, r.unit)} · n={r.n}</li>)}
              {plan.update.map((u) => <li key={'u' + u.from.id} className="border-b border-line/60 px-3 py-1"><Badge tone="info">Update</Badge> {u.from.id} {rowTitle(u.from)}: {changesOf(u.from, u.to).join('; ')}</li>)}
              {plan.deactivate.map((r) => <li key={'d' + r.id} className="border-b border-line/60 px-3 py-1"><Badge tone="warn">Deactivate</Badge> {r.id} {rowTitle(r)}</li>)}
            </ul>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  )
}

export function DeriveModal({ s, onClose }: { s: AppState; onClose: () => void }) {
  const sug = useMemo(() => deriveSuggestions(s), [s])
  const [pick, setPick] = useState<Set<string>>(() => new Set(sug.filter((x) => x.row).map((x) => x.key)))
  const closed = s.projects.filter((p) => p.status === 'closed')
  const apply = () => {
    const chosen = sug.filter((x) => pick.has(x.key))
    for (const x of chosen) {
      const codes = x.points.map((p) => p.code).join(', ')
      if (x.row) {
        const r = x.row
        mutate((d) => { const h = d.historicalCosts.find((y) => y.id === r.id); if (h) Object.assign(h, { p10: x.suggested.p10, p50: x.suggested.p50, p90: x.suggested.p90, n: x.suggested.n, sources: x.suggested.sources, updatedAt: d.settings.asOf, updatedById: d.currentUserId }) },
          { entity: 'historical-cost', entityId: r.id, action: 'derive', summary: `Updated ${r.id} (${rowTitle(r)}) from closed ${codes}: P50 ${fmtRate(r.p50, r.unit)} → ${fmtRate(x.suggested.p50, r.unit)}, n ${r.n} → ${x.suggested.n}` })
      } else {
        mutate((d) => { let k = d.historicalCosts.length, id = ''; do id = `hc-${++k}`; while (d.historicalCosts.some((y) => y.id === id)); d.historicalCosts.push({ ...x.suggested, id, updatedAt: d.settings.asOf, updatedById: d.currentUserId }) },
          { entity: 'historical-cost', entityId: 'new', action: 'derive', summary: `Added historical cost row ${rowTitle(x.suggested)} from closed ${codes}: P50 ${fmtRate(x.suggested.p50, x.suggested.unit)}` })
      }
    }
    toast(`${chosen.length} row${chosen.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} updated from closed projects`)
    onClose()
  }
  return (
    <Modal open wide onClose={onClose} title="Derive from closed projects"
      footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" disabled={!pick.size} onClick={apply}>Apply {pick.size} selected</Button></>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 text-[13px]">
        <p className="text-muted">Final actuals of {closed.length} closed project{closed.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} ({closed.map((p) => p.code).join(', ') || 'none'}), less loaders, divided by quantity and de-escalated to each row's base year. The suggested P50 blends the new points into the row weighted by n; P10 and P90 move with it and widen to cover any new point outside the band. Projects already listed as a row's source are skipped.</p>
        {!sug.length ? <Empty title="Nothing new to add">Every closed project with final actuals is already in the table.</Empty> : (
          <div className="scroll-x">
            <table className="tbl">
              <thead><tr><th style={{ width: 28 }} /><th>Row</th><th>Closed projects ($/unit, base-year $)</th><th className="num">P10</th><th className="num">P50</th><th className="num">P90</th><th className="num">n</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {sug.map((x) => {
                  const u = x.suggested.unit
                  const cell = (k: 'p10' | 'p50' | 'p90' | 'n') => {
                    const cur = x.current?.[k], nxt = x.suggested[k]
                    const f = (v: number) => (k === 'n' ? String(v) : fmtRate(v, u))
                    return <td className="num whitespace-nowrap text-[12px]">{cur != null ? <><span className="text-muted">{f(cur)}</span> → </> : null}<b className={cx(cur != null && nxt !== cur && (nxt > cur ? 'text-bad' : 'text-good'))}>{f(nxt)}</b></td>
                  }
                  return (
                    <tr key={x.key}>
                      <td><Checkbox checked={pick.has(x.key)} onChange={(v) => setPick((p) => { const n = new Set(p); v ? n.add(x.key) : n.delete(x.key); return n })} label="" /></td>
                      <td className="min-w-[200px] text-[12.5px]">{x.row ? <><span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-faint">{x.row.id}</span> {rowTitle(x.row)}</> : <><Badge tone="warn">New row</Badge> {rowTitle(x.suggested)}</>}{!x.row && <div className="text-[11px] text-warn">{x.suggested.note}</div>}</td>
                      <td className="text-[12px]">{x.points.map((p) => <div key={p.projectId}><span className="font-mono font-semibold">{p.code}</span> {fmtRate(p.perUnitBase, u)} <span className="text-faint">({fmtRate(p.perUnitNominal, u)} in {p.year}$, {p.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')} {u})</span></div>)}</td>
                      {cell('p10')}{cell('p50')}{cell('p90')}{cell('n')}
                    </tr>
                  )
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
        <p className="text-[11.5px] text-muted">Final actuals come from each project's cost periods. Review a new row's P10/P90 and category mix after applying.</p>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/views/estimating/quick/lib.ts =====
// Class 5 quick estimate helpers for the estimating module (pure functions and draft mutations).
// The pricing itself lives in core/engine/quick.ts; this file wires it to the request, review and table-maintenance flows.
import type { AppState, AssetType, AuditEntry, DiameterBand, Estimate, FactorInputs, HistoricalCost, HistUnit, Project, QuickEstimate, QuickInputs, SiteDrivers, Terrain, WorkMethod, WorkflowInstance } from '@/core/types'
import { CLASS_RANGES, estimateTotals } from '@/core/engine/estimates'
import { DIAMETER_BANDS, QUICK_CATALOG, UNIT_LABEL, adjustQuickEstimate, computeQuick, deriveFromClosed, escalationFactor, findHistorical, inputsFromProject, type QuickResult } from '@/core/engine/quick'
import { actOnWorkflow, startWorkflow } from '@/core/engine/workflow'
import { adjustFactorEstimate, createFactorEstimate, factorInputsFromProject, previewFactorAdjust, publishedModel, taxForDistrict } from '@/core/engine/factorModel'
import { nowISO, sum, uid } from '@/core/util'

// ───────────────────────────── Labels
export const ASSET_LABEL: Record<AssetType, string> = { 'transmission-pipeline': 'Transmission pipeline', 'distribution-main': 'Distribution main', 'compressor-station': 'Compressor station', 'valve-site': 'Valve site', 'storage-field': 'Storage field', 'regulator-station': 'Regulator station' }
export const METHOD_LABEL: Record<WorkMethod, string> = { 'replace-open-trench': 'Replace, open trench', 'replace-hdd': 'Replace, HDD', hydrotest: 'Hydrotest', abandon: 'Abandon', 'valve-automation': 'Valve automation', 'station-rebuild': 'Station rebuild', 'compressor-replacement': 'Compressor replacement', 'inline-inspection': 'In-line inspection' }
export const TERRAIN_LABEL: Record<Terrain | 'any', string> = { urban: 'Urban', suburban: 'Suburban', rural: 'Rural', any: 'Any setting' }
export const bandLabel = (b: DiameterBand) => (b === 'n/a' ? 'All diameters' : DIAMETER_BANDS.find((x) => x.id === b)?.label ?? b)
export const UNIT_SHORT: Record<HistUnit, string> = { ft: 'ft', site: 'site', hp: 'hp', station: 'station' }
export const QUICK_LABEL = 'Quick · Class 5'

export const catalogOf = (a: AssetType) => QUICK_CATALOG.find((c) => c.asset === a)
export const isBanded = (a: AssetType) => !!catalogOf(a)?.banded

/** Rate per unit for display: $2,415/ft or $1.2M/station. */
export function fmtRate(v: number, u: HistUnit): string {
  const n = v >= 1e5 ? `$${(v / 1e6).toFixed(v >= 1e7 ? 1 : 2)}M` : `$${Math.round(v).toLocaleString('en-US')}`
  return `${n}/${UNIT_SHORT[u]}`
}

// ───────────────────────────── Inputs
/** Keep inputs consistent after a dropdown changes: method valid for the asset, unit from the catalog, band only when banded. */
export function normalizeInputs(i: QuickInputs, prevAsset?: AssetType): QuickInputs {
  const c = catalogOf(i.assetType) ?? QUICK_CATALOG[0]
  const workMethod = c.methods.includes(i.workMethod) ? i.workMethod : c.methods[0]
  let diameterBand: DiameterBand = i.diameterBand
  if (!c.banded) diameterBand = 'n/a'
  else if (diameterBand === 'n/a') diameterBand = i.assetType === 'distribution-main' ? 'le-8' : '20-24'
  const qty = prevAsset && prevAsset !== c.asset && catalogOf(prevAsset)?.unit !== c.unit ? defaultQty(c.unit) : i.qty
  return { ...i, assetType: c.asset, workMethod, diameterBand, unit: c.unit, qty }
}
const defaultQty = (u: HistUnit) => (u === 'ft' ? 5280 : u === 'hp' ? 10000 : 1)

/** Starting inputs for a need that has no project yet. */
export function blankInputs(s: Pick<AppState, 'settings'>): QuickInputs {
  const y = Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4))
  const district = Object.keys(s.settings.quickEstimate.regionFactors)[0] ?? 'East'
  return { assetType: 'transmission-pipeline', workMethod: 'replace-open-trench', diameterBand: '20-24', terrain: 'suburban', district, qty: 5280, unit: 'ft', midpointYear: y + 1 }
}

/** Site drivers for a need with no project yet: everything at its baseline for the setting. */
export function defaultSite(terrain: Terrain): SiteDrivers {
  return { soil: 'normal', permitComplexity: 'ministerial', traffic: terrain === 'urban' ? 'heavy' : terrain === 'suburban' ? 'moderate' : 'low', workHours: 'standard', crossings: 0, remoteness: terrain === 'rural' ? 'regional' : 'local', classLocation: terrain === 'urban' ? 3 : terrain === 'suburban' ? 2 : 1, hca: terrain !== 'rural' }
}

/** Quick-estimate inputs completed with the site drivers and sales tax the factor model needs (from the project when there is one). */
export function toFactorInputs(s: AppState, p: Project | undefined, i: QuickInputs | FactorInputs): FactorInputs {
  if ('site' in i && i.site) return { ...i, site: { ...i.site }, taxPct: i.taxPct ?? (p ? factorInputsFromProject(s, p).taxPct : taxForDistrict(s, i.district)) }
  const from = p ? factorInputsFromProject(s, p) : undefined
  return { ...i, site: from?.site ?? defaultSite(i.terrain), taxPct: from?.taxPct ?? taxForDistrict(s, i.district) }
}
export const isFactorInputs = (i: QuickInputs): i is FactorInputs => 'site' in i && !!(i as FactorInputs).site

export function districtsOf(s: Pick<AppState, 'settings' | 'projects'>): string[] {
  return Array.from(new Set([...Object.keys(s.settings.quickEstimate.regionFactors), ...s.projects.map((p) => p.location.district)])).sort()
}

export function describeQuickInputs(i: QuickInputs): string {
  const band = isBanded(i.assetType) && i.diameterBand !== 'n/a' ? `, ${bandLabel(i.diameterBand)}` : ''
  return `${ASSET_LABEL[i.assetType]} · ${METHOD_LABEL[i.workMethod]}${band} · ${i.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${UNIT_LABEL[i.unit]} · ${TERRAIN_LABEL[i.terrain].toLowerCase()} · ${i.district} · ${i.midpointYear} midpoint`
}

// ───────────────────────────── Result interpretation
export interface MatchNote { tone: 'good' | 'warn' | 'bad'; label: string; detail: string }

/** How the historical row was found, and whether the reviewer should look harder. */
export function matchNote(s: Pick<AppState, 'historicalCosts'>, r: Pick<QuickResult, 'row' | 'match'>, i: QuickInputs): MatchNote {
  if (!r.row) return { tone: 'bad', label: 'No historical data', detail: `The table has no active row for ${ASSET_LABEL[i.assetType].toLowerCase()} · ${METHOD_LABEL[i.workMethod].toLowerCase()}. Ask the estimating team to add one, or prepare a parametric estimate.` }
  const row = r.row
  const small = row.n < 5
  const sample = small ? ` Only ${row.n} closed project${row.n === 1 ? '' : 's'} behind it — small sample.` : ''
  const allDia = isBanded(i.assetType) && row.diameterBand === 'n/a' ? ' The row covers all diameters.' : ''
  if (r.match === 'nearest-band') return { tone: 'warn', label: 'Fallback: nearest diameter band', detail: `No row for ${bandLabel(i.diameterBand)}; priced from ${bandLabel(row.diameterBand)} (${TERRAIN_LABEL[row.terrain].toLowerCase()}).${sample}` }
  if (r.match === 'any-terrain') {
    const pool = s.historicalCosts.filter((h) => h.active && h.assetType === i.assetType && h.workMethod === i.workMethod)
    const split = pool.some((h) => h.terrain !== 'any')
    if (split) return { tone: 'warn', label: 'Fallback: all settings', detail: `No ${TERRAIN_LABEL[i.terrain].toLowerCase()} row; priced from the row that pools every setting.${allDia}${sample}` }
    return { tone: small ? 'warn' : 'good', label: small ? 'Small sample' : 'Matched', detail: `${ASSET_LABEL[i.assetType]} · ${METHOD_LABEL[i.workMethod].toLowerCase()} is kept as one row across settings.${allDia}${sample}` }
  }
  return { tone: small ? 'warn' : 'good', label: small ? 'Small sample' : 'Exact match', detail: `Same asset type, method${isBanded(i.assetType) && !allDia ? ', diameter band' : ''} and setting.${allDia}${sample}` }
}

/** Historical P10–P90 spread around the point, beside the AACE Class 5 expected range. */
export function aaceCompare(r: Pick<QuickResult, 'total' | 'low' | 'high'>) {
  const c5 = CLASS_RANGES[5]
  const lo = r.total ? r.low / r.total - 1 : 0
  const hi = r.total ? r.high / r.total - 1 : 0
  const inside = lo >= c5.low && hi <= c5.high
  return { lo, hi, aaceLo: c5.low, aaceHi: c5.high, aaceLow: Math.round(r.total * (1 + c5.low)), aaceHigh: Math.round(r.total * (1 + c5.high)), inside }
}

/** Which model version priced a quick estimate, and whether the base rate was a fallback. */
export function modelNote(s: AppState, e: Estimate): MatchNote | undefined {
  if (!e.factor || !e.quick) return
  const pub = publishedModel(s)
  const m = storedMatch(s, e)
  const current = pub?.id === e.factor.modelId
  return { tone: m === 'nearest-band' ? 'warn' : current ? 'good' : 'warn', label: `Model v${e.factor.modelVersion}${current ? '' : ' (superseded)'}`, detail: m === 'nearest-band' ? 'Base rate from the nearest diameter band' : current ? 'Priced on the published factor model' : `Priced on v${e.factor.modelVersion}; v${pub?.version ?? '—'} is now published` }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Eligibility, request, revise
export function quickEligibility(s: AppState, pid: string): { ok: boolean; reason?: string } {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === pid)
  if (!p) return { ok: false, reason: 'Pick a project' }
  if (p.status === 'closed' || p.status === 'cancelled') return { ok: false, reason: `${p.code} is ${p.status}` }
  const ests = s.estimates.filter((e) => e.projectId === pid)
  const review = ests.find((e) => e.status === 'in-review')
  if (review) return { ok: false, reason: `v${review.version} is already in review` }
  const better = ests.find((e) => e.status === 'approved' && e.cls < 5)
  if (better) return { ok: false, reason: `${p.code} already has an approved Class ${better.cls} estimate (v${better.version})` }
  const draft = ests.find((e) => e.status === 'draft')
  if (draft) return { ok: false, reason: `Finish or submit draft v${draft.version} first` }
  return { ok: true }
}

export interface RequestArgs { inputs: QuickInputs | FactorInputs; note: string; needBy?: string; byId?: string }
export interface RequestResult { estimateId?: string; instId?: string; message: string; ok: boolean }

const names = (d: AppState, ids: string[]) => ids.map((id) => d.people.find((p) => p.id === id)?.name ?? id).join(', ')

/** Route an existing quick estimate (status draft) to the estimating team: start class5-review and submit the prep step. */
function route(d: AppState, e: Estimate, note: string, byId: string): RequestResult {
  const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === e.projectId)!
  const total = estimateTotals(e).total
  const inst = startWorkflow(d, 'class5-review', { title: `${p.code} Class 5 quick estimate`, recordType: 'estimate', recordId: e.id, projectId: p.id, data: { amount: total, note }, startedById: byId })
  if (!inst) return { ok: false, estimateId: e.id, message: 'No published Class 5 quick estimate review process' }
  const slot = inst.active[0]
  if (slot && slot.assigneeIds.includes(byId)) actOnWorkflow(d, inst.id, slot.nodeId, byId, 'submit', note || 'Quick estimate requested')
  const next = d.workflows.find((w) => w.id === inst.id)?.active[0]
  return { ok: true, estimateId: e.id, instId: inst.id, message: next ? `Sent to the estimating team — ${names(d, next.assigneeIds)}` : 'Submitted' }
}

/** New quick estimate on a project, routed for review. Mutates the draft. */
export function submitQuickRequest(d: AppState, pid: string, a: RequestArgs): RequestResult {
  const byId = a.byId ?? d.currentUserId
  const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === pid)
  if (!publishedModel(d)) return { ok: false, message: 'No published factor model — ask the estimating lead' }
  const e = createFactorEstimate(d, pid, toFactorInputs(d, p, a.inputs), a.note.trim(), byId, a.needBy || undefined)
  if (!e) return { ok: false, message: 'The factor model has no base rate for this asset and method' }
  return route(d, e, a.note.trim(), byId)
}

/** Sent back by the estimating team: the workflow is waiting on the requester at the prep step again. */
export function returnedToRequester(s: Pick<AppState, 'workflows'>, e: Estimate) {
  const inst = s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === e.workflowInstanceId)
  if (!inst || inst.status !== 'running') return
  const slot = inst.active.find((a) => a.nodeId === 'prep')
  if (!slot) return
  const ev = [...inst.history].reverse().find((h) => h.action === 'return')
  return { inst, slot, event: ev }
}

export const canRevise = (s: Pick<AppState, 'workflows'>, e: Estimate) => e.method === 'quick' && (e.status === 'draft' || !!returnedToRequester(s, e))

/**
 * Revise a quick estimate that came back (returned to the requester, or recalled to draft): reprice it in place
 * from new inputs and send it to the estimating team again.
 */
export function reviseQuickRequest(d: AppState, estimateId: string, a: RequestArgs): RequestResult {
  const e = d.estimates.find((x) => x.id === estimateId)
  if (!e || !canRevise(d, e)) return { ok: false, message: 'Only a returned or recalled quick estimate can be revised' }
  const byId = a.byId ?? d.currentUserId
  const nAudit = d.audit.length
  const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === e.projectId)
  const fresh = createFactorEstimate(d, e.projectId, toFactorInputs(d, p, a.inputs), a.note.trim(), byId, a.needBy || undefined)
  if (!fresh) return { ok: false, message: 'The factor model has no base rate for these inputs' }
  // Use the engine's pricing and basis text, but keep this record (same id and version).
  d.estimates.splice(d.estimates.indexOf(fresh), 1)
  d.audit.splice(nAudit)
  e.lines = fresh.lines; e.loaders = fresh.loaders; e.contingency = fresh.contingency; e.basis = fresh.basis; e.quick = fresh.quick; e.factor = fresh.factor; e.salesTax = fresh.salesTax; e.region = fresh.region; e.escalationPct = 0; e.cls = 5
  d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: nowISO(), actorId: byId, entity: 'estimate', entityId: e.id, projectId: e.projectId, action: 'revise', summary: `Revised Class 5 quick estimate v${e.version} on factor model v${e.factor?.modelVersion ?? '—'} (${describeQuickInputs(a.inputs)}): $${(estimateTotals(e).total / 1e6).toFixed(2)}M` })
  const back = returnedToRequester(d, e)
  if (back) {
    back.inst.data.amount = estimateTotals(e).total
    back.inst.data.note = a.note.trim()
    actOnWorkflow(d, back.inst.id, 'prep', byId, 'submit', `Revised: ${a.note.trim()}`)
    const next = d.workflows.find((w) => w.id === back.inst.id)?.active[0]
    return { ok: true, estimateId: e.id, instId: back.inst.id, message: next ? `Resubmitted to the estimating team — ${names(d, next.assigneeIds)}` : 'Resubmitted' }
  }
  e.workflowInstanceId = undefined
  return route(d, e, a.note.trim(), byId)
}

// ───────────────────────────── Review & adjust
/** The running class5-review step on an estimate, if any. */
export function reviewSlot(s: Pick<AppState, 'workflows'>, e: Estimate): { inst: WorkflowInstance; slot: WorkflowInstance['active'][number] } | undefined {
  const inst = s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === e.workflowInstanceId)
  if (!inst || inst.status !== 'running') return
  const slot = inst.active.find((a) => a.nodeId === 'est') ?? inst.active[0]
  return slot ? { inst, slot } : undefined
}

/** How a saved quick estimate's row relates to its inputs (the same categories the request form showed). */
export function storedMatch(s: Pick<AppState, 'historicalCosts'>, e: Estimate): QuickResult['match'] {
  const q = e.quick
  if (q && e.factor) {
    const m = (s as AppState).factorModels?.find((x) => x.id === e.factor!.modelId)
    const br = m?.baseRates.find((b) => b.id === e.factor!.baseRateId)
    if (!br) return 'none'
    return isBanded(q.inputs.assetType) && br.diameterBand !== 'n/a' && br.diameterBand !== q.inputs.diameterBand ? 'nearest-band' : 'exact'
  }
  const row = q && s.historicalCosts.find((h) => h.id === q.historicalCostId)
  if (!q || !row) return 'none'
  const band = isBanded(q.inputs.assetType) ? q.inputs.diameterBand : 'n/a'
  if (row.diameterBand !== band && row.diameterBand !== 'n/a') return 'nearest-band'
  return row.terrain === 'any' ? 'any-terrain' : 'exact'
}

export function canAdjust(s: AppState, e: Estimate, personId: string): boolean {
  if (e.method !== 'quick' || e.status !== 'in-review') return false
  const r = reviewSlot(s, e)
  if (!r || r.slot.nodeId !== 'est') return false
  const me = s.people.find((p) => p.id === personId)
  return (r.slot.assigneeIds.includes(personId) && !r.slot.actedIds.includes(personId)) || !!me?.roles.some((x) => x === 'admin' || x === 'estimating-lead')
}

/** What the estimate would total after an adjustment (same pricing as adjustQuickEstimate). */
export function previewAdjust(s: AppState, e: Estimate, basis: QuickEstimate['basis'], pctAdj: number): { total: number; low: number; high: number; baseRate: number } | undefined {
  if (e.factor) {
    const r = previewFactorAdjust(s, e, pctAdj, basis)
    return r && { total: r.total, low: r.p10, high: r.p90, baseRate: r.base?.rate ?? 0 }
  }
  const row = e.quick && s.historicalCosts.find((h) => h.id === e.quick!.historicalCostId)
  if (!row || !e.quick) return
  return computeQuick({ ...s, historicalCosts: [{ ...row, active: true }] }, e.quick.inputs, basis, pctAdj)
}

/** Apply an estimator adjustment and keep the workflow's amount current. */
export function applyAdjustment(d: AppState, estimateId: string, o: { pct: number; basis: QuickEstimate['basis']; reason: string; note?: string }, byId = d.currentUserId) {
  const e0 = d.estimates.find((x) => x.id === estimateId)
  if (e0?.factor) adjustFactorEstimate(d, estimateId, o, byId)
  else adjustQuickEstimate(d, estimateId, o, byId)
  const e = d.estimates.find((x) => x.id === estimateId)
  const inst = e && d.workflows.find((w) => w.id === e.workflowInstanceId)
  if (e && inst && inst.status === 'running') { inst.data.amount = estimateTotals(e).total; e.quick!.reviewNote = o.note?.trim() ? `${o.reason} — ${o.note.trim()}` : o.reason }
}

export interface AdjustmentEntry { at: string; byId: string; pct?: number; basis?: string; from?: number; to?: number; reason: string; raw: string }

/** Every adjustment ever made to the estimate, from the audit trail (the estimate itself keeps only the latest). */
export function adjustmentHistory(s: Pick<AppState, 'audit'>, estimateId: string): AdjustmentEntry[] {
  return s.audit.filter((a: AuditEntry) => a.entityId === estimateId && a.action === 'adjust').map((a) => {
    const m = a.summary.match(/([+-]?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)%(?: at (P\d0))?: \$([\d.]+)M → \$([\d.]+)M — (.*)$/s)
    return m ? { at: a.at, byId: a.actorId, pct: Number(m[1]), basis: m[2] ?? 'P50', from: Number(m[3]) * 1e6, to: Number(m[4]) * 1e6, reason: m[5], raw: a.summary } : { at: a.at, byId: a.actorId, reason: a.summary, raw: a.summary }
  })
}

// ───────────────────────────── Historical cost table maintenance
export const histKey = (h: Pick<HistoricalCost, 'assetType' | 'workMethod' | 'diameterBand' | 'terrain'>) => `${h.assetType}|${h.workMethod}|${h.diameterBand}|${h.terrain}`
export const mixTotal = (mix: Record<string, number>) => Math.round(sum(Object.values(mix)) * 10000) / 100 // percent

export function validateHistorical(h: HistoricalCost, all: HistoricalCost[]): string[] {
  const out: string[] = []
  const c = catalogOf(h.assetType)
  if (!c) out.push(`${ASSET_LABEL[h.assetType] ?? h.assetType} is not in the quick estimate catalog`)
  else {
    if (!c.methods.includes(h.workMethod)) out.push(`${METHOD_LABEL[h.workMethod] ?? h.workMethod} is not a work method for ${c.label.toLowerCase()}`)
    if (h.unit !== c.unit) out.push(`Unit must be ${c.unit} for ${c.label.toLowerCase()}`)
  }
  if (!(h.p50 > 0)) out.push('P50 must be greater than zero')
  if (h.p10 > h.p50 || h.p50 > h.p90) out.push('P10 ≤ P50 ≤ P90')
  if (!(h.n >= 1)) out.push('n must be at least 1')
  if (!(h.baseYear >= 2000 && h.baseYear <= 2100)) out.push('Base year looks wrong')
  if (Math.abs(mixTotal(h.mix) - 100) > 0.5) out.push(`Category mix must total 100% (now ${mixTotal(h.mix).toFixed(1)}%)`)
  if (h.active && all.some((x) => x.id !== h.id && x.active && histKey(x) === histKey(h))) out.push('Another active row already covers this asset, method, band and setting')
  return out
}

export const HIST_COLUMNS = ['id', 'assetType', 'workMethod', 'diameterBand', 'terrain', 'unit', 'p10', 'p50', 'p90', 'n', 'baseYear', 'mix', 'sources', 'updatedAt', 'updatedById', 'active', 'note'] as const

export function historicalCsvRows(rows: HistoricalCost[]): Record<string, unknown>[] {
  return rows.map((h) => ({ id: h.id, assetType: h.assetType, workMethod: h.workMethod, diameterBand: h.diameterBand, terrain: h.terrain, unit: h.unit, p10: h.p10, p50: h.p50, p90: h.p90, n: h.n, baseYear: h.baseYear, mix: Object.entries(h.mix).map(([k, v]) => `${k}:${v}`).join(';'), sources: h.sources.join(';'), updatedAt: h.updatedAt, updatedById: h.updatedById, active: h.active ? 'yes' : 'no', note: h.note ?? '' }))
}

/** Minimal RFC 4180 CSV reader (quoted fields, doubled quotes, CRLF). */
export function readCsv(text: string): string[][] {
  const out: string[][] = []
  let row: string[] = [], f = '', q = false
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const c = text[i]
    if (q) {
      if (c === '"') { if (text[i + 1] === '"') { f += '"'; i++ } else q = false } else f += c
    } else if (c === '"') q = true
    else if (c === ',') { row.push(f); f = '' }
    else if (c === '\n' || c === '\r') { if (c === '\r' && text[i + 1] === '\n') i++; row.push(f); f = ''; if (row.some((x) => x.trim() !== '')) out.push(row); row = [] }
    else f += c
  }
  row.push(f)
  if (row.some((x) => x.trim() !== '')) out.push(row)
  return out
}

/** Parse an exported (or hand-edited) historical cost CSV. Rows with errors are reported, not imported. */
export function parseHistoricalCsv(text: string, s: Pick<AppState, 'settings' | 'currentUserId'>): { rows: HistoricalCost[]; errors: string[] } {
  const grid = readCsv(text.replace(/^﻿/, ''))
  if (!grid.length) return { rows: [], errors: ['The file is empty'] }
  const head = grid[0].map((h) => h.trim())
  const need = ['assetType', 'workMethod', 'diameterBand', 'terrain', 'p10', 'p50', 'p90', 'n', 'baseYear', 'mix']
  const missing = need.filter((k) => !head.includes(k))
  if (missing.length) return { rows: [], errors: [`Missing column${missing.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}: ${missing.join(', ')}`] }
  const rows: HistoricalCost[] = []
  const errors: string[] = []
  grid.slice(1).forEach((cells, k) => {
    const g = (key: string) => (cells[head.indexOf(key)] ?? '').trim()
    const line = k + 2
    const assetType = g('assetType') as AssetType
    const c = catalogOf(assetType)
    const mix: Record<string, number> = {}
    for (const part of g('mix').split(/[;|]/).map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean)) {
      const [cat, v] = part.split(':')
      const n = Number(v)
      if (!cat || !Number.isFinite(n)) { errors.push(`Line ${line}: mix entry "${part}" should look like cat-contract:0.42`); return }
      mix[cat.trim()] = n > 1.0001 ? n / 100 : n
    }
    const num = (key: string) => Number(g(key).replace(/[$,\s]/g, ''))
    const h: HistoricalCost = {
      id: g('id') || '', assetType, workMethod: g('workMethod') as WorkMethod, diameterBand: (g('diameterBand') || 'n/a') as DiameterBand,
      terrain: (g('terrain') || 'any') as HistoricalCost['terrain'], unit: (g('unit') || c?.unit || 'ft') as HistUnit,
      p10: num('p10'), p50: num('p50'), p90: num('p90'), n: Math.round(num('n')), baseYear: Math.round(num('baseYear')), mix,
      sources: g('sources').split(/[;|]/).map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean), updatedAt: s.settings.asOf, updatedById: s.currentUserId,
      active: !/^(no|false|0|inactive)$/i.test(g('active')), note: g('note') || undefined,
    }
    if (!c) { errors.push(`Line ${line}: unknown asset type "${assetType}"`); return }
    if (!DIAMETER_BANDS.some((b) => b.id === h.diameterBand)) { errors.push(`Line ${line}: unknown diameter band "${h.diameterBand}"`); return }
    if (!['urban', 'suburban', 'rural', 'any'].includes(h.terrain)) { errors.push(`Line ${line}: unknown setting "${h.terrain}"`); return }
    const v = validateHistorical(h, []).filter((x) => !x.startsWith('Another active row'))
    if (v.length) { errors.push(`Line ${line}: ${v.join('; ')}`); return }
    rows.push(h)
  })
  const keys = rows.filter((r) => r.active).map(histKey)
  const dup = keys.filter((k, i) => keys.indexOf(k) !== i)
  if (dup.length) errors.push(`Duplicate active rows in the file: ${Array.from(new Set(dup)).map((k) => k.replace(/\|/g, ' · ')).join('; ')}`)
  return { rows, errors }
}

export interface ImportPlan { add: HistoricalCost[]; update: { from: HistoricalCost; to: HistoricalCost }[]; deactivate: HistoricalCost[]; unchanged: number }

/** What an import would do. Rows match on id, else on asset/method/band/setting. Replace deactivates rows not in the file (never deletes). */
export function planImport(existing: HistoricalCost[], incoming: HistoricalCost[], mode: 'merge' | 'replace'): ImportPlan {
  const plan: ImportPlan = { add: [], update: [], deactivate: [], unchanged: 0 }
  const touched = new Set<string>()
  const canon = (h: HistoricalCost) => JSON.stringify([histKey(h), h.unit, h.p10, h.p50, h.p90, h.n, h.baseYear, Object.entries(h.mix).filter(([, v]) => v).sort(), h.sources, h.active, h.note ?? ''])
  const same = (a: HistoricalCost, b: HistoricalCost) => canon(a) === canon(b)
  for (const r of incoming) {
    const hit = (r.id && existing.find((x) => x.id === r.id)) || existing.find((x) => histKey(x) === histKey(r) && (x.active || !r.active))
    if (hit) {
      touched.add(hit.id)
      const to = { ...r, id: hit.id }
      if (same(hit, to)) plan.unchanged++
      else plan.update.push({ from: hit, to })
    } else plan.add.push({ ...r, id: r.id && !existing.some((x) => x.id === r.id) ? r.id : '' })
  }
  if (mode === 'replace') plan.deactivate = existing.filter((x) => x.active && !touched.has(x.id))
  return plan
}

export function applyImport(d: AppState, plan: ImportPlan, byId = d.currentUserId) {
  let k = d.historicalCosts.length
  for (const r of plan.add) {
    let id = r.id
    while (!id || d.historicalCosts.some((x) => x.id === id)) id = `hc-${++k}`
    d.historicalCosts.push({ ...r, id, updatedAt: d.settings.asOf, updatedById: byId })
  }
  for (const u of plan.update) { const x = d.historicalCosts.find((h) => h.id === u.from.id); if (x) Object.assign(x, u.to, { updatedAt: d.settings.asOf, updatedById: byId }) }
  for (const r of plan.deactivate) { const x = d.historicalCosts.find((h) => h.id === r.id); if (x) { x.active = false; x.updatedAt = d.settings.asOf; x.updatedById = byId } }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Derive from closed projects
export interface DerivedPoint { projectId: string; code: string; perUnitBase: number; perUnitNominal: number; year: number; qty: number }
export interface Suggestion { key: string; row?: HistoricalCost; points: DerivedPoint[]; current?: Pick<HistoricalCost, 'p10' | 'p50' | 'p90' | 'n'>; suggested: HistoricalCost }

/**
 * Closed projects whose final actuals are not yet in the table, grouped by the row they price into.
 * Unit cost is normalized to the row's base year; the suggested P50 is the n-weighted blend, P10/P90 move with it
 * and widen to cover any new point outside the band.
 */
export function deriveSuggestions(s: AppState): Suggestion[] {
  const groups = new Map<string, { row?: HistoricalCost; inputs: QuickInputs; points: DerivedPoint[] }>()
  for (const p of s.projects.filter((x) => x.status === 'closed')) {
    const dv = deriveFromClosed(s, p)
    if (!dv) continue
    const inputs = inputsFromProject(s, p)
    const m = findHistorical(s, inputs)
    const row = m.match === 'exact' || m.match === 'any-terrain' ? m.row : undefined
    if (row?.sources.includes(p.code)) continue
    const baseYear = row?.baseYear ?? dv.year
    const f = dv.year >= baseYear ? 1 / escalationFactor(s, baseYear, dv.year) : escalationFactor(s, dv.year, baseYear)
    const key = row ? row.id : histKey({ ...inputs, terrain: inputs.terrain })
    const g = groups.get(key) ?? { row, inputs, points: [] }
    g.points.push({ projectId: p.id, code: p.code, perUnitBase: Math.round(dv.perUnit * f), perUnitNominal: Math.round(dv.perUnit), year: dv.year, qty: dv.qty })
    groups.set(key, g)
  }
  return Array.from(groups.entries()).map(([key, g]) => {
    const vals = g.points.map((x) => x.perUnitBase)
    const codes = g.points.map((x) => x.code)
    if (g.row) {
      const r = g.row
      const n = r.n + vals.length
      const p50 = Math.round((r.p50 * r.n + sum(vals)) / n)
      const ratio = p50 / r.p50
      const p10 = Math.min(Math.round(r.p10 * ratio), ...vals)
      const p90 = Math.max(Math.round(r.p90 * ratio), ...vals)
      return { key, row: r, points: g.points, current: { p10: r.p10, p50: r.p50, p90: r.p90, n: r.n }, suggested: { ...r, p10, p50, p90, n, sources: [...r.sources, ...codes], note: r.note } }
    }
    const near = findHistorical(s, g.inputs).row
    const v = Math.round(sum(vals) / vals.length)
    return {
      key, points: g.points,
      suggested: { id: '', assetType: g.inputs.assetType, workMethod: g.inputs.workMethod, diameterBand: g.inputs.diameterBand, terrain: g.inputs.terrain, unit: g.inputs.unit, p10: Math.min(...vals), p50: v, p90: Math.max(...vals), n: vals.length, baseYear: g.points[0].year, mix: near ? { ...near.mix } : {}, sources: codes, updatedAt: s.settings.asOf, updatedById: s.currentUserId, active: true, note: `New row from ${codes.join(', ')}${near ? `; category mix copied from ${bandLabel(near.diameterBand)} ${TERRAIN_LABEL[near.terrain].toLowerCase()} row` : ''} — set P10/P90 before relying on it` },
    }
  })
}

/** Projects whose quick estimates reference a historical row (to warn before editing it). */
export function rowUsage(s: Pick<AppState, 'estimates'>, rowId: string) {
  const es = s.estimates.filter((e) => e.method === 'quick' && e.quick?.historicalCostId === rowId)
  return { total: es.length, inReview: es.filter((e) => e.status === 'in-review').length }
}

export const projectLabel = (p?: Project) => (p ? `${p.code} · ${p.name}` : '—')
